------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE abrecupom
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE abrecupom
Lparameters oFormulario

Local llRetorno

lcCliente = (TprMvCab.Contads)
=ThisForm.PoDataMgr.CursorQuery('SigCdCli','CrTmpCli','Iclis',lcCliente,[Cpfs,Rclis,paises,Endes,Nums,cidas,Estas,Rgs,Compls])

*** Tiago - 17/02/2011 - PAF - Verifica GT do ECF
If CrSigOpCdd.PafEcfs = 1 And Used('CrSigFiMpf') And InList(crSigFiFis.Imps,'BEMA8501','DARUMA','NSWEDA','EPSON')
*!*		lcGt = GtAtual()
*!*		lnGt = Val(lcGt)/100
*!*		lnGtatua = Val(Voltasenha(CrSigFiMpf.cgtatuas))
*!*		If lnGt <> lnGtatua
*!*			MessageBox('GT atual da impressora diferente do GT atual do sistema.',64,'Aviso')
*!*			Return .f.
*!*		EndIf
	If ! thisform.parentform.AtuaGt(0,.f.,.t.)
		Return .f.
	EndIf
*!*		ThisForm.ParentForm.Gtecf = lnGt
Endif

lcCliente = (TprMvCab.Contads)
=ThisForm.PoDataMgr.CursorQuery('SigCdCli','CrTmpCli','Iclis',lcCliente,[Cpfs,Rclis,paises,Endes,Nums,cidas,Estas,Rgs,Compls])

lcCpf = ''
lcNom = ''
lcEnd = ''
If InList(crSigCdOpe.ImpClis,[S],[O]) && mena - 04/04/2014 para nao sair cnpj, deixar em branco no cadastro --  and !EMPTY(ThisForm.Cnt_Identifica.Destino.Get_cpf.Value)	&& mena 27/03/2014 - se usu nao digitou cpf/cnpj nao imprime
	If CrSigOpCdd.ConfIcs <> 1 Or MessageBox('Deseja Imprimir o CPF/CNPJ do Cliente? ',4+32,'Confirmação') = 6
		thisform.imprimecpf = .T.		&& mena- 11/08/2016 imprime CPF no CFe
		lcCpf = CrTmpCli.Cpfs
		lcNom = Alltrim(CrTmpCli.Rclis)
		lcEnd = Alltrim(CrTmpCli.Endes)+' '+Alltrim(CrTmpCli.Nums)+' '+Alltrim(CrTmpCli.compls)+' '+Alltrim(CrTmpCli.Cidas)+' '+Alltrim(CrTmpCli.Estas)
	ELSE
		thisform.imprimecpf = .F.		&& mena- 11/08/2016 nao imprime CPF no CFe
	Endif
EndIf

llRetorno = AbrirCupom(oFormulario,lcCpf,lcNom,lcEnd)

If Used('crTefImp')
	Select crTefImp
	Zap
Endif

Return (llRetorno)

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE atuacupom
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE atuacupom
Lparameters vTipo,vnItem

Local LnCodBarra,lcDescr

*!*ThisForm.CaixaLivre.Visible = .f.

If not vTipo

	lcDescr = crTpmMvItn.Dpros
	
	lcSql = [Select Cpros,Cbars,DescFis,DescEcfs,Reffs,Compos, codimppro, dcodimppro, codnacpro, dcodnacpro, coddcr From SigCdPro Where Cpros = ']+crTpmMvItn.Cpros+[']
	=ThisForm.Podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcSql,'xPro')

	If CrSigOpCdd.IdentPro = 1 And CrSigCdOpe.OpCrs <> 4
		_Memo = Set( 'Memo' )
		Set Memo to 40
		lcDescr = Padr(fLimpaTexto( MLine( xPro.DescFis, 1 ) ),40)
		Set Memo to _Memo
	Else
		If Not IsEmpty( crTpmMvItn.CodBarras)
			lnCodBarra = crTpmMvItn.CodBarras
		Else
			lcSql = [Select Cpros,Cbars,DescFis,DescEcfs,Reffs,Compos From SigCdPro Where Cpros = ']+crTpmMvItn.Cpros+[']
			=ThisForm.Podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcSql,'xPro')
			lnNotas = Val( crTpmMvItn.Notas )
			If crSigCdOpe.OpCrs = 4 And Not IsEmpty( AllTrim( crTpmMvItn.Notas ) ) And ;
					( Transform( lnNotas, '@L 999999' ) = crTpmMvItn.Notas )
				lnCodBarra = 0 + lnNotas
			Else
				lnCodBarra = xPro.CBars
			EndIf
		EndIf
	Endif
	If Not Empty(xPro.DescEcfs)
		lcDescr = xPro.DescEcfs
	Endif
	
	lcQuery = [Select * From SigOpCdi Where Dopes = ']+TprMvCab.Dopes+[']
	If (ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'CrSigOpCdi') < 1)
		=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (CrSigOpCdiSigOpCdi)')
		Return 0
	EndIf
		
	lcCodPro = Iif(CrSigOpCdi.SubCdProrF=1,Alltrim(xPro.Reffs),;
				Iif(CrSigOpCdi.SubCdProrF=3, Alltrim(xPro.Compos),;
				Iif(CrSigOpCdi.SubCdProrF=4, Alltrim(xPro.codimppro),;
				Iif(CrSigOpCdi.SubCdProrF=5, Alltrim(xPro.codnacpro),;
				Alltrim(xPro.Cpros)))))
		
	Select crTpmMvItnC
	Delete For cItens >= 1000
	If Type('vnItem') = "N" And vnItem > 0
		xTexto1 = '..Cancelamento do Item: '+Transform(vnItem,"@L 999")
		Insert Into crTpmMvItnC (cItens, Linha, Texto ) Values ;
			(ThisForm.Citem - 1,4,xTexto1)
	Else
		If Not Seek(Str(crTpmMvItn.cItens,4))
			xTexto1 = Transform(crTpmMvItn.cItens,"@Z 9999")+' '+Padr(Iif(CrSigFiFis.Imps='S/IMPRESSORA',lcCodPro,PADL(lnCodBarra,14,'0')),14)+' '+lcDescr
			xTexto2 = '    '+Transform(crTpmMvItn.Qtds,"@Z 999.99")+' '+crTpmMvItn.cunis+' X  '+;
					  Transform(crTpmMvItn.Units,"@Z 999,999,999.99")+' '+;
					  Transform(crTpmMvItn.Totas,"@Z 999,999,999.99")
			Insert Into crTpmMvItnC (cItens, Linha, Texto ) Values ;
				(crTpmMvItn.cItens,1,xTexto1)
			Insert Into crTpmMvItnC (cItens, Linha, Texto ) Values ;
				(crTpmMvItn.cItens,2,xTexto2)
		Endif
	EndIf
Else
	Select [ ] as Agrupar, Sum(Qtds) as QtItem From crTpmMvItn Into Cursor TotItem Group by 1
	_cit = 1004
	Select crTpmMvItnC
	Delete For cItens >= 1000

	xTexto  = Replicate('-',47)
	xTexto0 = 'TI:'+Transform(TotItem.QtItem,"@z 9,999.99")
	xTexto1 = ''
	xTexto2 = Padr('..TOTAL',33)+Transform(ThisForm.Cnt_fpagto.cnt_saldo.get_totliquido.Value,"@Z 999,999,999.99")

	Insert Into crTpmMvItnC (cItens, Linha, Texto ) Values (1001,0,xTexto)
	Insert Into crTpmMvItnC (cItens, Linha, Texto, Cores ) Values (1001,1,xTexto0, 1)
	Insert Into crTpmMvItnC (cItens, Linha, Texto, Cores ) Values (1002,1,xTexto1, 1)
	Insert Into crTpmMvItnC (cItens, Linha, Texto, Cores ) Values (1003,2,xTexto2, 1)

	Select nChaves, fPags, Trocos, cnIdTefs, Infos, Max(tParcs) as tParcs, Max(pDescs) as pDescs, ;
	Sum(Valos) as Valos, Sum(ValDescs) as Valdescs, Sum(Bcds) as Bcds ;
	From xPar Into Cursor xParECF group by 1,2,3,4,5 order by nChaves,Fpags

	_ttBcd = 0
	Select xParEcf	
	Scan
		*!* - mena - 17/10/2016 - substituido pela linha abaixo xTexto = Padr('..'+xparEcf.Fpags+Iif(Not InList(xParEcf.Infos,[T],[C]),'',' ('+Transform(xparEcf.tParcs,"@L 99")+'x)'+' '+Iif(xParEcf.tParcs=0,'',Transform(xParEcf.Valos/xParEcf.tParcs,"@Z 9999,999.99"))),33)+Transform(xparEcf.valos,"@Z 999,999,999.99")
		xTexto = Padr('..'+xparEcf.Fpags+Iif(xparEcf.tParcs=1,'',' ('+Transform(xparEcf.tParcs,"@L 99")+'x)'+' '+Iif(xParEcf.tParcs=0,'',Transform(xParEcf.Valos/xParEcf.tParcs,"@Z 9999,999.99"))),33)+Transform(xparEcf.valos,"@Z 999,999,999.99")
		Insert Into crTpmMvItnC (cItens, Linha, Texto, Cores ) Values ;
			(_cIt,1,xTexto, Iif(xparEcf.Trocos=1,4,2) )
		_cIt = _cIt + 1
		If xParEcf.ValDescs <> 0
			xTexto = Padr(Space(10)+'Desconto '+Alltrim(Transform(xparEcf.pDescs,"@Z 9999.9999%")),33)+Transform(xparEcf.ValDescs,"@Z 999,999,999.99")
			Insert Into crTpmMvItnC (cItens, Linha, Texto, Cores ) Values ;
				(_cIt,1,xTexto, 2 )
			_cIt = _cIt + 1
		EndIf
		_ttBcd = _ttBcd + xParEcf.Bcds
		Thisform.cntGrade.GrdCupom.Refresh
	EndScan
	ThisForm.Bcds = _ttBcd
	Select xpar
	Go Bott

	If ThisForm.Cnt_fpagto.cnt_pag.get_totforma.Value <> 0
		xTexto = Padr('..Valor Recebido',33)+Transform(ThisForm.Cnt_fpagto.cnt_pag.get_totforma.Value,"@Z 999,999,999.99")
		Insert Into crTpmMvItnC (cItens, Linha, Texto, Cores ) Values ;
			(2000,1,xTexto,3)
	EndIf
	If ThisForm.Cnt_Fpagto.cnt_saldo.get_totliquido.Value < ThisForm.Cnt_fpagto.cnt_pag.get_totforma.Value And ;
		ThisForm.Cnt_Fpagto.cnt_saldo.get_totliquido.Value > 0 And ThisForm.Libtroco
		xTroco = ThisForm.Cnt_fpagto.cnt_pag.get_totforma.Value - ThisForm.Cnt_Fpagto.cnt_saldo.get_totliquido.Value 
		xTexto = Padr('..TROCO',33)+Transform(xtroco,"@Z 999,999,999.99")
		Insert Into crTpmMvItnC (cItens, Linha, Texto, Cores ) Values ;
			(3000,1,xTexto,4)
	EndIf
EndIf
Select crTpmMvItnC
Thisform.cntGrade.GrdCupom.Refresh

ThisForm.LockScreen = .T.
=fCarregaGrade(ThisForm.cntGrade.GrdCupom)
ThisForm.LockScreen = .F.
ThisForm.Cnt_mensagem.Mensagem.Caption = ''
Select crTpmMvItn

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE atualizatotal
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE atualizatotal
ctotal=0
Select xPar
Scan
	=ThisForm.Parentform.podatamgr.cursorquery('SigOpFp','CrSigOpFp','Fpags',xPar.Fpags)
	cotacao = ThisForm.ParentForm.Cotacao( xpar.moefpgs )
	cotoper = 1
	If crSigOpFp.Trocos = 1
		ctotal = Round( ctotal - ( xpar.valos * xpar.cotfpgs / cotoper ), 2 )
	Else
		ctotal = Round( ctotal + ( xpar.valos * xpar.cotfpgs / cotoper ), 2 )
	Endif
Endscan
With Thisform.Cnt_Fpagto
	ThisForm.CalculaDesconto()
	_Enc = 0
	lnvTot = ThisForm.AtuaTotal() + .Acrescimo.Get_TotVariação.Value - .Desconto.Get_TotVariação.Value - .cnt_descp.get_tvariacao.Value
	Thisform.Parentform.pagina.dados.pgfprincipal.pgoperacional.getvaltos.value = lnvTot
	.cnt_saldo.get_totliquido.Value = lnvTot
	.cnt_pag.get_totforma.Value = ctotal
	.cnt_apag.get_saldo.Value = .cnt_saldo.get_totliquido.Value - .cnt_pag.get_totforma.value
Endwith
Return .t.
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE atuatotal
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE atuatotal
Local _Valos

_valos   = TprMvCab.valinis + CrSigMvNfi.VlFretes + CrSigMvNfi.Vlseguros + CrSigMvNfi.DespAces
Return (_Valos)


ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE calcsubt
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE calcsubt
SELECT crTpmMvItn
SUM (crTpmMvItn.qtds*crTpmMvItn.units) to thisform.cntGrade.cnt_subt.get_Total.Value
thisform.cntGrade.cnt_subt.get_Total.refresh
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE calculadesconto
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE calculadesconto
Lparameters pSubNivel

Local ValDsc, lnReg

With ThisForm.Cnt_Fpagto

	If CrSigOpCdd.Varps = 1

		Select xPar
		Sum ValDescs to ValDsc
		Go Bottom

		.cnt_descp.get_tvariacao.Value = ValDsc
		Replace ValVarPs With ValDsc * -1 in TprMvCab

		lnvVar = .Acrescimo.Get_TotVariação.Value - .Desconto.Get_TotVariação.Value - .cnt_descp.get_tvariacao.Value
		_Total = ThisForm.AtuaTotal()
		_Varia = IIf(_Total=0,0,Round( Abs(lnvVar) / _Total * 100, 4 ))

		If lnvVar < 0
			Replace ValVars With -Abs(lnvVar) ,;
					Vars    With -Abs(_Varia) in TprMvCab
		Else
			Replace ValVars With Abs(lnvVar) ,;
					Vars    With Abs(_Varia) in TprMvCab
		Endif
		_Enc = 0
		lnvTot = ThisForm.AtuaTotal() + .Acrescimo.Get_TotVariação.Value - .Desconto.Get_TotVariação.Value - .cnt_descp.get_tvariacao.Value
		Thisform.Parentform.pagina.dados.pgfprincipal.pgoperacional.getvaltos.value = lnvTot
		.cnt_saldo.get_totliquido.Value = lnvTot
	EndIf
	If pSubNivel
		Select ParcTef
		lnReg = Recno()
		Sum (Valos)*Iif(Trocos=1,-1,1) To x
		Sum (ValDescs)*Iif(Trocos=1,-1,1) For Baixado <> 1 To w
		Sum ValDescs to ValDsc
		Sum Valos+ValDescs to Valbcd For ValDescs <> 0
		ThisForm.Bcds = Valbcd
		_Total = ThisForm.Atuatotal() + .Acrescimo.Get_TotVariação.Value - .Desconto.Get_TotVariação.Value 
		_Varia = IIf(_Total=0,0,Round( ValDsc / _Total * 100, 4 ))

		Thisform.TotParTef = .cnt_saldo.get_totliquido.Value - x - w

		Select ParcTef
		Locate For Recno() = lnReg
	EndIf
	.cnt_apag.get_saldo.Value = .cnt_saldo.get_totliquido.Value - .cnt_pag.get_totforma.value
	.cnt_saldo.get_totliquido.Refresh
	.cnt_apag.get_saldo.Refresh

EndWith

Return .t.

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE calc_valite
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE calc_valite
=ThisForm.ParentForm.Calc_ValIte()

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE cancelacfe
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE cancelacfe
parameters pcXml
LOCAL lcXml, llOks, lcCpd

*** mena - 29/05/2015 - cancela NFCe emitida pelo migrate ***
IF crSigCdOpe.CupFis=5
	llOks=.t.
	DECLARE integer tCFCancelar_NFCe_Daruma in 'DarumaFramework' string StrNNF, string StrNSerie, string StrChAcesso, string StrProtAutorizacao, string StrJustificativa
	DECLARE integer rAvisoErro_NFCe_Daruma in 'DarumaFramework' String StrCodigo, String StrMensagem
	DECLARE integer regRetornarValor_NFCe_Daruma in 'DarumaFramework' String StrTagNFCe, String StrValorTagNFCe

	IF 	crSigPrNfe.stats = 'EMI'	&& cancelamento de NFCe
		lcCha = ALLTRIM(crSigmvNfi.nfechv)
		lcNf  = ALLTRIM(crSigmvNfi.nfis)
		lcSer = ALLTRIM(crSigmvNfi.series)
		lcPwe  = ALLTRIM(crSigmvNfi.nfeprt)
		lcJu  = 'ERRO DE DIGITACAO - CANCELAMENTO AUTOMATICO'
		  	
		lnRet = tCFCancelar_NFCe_Daruma(lcNf, lcSer, lcCha, lcPwe, lcJu)
		IF lnRet <> 1
			=MostraErro(lnRet)	&& mostra o erro sem cancelar para não correr o risco de cancelar nota emitida anteriormente 
		ENDIF

		lcCod = SPACE(4)
		lcDes = SPACE(600)
		=rAvisoErro_NFCe_Daruma(@lcCod, @lcDes)

		IF lnRet = 1 or lnRet = 0 or 'DUPLICIDADE'$UPPER(lcDes) or 'HOMOLOGADO'$UPPER(lcDes)
			replace cancelas with .T. in crSigMvNfi
			replace stats with 'CAN' in crSigPrNfe
			replace statts with '0' in crSigPrNfe
			replace arquivos with lcJu in crSigPrNfe
			SELECT * from TprMvCab into cursor crSigmvCab readwrite
			replace lcancelas with .T. in crSigMvCab		&& mena 13/08/2014 - seta operacao como cancelada
			replace ncupoms with '' in crSigMvCab			&& mena 17/12/2016 limpa o campo que indica cupom nao finalizado pois a emissão foi finalizada	
			llOks=.t.
		ENDIF
	ENDIF
	RETURN llOks
ENDIF
***********************************************************************************************

*** mena - 10/07/2015 - cancela CF-e emitido pelo SAT ***
IF crSigCdOpe.CupFis=6
*** gera xml para o SAT diretamente pelo SAT.dll ***
	LOCAL lcDt, lcTm, lcAmb, lcCnpjAC, lcAssi, lcRat, lnRet, llSatShar

	*** mena 10/07/2015 - cria estrutura do xml de cancelamento para o CFe - SAT
	CREATE CURSOR CFTags  (c1 c(20), c2 c(20), c3 c(20), c4 c(20), c5 c(20), c6 c(20), c7 c(20), c8 c(20), c9 c(20), c10 c(20), c11 c(20), c12 c(20), c13 c(20), c14 c(20), c15 c(20), c16 c(20), c17 c(20), c18 c(20), c19 c(20), c20 c(20), c21 c(20), c22 c(20), c23 c(20), c24 L, ;
						   c1a n(1,0), c2a n(1,0), c3a n(1,0), c4a n(1,0), c5a n(1,0), c6a n(1,0), c7a n(1,0), c8a n(1,0), c9a n(1,0), c10a n(1,0), c11a n(1,0), c12a n(1,0), c13a n(1,0), c14a n(1,0), c15a n(1,0), c16a n(1,0), c17a n(1,0), c18a n(1,0), c19a n(1,0), c20a n(1,0), c21a n(1,0), c22a n(1,0), c23a n(1,0), ;
						   c1b n(3,0), c2b n(3,0), c3b n(3,0), c4b n(3,0), c5b n(3,0), c6b n(3,0), c7b n(3,0), c8b n(3,0), c9b n(3,0), c10b n(3,0), c11b n(3,0), c12b n(3,0), c13b n(3,0), c14b n(3,0), c15b n(3,0), c16b n(3,0), c17b n(3,0), c18b n(3,0), c19b n(3,0), c20b n(3,0), c21b n(3,0), c22b n(3,0), c23b n(3,0), ;
						   c1c n(3,0), c2c n(3,0), c3c n(3,0), c4c n(3,0), c5c n(3,0), c6c n(3,0), c7c n(3,0), c8c n(3,0), c9c n(3,0), c10c n(3,0), c11c n(3,0), c12c n(3,0), c13c n(3,0), c14c n(3,0), c15c n(3,0), c16c n(3,0), c17c n(3,0), c18c n(3,0), c19c n(3,0), c20c n(3,0), c21c n(3,0), c22c n(3,0), c23c n(3,0))

	INSERT into CFTags (c1, c24, c2) ;
				values ('A', .F., 'CFeCanc')   

	INSERT into CFTags (c1, c24, c2) ;
				values ('A', .F., 'infCFe')   

	INSERT into CFTags (c1, c24, c2) ;
				values ('A', .T., 'chCanc')   

	INSERT into CFTags (c1, c24, c2) ;
				values ('B', .F., 'ide')   

	INSERT into CFTags (c1, c24, c2, c3, c4) ;
				values ('B', .T., 'CNPJ', 'signAC', 'numeroCaixa')

	INSERT into CFTags (c1, c24, c2) ;
				values ('C', .F., 'emit')   

	INSERT into CFTags (c1, c24, c2) ;
				values ('E', .F., 'dest')   

	INSERT into CFTags (c1, c24, c2, c3, c4) ;
				values ('E', .T., 'CNPJ', 'CPF', 'xNome')   

	INSERT into CFTags (c1, c24, c2) ;
				values ('W', .F., 'total')   

	INSERT into CFTags (c1, c24, c2) ;
				values ('Z', .F., 'infAdic')   

	INSERT into CFTags (c1, c24, c2) ;
				values ('Z', .T., 'infCpl')   

	SELECT CFTags
	INDEX on ALLTRIM(C1) tag C1
	SET ORDER TO tag C1


	*** mena - 03/12/2015 - alterado para ler a marca do SAT (esta depois da assinatura) ***
	lcCai = ALLTRIM(crSigOpCdc.cncaixas)			&&'001'
	lcCnpjAC = ALLTRIM(SUBSTR(localEmp.textoPaf,1,14))		&& AC de teste cadastrar no texto do ECF '16716114000172'	&&'11458830000183'
	lcAssi = ALLTRIM(SUBSTR(localEmp.textoPaf,16,344))		&& cadastrar no texto do ECF a partir da posicao 16	'SGR-SAT SISTEMA DE GESTAO E RETAGUARDA DO SAT'
	lcCha  = 'CFe'+ALLTRIM(crSigmvNfi.nfechv)
	lcFab  = ALLTRIM(SUBSTR(localEmp.textoPaf,361,10))		&& pega fabricante do SAT
	lnFab  = IIF(lcFab='SWEDA','3',IIF(lcFab='BEMAT','2','0'))
	lcArq = '.\SATDados.xml'
	lcChr = CHR(13) + CHR(10)

	*** Mota arquivo XML de cancelamento ***

	SELECT CFTags
	SEEK 'A'
	lcTag4 = ALLTRIM(c2)
	=Strtofile(Alltrim([<] +lcTag4 + [>]+ lcChr), lcArq, .F.)
	SKIP
	lcTag3 = ALLTRIM(c2)
	SKIP
	lcTag2 = ALLTRIM(c2)
	=Strtofile(Alltrim([<] + [infCFe ]+lcTag2+[="]+lcCha + [">]+lcChr), lcArq, .T.)


	**** Monta linha B *****************************
	SEEK 'B'
	lcTag1 = ALLTRIM(c2)
	=Strtofile(Alltrim([<] +lcTag1 + [>]+ lcChr), lcArq, .T.)
	SKIP
	lcTag2 = ALLTRIM(c2)
	=Strtofile(Alltrim([<] +lcTag2 + [>]+ lcCnpjAC +[</] +lcTag2 + [>]+lcChr), lcArq, .T.)
	lcTag2 = ALLTRIM(c3)
	=Strtofile(Alltrim([<] +lcTag2 + [>] + lcAssi + [</] +lcTag2 + [>]+lcChr), lcArq, .T.)
	lcTag2 = ALLTRIM(c4)
	=Strtofile(Alltrim([<] +lcTag2 + [>]+ lcCai +[</] +lcTag2 + [>]+lcChr), lcArq, .T.)
	=Strtofile(Alltrim([</] +lcTag1 + [>]+ lcChr), lcArq, .T.)


	**** Monta linha de emitente *****************************
	*** mena 03/12/2015 alteracao na montagem da linha para ser aceita no Sat Tanca e Sweda ***
	SEEK 'C'
	lcTag1 = ALLTRIM(c2)+ ' /'
	=Strtofile(Alltrim([<] +lcTag1 + [>]+ lcChr), lcArq, .T.)
	*!*=Strtofile(Alltrim([</] +lcTag1 + [>]+ lcChr), lcArq, .T.)
	***********************************************************

	**** Monta linha do destinatario *****************************
	lcCpd = STREXTRACT(pcXml,'<CNPJ>','</CNPJ>',3)
	lcCpd = IIF(!EMPTY(lcCpd), lcCpd,STREXTRACT(pcXml,'<CPF>','</CPF>',1))
	*** mena 03/12/2015 alteracao na montagem da linha para ser aceita no Sat Tanca e Sweda ***
	*** mena 10/05/2017 - nao coloca tag de cnpj/cpf ***
*!*		IF !EMPTY(lcCpd)
*!*			SELECT CFTags
*!*			SEEK 'E'
*!*			lcTag1 = ALLTRIM(c2)
*!*			=Strtofile(Alltrim([<] +lcTag1 + [>]+ lcChr), lcArq, .T.)
*!*			SKIP
*!*			lcTag2 = ALLTRIM(IIF(LEN(lcCpD) = 11, c3, c2))
*!*			=Strtofile(Alltrim([<] +lcTag2 + [>]+ lcCPD +[</] +lcTag2 + [>]+lcChr), lcArq, .T.)
*!*			=Strtofile(Alltrim([</] +lcTag1 + [>]+ lcChr), lcArq, .T.)
*!*		ELSE
	SELECT CFTags
	SEEK 'E'
	lcTag1 = ALLTRIM(c2)+ ' /'
	=Strtofile(Alltrim([<] +lcTag1 + [>]+ lcChr), lcArq, .T.)
*!*		ENDIF
	***********************************************************

	*** mena 03/12/2015 alteracao na montagem da linha para ser aceita no Sat Tanca e Sweda ***
	SELECT CFTags
	SEEK 'W'
	lcTag1 = ALLTRIM(c2) + ' /'
	=Strtofile(Alltrim([<] +lcTag1 + [>]+ lcChr), lcArq, .T.)
	*!*=Strtofile(Alltrim([</] +lcTag1 + [>]+ lcChr), lcArq, .T.)

	=Strtofile(Alltrim([</] +lcTag3 + [>]+ lcChr), lcArq, .T.)
	=Strtofile(Alltrim([</] +lcTag4 + [>]+ lcChr), lcArq, .T.)

	*** mena 03/12/2015 numero da sessao é integer ***
	Declare String CancelarUltimaVenda   IN "SAT.dll" Integer numeroSessao, String codigoDeAtivacao, String chave, String dadosCancelamento
	Declare String ConsultarSAT          IN "SAT.dll" Integer numeroSessao

	llSatShar = IIF(!EMPTY(crSigcdpac.dirresta),.t.,.f.)	&& se o aparelho do sat for compartilhado, envia arquivos para area comum
	lnRet=.f.
	IF FILE(lcArq)
		lcTexto = FILETOSTR(lcArq)
		lcCodAti=[12345678]
		DO WHILE .T.
		 	lcArqLog = 'SatLog'
			lnSessao = fGerUniqueKey('SAT')
			lcSessao = PADL(alltrim(STR(lnSessao)),8,'0')	&& mena - para o SAT sweda	&&IIF(INLIST(lnFab,2,3), lnSessao, PADL(alltrim(STR(lnSessao)),6,'0'))	&& mena - para o SAT sweda
			lcRetorno = IIF(llSatShar,EnviarComandoSat('ConsultarSAT',lcSessao, lnFab),ConsultarSAT(lnSessao))
			lcMsg1 = STREXTRACT(lcRetorno, [|],[|],1)

			IF lcMsg1 = '08000'	&& Verifica se SAT está ativo
				lnSessao = fGerUniqueKey('SAT')
				lcSessao = PADL(alltrim(STR(lnSessao)),8,'0')	&& mena - para o SAT sweda	&&IIF(INLIST(lnFab,2,3), lnSessao, PADL(alltrim(STR(lnSessao)),6,'0'))	&& mena - para o SAT sweda
				*** verifica se sat local ou compartilhado ***
				lcRetorno = IIF(llSatShar,EnviarComandoSat('CancelarUltimaVenda',lcSessao, lnFab, lcCodAti, lcCha, lcTexto),CancelarUltimaVenda(lnSessao, lcCodAti, lcCha, lcTexto))
				lcMsg1 = STREXTRACT(lcRetorno, [|],[|],1)
				lcMsg2 = STREXTRACT(lcRetorno, [|],[|],2)
				lcMsg3 = STREXTRACT(lcRetorno, [|],[|],3)
				=Strtofile(lcChr+TTOC(DATETIME())+' Retorno Cancelamento CFe - SESSAO:' + ALLTRIM(STR(lnSessao))+lcChr+lcRetorno, lcArqLog, .T.)
							
				lcMsg="Codigo de retorno: " + lcMsg1 + ' ' + lcMsg2 + ' ' + lcMsg3 + ' Sessão: ' + ALLTRIM(STR(lnSessao))
				IF lcMsg1 = '07000'	&& cupom cancelado
					*!*Messagebox(lcMsg, 0+64, [Retorno do SAT])
					*** mena - 03/08/2015 - prepara dados para impressão de cancelamento e gravação de XML
					lcXml = STREXTRACT(lcRetorno, [|],[|],6)
					lcXml = STRCONV(lcXml,14,1046)
					replace crSigmvnfi.canxml with lcXml in crSigmvnfi
					replace cancelas with .T. in crSigMvNfi
					replace stats with 'CAN' in crSigPrNfe
					replace statts with '0' in crSigPrNfe
					replace lcancelas with .T. in crSigMvCab		&& mena 12/12/2016 seta peracao como cancelada
					replace ncupoms with '' in crSigMvCab			&& mena 17/12/2016 limpa o campo que indica cupom nao finalizado pois a emissão foi finalizada	
					=GrvArqxml('C')	&& grava arquivo xml de cancelamento
					=PrintCfeCan(lcXml)	&& imprime CFe de cancelamento
					lnRet = .T.
				else			
					Messagebox(lcMsg, 0+64, [Retorno do SAT])
				ENDIF
				EXIT
			ELSE
				Messagebox(lcRetorno, 0+64, [Retorno do SAT])
				lnRet = .f.
				EXIT
			ENDIF
		ENDDO
	ENDIF

RETURN lnRet
ENDIF
***********************************************************************************************

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE cancelcupom
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE cancelcupom
Thisform.Subtotac = .t.
oFormulario = Thisform.Parentform.oModemEest
ThisForm.Cnt_mensagem.Mensagem.Caption = 'Verificando Totalização...'
_ValCupFis = VerSubt(oFormulario)
ThisForm.Cnt_mensagem.Mensagem.Caption = ' '
If _ValcupFis = TprMvCab.Valinis
	Return .f.
Else
	Return .t.
EndIf

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE cmdenviaprod
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE cmdenviaprod
If Thisform.Cnt_val.Get_Valor.Value # 0
	ThisForm.Cnt_mensagem.Mensagem.Caption = 'Imprimindo Itens...'
	
	If Not Thisform.ItemFiscal()
		If ThisForm.CancelCupom()
			Thisform.mExcluiItem()
			ThisForm.Total_item()
		Else
			Select crTpmMvItn
			Replace ImpCupFis with .t.
			ThisForm.AtuaCupom()
			ThisForm.ParentForm.CancelaItem = .T.
			ThisForm.Barra_Botoes.Apaga.Enabled = .T.
		Endif
	Else
		Select crTpmMvItn
		Replace ImpCupFis with .t.
		ThisForm.AtuaCupom()
		ThisForm.ParentForm.CancelaItem = .T.
		ThisForm.Barra_Botoes.Apaga.Enabled = .T.
	Endif
	Thisform.mLimpaCntItem(.T.)
Else
	Messagebox('Valor do Produto Inválido !!',0+48,'')
	Thisform.mExcluiItem()
	Thisform.mLimpaCntItem( .T. )
	Thisform.MontaTela()
Endif

ThisForm.Cnt_mensagem.Mensagem.caption = ''
Thisform.mLimpaCntItem()
Thisform.CntItem.Cnt_Prod.Get_Produto.SetFocus

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE codibge
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE codibge
LPARAMETERS pTp, pMun, pUfs, pPai


IF pTp=1	&& pega codigo do municipio
	lnRet = 9999999

	lcQuery = [Select a.Codigos ] + ;
			    [From SigCdMun a, SigCdUfs b ] + ;
			   [Where a.Descs = '] + Padr(fLimpaTexto(Upper(pMun)), 40) + [' And ] + ;
			   		 [a.UFIBGEs = b.UFIBGEs And ] + ;
			   		 [b.Estados = '] + pUFs + [']

	If (ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, [LocalMunic]) < 1)
		=MessageBox([Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!], 16, [Falha na Conexão (LocalMunic)])
		Return 0
	EndIf

	Select LocalMunic
	Go Top In LocalMunic
	If Not Eof([LocalMunic])
		lnRet = Int(Val(LocalMunic.Codigos))
	EndIf

	Return lnRet
ENDIF


IF pTp=2	&& pega codigo do estado
	lnRet = 99

	lcQuery = [Select UFIBGEs ] + ;
			    [From SigCdUfs ] + ;
			   [Where Estados = '] + Padr(pUFs, 2) + [']

	If (ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, [LocalUF]) < 1)
		=MessageBox([Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!], 16, [Falha na Conexão (LocalUF)])
		Return 0
	EndIf

	Select LocalUF
	Go Top In LocalUF
	If Not Eof([LocalUF])
		lnRet = LocalUF.UFIBGEs
	EndIf

	Return lnRet
ENDIF


IF pTp=3	&& pega codigo do pais
	lnRet = 0

	lcQuery = [Select Codigos ] + ;
			    [From SigCdPai ] + ;
			   [Where Descs = '] + Padr(fLimpaTexto(Upper(pPai)), 40) + [']

	If (ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, [LocalPais]) < 1)
		=MessageBox([Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!], 16, [Falha na Conexão (LocalPais)])
		Return 0
	EndIf

	Select LocalPais
	Go Top In LocalPais
	If Not Eof([LocalPais])
		lnRet = Int(Val(LocalPais.Codigos))
	EndIf

	Return lnRet
ENDIF

* Fim ********************************************************************************************
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE enviasat
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE enviasat
LOCAL lcTexto, lnNumero, lcCNPJ, lcCodAti, lcCnpjAC, lnSessao, lcSessao, lcArq, lcChr, lnRet, lcRet, lcChr
LOCAL pIni, pFim, lcMsg1, lcMsg2, lcMsg3, lnFab, lcSatShar

lcArq = '.\SATDados.xml'
lcRet = '.\SATRetorno.txt'
lcChr = CHR(13) + CHR(10)
lnRet = 0
lnFab = IIF(lcFab='BEMAT','2',IIF(lcFab='SWEDA','3','0'))
llSatShar = IIF(!EMPTY(crSigcdpac.dirresta),.t.,.f.)	&& se o aparelho do sat for compartilhado, envia arquivos para area comum

Declare String AtivarSAT             IN "SAT.dll" Integer numeroSessao, String subComando, String codigoDeAtivacao, String CNPJ, Integer cUF
Declare String EnviarDadosVenda      IN "SAT.dll" Integer numeroSessao, String codigoDeAtivacao, String dadosVenda
Declare String CancelarUltimaVenda   IN "SAT.dll" Integer numeroSessao, String codigoDeAtivacao, String chave, String dadosCancelamento
Declare String ConsultarSAT          IN "SAT.dll" Integer numeroSessao
Declare String ConsultarNumeroSessao IN "SAT.dll" Integer numeroSessao, String numeroSessaoAConsultar
Declare String DesbloquearSAT        IN "SAT.dll" Integer numeroSessao, String codigoDeAtivacao
Declare String AssociarAssinatura    IN "SAT.dll" Integer numeroSessao, String CNPJs, String assinaturaCNPJs
Declare String TesteFimAFim          IN "SAT.dll" Integer numeroSessao, String codigoDeAtivacao, String dadosVenda

IF FILE(lcArq)
	lcTexto = FILETOSTR(lcArq)
*!*		lcTexto = 	[<CFe><infCFe versaoDadosEnt="00.00" ><indCFCanc>c</indCFCanc><ide><CNPJ>01234567891234</CNPJ>]+;
*!*					[<signAC>4FUyc5H5xQx7JFGGY8cKlPxESgHS</signAC></ide><emit><CNPJ>01234567891234</CNPJ><IE>000000000000</IE>]+;
*!*					[<IM>394759283045</IM><CRT>3</CRT></emit><dest><CPF>01234567891</CPF><xNome>joão da silva</xNome></dest>]+;
*!*					[<det nItem="1"><prod><cProd>000001</cProd><cEAN>65645376</cEAN><xProd>texto texto</xProd><NCM>00</NCM>]+;
*!*					[<CFOP>0000</CFOP><uCom>dz</uCom><qCom>10000000000</qCom><vUnCom>100</vUnCom></prod><infAdProd>texto texto texto texto]+;
*!*					[texto</infAdProd></det> <infAdic><infCpl>texto texto texto texto</infCpl></infAdic></infCFe></CFe>]


	lcCNPJ = STREXTRACT(lcTexto, [<CNPJ>],[</CNPJ>],2)
	lcCodAti = '12345678'
	lcCnpjAC  = '11458830000183'

	DO WHILE .T.
	 
		lcMaqui  =ALLTRIM(STREXTRACT(SYS(0),'','#',1))	
		lnSessao = fGerUniqueKey('SAT')
		lcSessao = PADL(alltrim(STR(lnSessao)),8,'0')	&& mena 03/12/2015 transforma em string
		lcRetorno = IIF(llSatShar,EnviarComandoSat('ConsultarSAT',lcSessao, lnFab),ConsultarSAT(lnSessao))
		=Strtofile(lcChr+TTOC(DATETIME())+' Consultando S@T - SESSAO:' + ALLTRIM(STR(lnSessao)) + ' ' + lcRetorno, lcRet, .T.)
		lcMsg1 = STREXTRACT(lcRetorno, [|],[|],1)

		IF lcMsg1 = '08000'	&& Verifica se SAT está ativo
			=Strtofile(lcChr+TTOC(DATETIME())+' Emitindo CF-S@T - SESSAO:' + ALLTRIM(STR(lnSessao)) + lcChr + lcTexto, lcRet, .T.)
			lnSessao = fGerUniqueKey('SAT')
			lcSessao = PADL(alltrim(STR(lnSessao)),8,'0')	&& transforma para string 03/12/2015
			lcRetorno = IIF(llSatShar,EnviarComandoSat('EnviarDadosVenda',lcSessao, lnFab, lcCodAti, .f., lcTexto), EnviarDadosVenda(lnSessao, lcCodAti, lcTexto))
			lcMsg1 = STREXTRACT(lcRetorno, [|],[|],1)
			lcMsg2 = STREXTRACT(lcRetorno, [|],[|],2)
			lcMsg3 = STREXTRACT(lcRetorno, [|],[|],3)
			
			lcMsg="Codigo de retorno: " + lcMsg1 + ' ' + lcMsg2 + ' ' + lcMsg3 + ' Sessão: ' + ALLTRIM(STR(lnSessao))
			IF lcMsg1 = '06000'	&& cupom emitido
				lnRet = 1
				WAIT ALLTRIM(lcMsg) window NOWAIT
			else			
				Clear Typeahead
				fBlockInput(.F.)		&& destrava teclado durante emissão
				Messagebox(lcMsg, 0+64, [Retorno do SAT])
			ENDIF
			=Strtofile(lcChr+TTOC(DATETIME())+ ': ' + lcMsg, lcRet, .T.)
			EXIT
		ELSE
			Clear Typeahead
			fBlockInput(.F.)		&& destrava teclado durante emissão
			Messagebox(lcRetorno, 0+64, [Retorno do SAT])
			lnRet = 0
			EXIT
		ENDIF
	ENDDO
ELSE
	=Strtofile(lcChr+TTOC(DATETIME())+ ' XML nao encontrado: ' + lcArq, lcRet, .T.)
ENDIF

Clear Typeahead
fBlockInput(.T.)		&& trava teclado durante emissão
RETURN IIF(lnRet = 1, lcRetorno, '0')

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE gerarcupom
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE gerarcupom
Local lnTenta, lcNocupom, lcNoCcf, lnNoCcf, lnNoCupom

oFormulario = Thisform.Parentform.oModemEest


If INLIST(CrSigCdOpe.CupFis,5,6)
	lcNoCupom = ''
	RETURN .t.
ENDIF

lnTenta = 2
DO While lnTenta > 0
	lcNoCupom = ''
	lcNoCupom = LeituraNoCupom(oFormulario)
	If type('lcNoCupom')='C' And Not Empty(lcNoCupom)
		Do Case
			Case  Inlist(crSigFiFis.Imps,'SWEDA','NSWEDA','BEMATECH','ELGIN','BEMA8501','DARUMA','S/IMPRESSORA','EPSON')
				lnNoCupom = val(lcNoCupom)
				If InList(crSigFiFis.Imps,'BEMATECH','BEMA8501','EPSON') And crSigCdOpe.CupFis=3
					lnNoCupom = lnNoCupom + 1
				Endif
			Case  InList(crSigFiFis.Imps,'DATAREGIS','NDATAREGIS')
				lnNoCupom = val(lcNoCupom) + 1
		EndCase
		lcNoCupom = Transform(lnNoCupom,'@L 999999')
		lnTenta   = 0
	Else
		lnTenta = lnTenta - 1
		ldtime = DateTime()
		Do While DateTime() - ldtime <= 1	&& mena 20/11/2014 diminui tempo 2
			=chrsaw(.5)
		EndDo
		lcNoCupom = []
	EndIf
	If Val(lcNoCupom) = 0 And lnTenta = 0 And Messagebox('Impressora Não Responde (50), Tentar Novamente ?',4+48,'Leitura do No.Fabricacao') = 6
		lnTenta = 2
	Endif
EndDo
If Val(lcNoCupom) = 0
	Return .f.
EndIf

lnTenta = 2
DO While lnTenta > 0

	If CrSigCdOpe.CupFis = 3
		lcNoccf	= LeituraNoGnf()
	Else
		lcNoCcf = LeituraNoCCf()
	Endif
	lnNoCcf = 0
	If type('lcNoccf')='C' And Not Empty(lcNoccf)
		Do Case
			Case  Inlist(crSigFiFis.Imps,'SWEDA','NSWEDA','BEMATECH','ELGIN','BEMA8501','DARUMA','S/IMPRESSORA','EPSON')
				lnNoccf = val(lcNoccf)
				If InList(crSigFiFis.Imps,'BEMATECH','BEMA8501','NSWEDA') And crSigCdOpe.CupFis=3
					lnNoccf = lnNoccf + 1
				Endif
				If InList(crSigFiFis.Imps,'DARUMA') And crSigCdOpe.CupFis=3
					lnModelo = ImpModelo()
					If lnModelo = 3
						lnNoccf = lnNoccf + 1
					Endif
				Endif
			Case  InList(crSigFiFis.Imps,'DATAREGIS','NDATAREGIS')
				lnNoccf = val(lcNoccf) + 1
		EndCase
		lcNoccf = Transform(lnNoccf,'@L 999999')
		lnTenta   = 0
	Else
		lnTenta = lnTenta - 1
		ldtime = DateTime()
		Do While DateTime() - ldtime <= 1	&& mena 20/11/2014 diminui tempo 2
			=chrsaw(.5)
		EndDo
		lcNoCcf = []
	EndIf
	If Val(lcNoccf) = 0 And lnTenta = 0 And Messagebox('Impressora Não Responde (50), Tentar Novamente ?',4+48,'Leitura do No.'+Iif(CrSigCdOpe.CupFis=3,'GNF','CCF')) = 6
		lnTenta = 2
	EndIf
EndDo
If Val(lcNoccf) = 0
	Return .f.
EndIf

Select TprMvCab
Replace notas   With lcNoCupom ,;
		nCupoms With lcNoCupom ,;
		ccfgnfs With lcNoCCf In TprMvCab

Return (.T.)

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE ibpt
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE ibpt
parameters pnOpcao, pcCpD, pcOri, pcClf

*** mena - 10/07/2017 - pega ibpt do banco
IF pnOpcao = '1'	&& inicia variáveis
	thisform.lnVTTribF= 0
	thisform.lnVTTribE= 0
	thisform.lnVTTribM= 0
	*********************************************************
ENDIF

IF pnOpcao = '2'	&& soma os impostos aproximados
	lnVTTribFP = 0
	lnVTTribEP = 0
	lnVTTribMP = 0

	IF INLIST(localemp.mostraim, 3,4) and INT(VAL(SUBSTR(crSigOpCdd.ConfigGers, 208,1)))=1	&& nf ou ambos / operação permite apresentar o imposto
		*** Se CPF setado e destinatario CPF mostra, Se CNPJ setado e destinatario CNPJ mostra, 	
		lnLen=Len(Alltrim(pcCpD))
		IF (INLIST(localemp.mostrapara, 1,2) and (lnLen = 11 or lnLen=0)) or (INLIST(localemp.mostrapara, 1,3) and lnLen = 14)
			IF localemp.aliqIA=0
				ldDat = SUBSTR(DTOS(DATE()),1,8)	&& Mena - 20/20/2017 - pega imposto pelo intervalo de data
				lcQuery = [Select * From SigCdIbp Where ufs = ']+localEmp.estas+[' and codigo = ']+ALLTRIM(pcClf)+[' ]+;
						  [ and  ']+ldDat+[' between LEFT(convert(char,datini,112),8) and LEFT(convert(char,datfin,112),8) order by datini desc]
*!*					lcQuery = [Select * From SigCdIbp Where ufs = ']+localEmp.estas+[' and codigo = ']+ALLTRIM(pcClf)+[' order by datini desc]
				If (ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, [LocalIbp]) < 1)
					=MessageBox([Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!], 16, [Falha na Conexão (LocalIbp)])
					Return 0
				ENDIF
				SELECT LocalIbp
				GO top
				*** verifica qual a Origem da mercadoria para aplicar a aliquota correspondente da tabela IBPT ***
				lnImposF = IIF(INLIST(ALLTRIM(pcOri), '0','3','4','5'), NVL(LocalIbp.nacFederal,0), NVL(LocalIbp.impFederal,0))
				lnImposE = NVL(LocalIbp.estadual,0)
				lnImposM = NVL(LocalIbp.Municipal,0)
			ELSE
				lnImposF = localemp.aliqIA
				lnImposE = 0.00
				lnImposM = 0.00
			ENDIF
			lnVTTribFP = ROUND(lnImposF*crTpmMvItn.Totas/100, 2)
			lnVTTribEP = ROUND(lnImposE*crTpmMvItn.Totas/100, 2)
			lnVTTribMP = ROUND(lnImposM*crTpmMvItn.Totas/100, 2)
		ENDIF
	ENDIF
	thisform.lnVTTribF= thisform.lnVTTribF + lnVTTribFP		&& acumula valor total de tributo federal do produto
	thisform.lnVTTribE= thisform.lnVTTribE + lnVTTribEP		&& acumula valor total de tributo estadual do produto
	thisform.lnVTTribM= thisform.lnVTTribM + lnVTTribMP		&& acumula valor total de tributo municipal do produto
ENDIF


IF pnOpcao = '3'	&& monta string para mostrar
	lcTxt = ''
	IF thisform.lnVTTribF+thisform.lnVTTribE+thisform.lnVTTribM <> 0
		lcTxt = '        Trib aprox R$: '
		lcTXT = lcTxt+IIF(thisform.lnVTTribF<>0,TRANSFORM(thisform.lnVTTribF, '@Z 99999.99') + 'Federal ','')
		lcTXT = lcTxt+IIF(thisform.lnVTTribE<>0,TRANSFORM(thisform.lnVTTribE, '@Z 99999.99') + 'Estadual ','')
		lcTXT = lcTxt+IIF(thisform.lnVTTribM<>0,TRANSFORM(thisform.lnVTTribM, '@Z 99999.99') + 'Municipal ','')
		lcTXT = lcTxt+ 'Fonte: ' + ALLTRIM(LocalIbp.fonte)
	ENDIF
	RETURN lcTxt
ENDIF

IF pnOpcao = '4'	&& monta string para mostrar imposto total
	lcTxt = ''
	lnTot = thisform.lnVTTribF+thisform.lnVTTribE+thisform.lnVTTribM
	IF lnTot <> 0
		lcTxt = 'Valor aproximado dos tributos desse cupom  R$'+ALLTRIM(TRANSFORM(lnTot, '@Z 99,999.99'))
		lcTXT = lcTxt+'           (conforme Lei Fed 12.741/2012)'
	ENDIF
	RETURN lcTxt
ENDIF

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE impcheque
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE impcheque
If ! crSigFiMpF.Pertos
	=ThisForm.Podatamgr.cursorquery('SigCdEmp','CrTmpEmp','Cemps',_Empr,[Cidas,razsocs])

	fMensagemFixa( "Posicione o cheque na impressora...", 48, "IMPRIME CHEQUE", .F., .t., "\VBMP\a_print4.bmp" )

	lcCidade = fLimpaTexto(Alltrim( CrTmpEmp.Cidas ))
	lcFavo = Iif(CrSigFiMpf.Nominal,fLimpaTexto(Alltrim(CrTmpEmp.Razsocs)),'')
	ldData = Iif(Gomonth(Date(),CrSigCdPac.MesVencs) < xPar.Vencs Or CrSigCdPac.MesVencs = 0,xPar.Vencs,Date())

	RetImpChe = ImpChe(xpar.Bancos,xpar.Valos,lcFavo,lcCidade,ldData, Thisform.parentform.oModemEest)
	
	If CrSigFiMpf.BomPara And Ttod(xpar.Vencs) # Date() And MessageBox('Confirma Impressão Bom Para',4+32,'Imprime Cheque') = 6
		fMensagemFixa( "Posicione o cheque na impressora...", 48, "IMPRIME BOM PARA", .F., .t., "\VBMP\a_print4.bmp" )
		RetImpChe = BomPara(Dtoc(Ttod(xpar.Vencs)),ThisForm.parentform.oModemEest)
	Endif
Else
	If ThisForm.ParentForm.pImpcheque And !Empty(xpar.Bancos) And !Empty(xpar.Agencias) And !Empty(xpar.Contas) And !Empty(xPar.Numeros)
		If MessageBox('Confirma Impressão do Cheque',4+32,'Imprime Cheque') = 6
			If Not Empty(CrCfComItc.Portas)
				If CrCfComItc.TpLeChqs = "K"
					llRet = PertoHabilita(crCfComitc.Portas,Iif(crCfComitc.TpBarras=3,.t.,.f.))
					If llRet = 1
						llRet = PertoData(Iif(Gomonth(Date(),CrSigCdPac.MesVencs) < xPar.Vencs Or CrSigCdPac.MesVencs = 0,xPar.Vencs,Date()),Iif(crCfComitc.TpBarras=3,.t.,.f.))
					Else
						llRet = .f.
					Endif
					ThisForm.Podatamgr.cursorquery('SigCdEmp','CrTmpEmp','Cemps',_Empr,[Cidas,Razsocs])
					If llRet 
						lcCidade = fLimpaTexto(Alltrim( CrTmpEmp.Cidas ))
						llRet = PertoCidade(lcCidade,Iif(crCfComitc.TpBarras=3,.t.,.f.))
					Endif
					If llRet 
						lcFavo = Iif(CrSigFiMpf.Nominal,fLimpaTexto(Alltrim(CrTmpEmp.Razsocs)),'')
						llRet = PertoBenef(lcFavo,Iif(crCfComitc.TpBarras=3,.t.,.f.))
					Endif
					If llRet 
						If crSigFiMpF.BomPara And xPar.Vencs > Date()
							llRet = PertoBomPara(xPar.Vencs,Iif(crCfComitc.TpBarras=3,.t.,.f.))
						Else
							llRet = Perto20Ger(Space(20),Iif(crCfComitc.TpBarras=3,.t.,.f.))
						Endif
					EndIf
					If llRet And crSigFiMpF.Cruzado
						llRet = PertoCruzamento('3','','','',Iif(crCfComitc.TpBarras=3,.t.,.f.))
					EndIf
					fMensagemFixa( "Posicione o cheque na impressora...", 48, "IMPRIME CHEQUE", .F., .t., "\VBMP\a_print4.bmp" )
					If llRet 
						If crSigFiMpF.Cruzado
							llRet = PertoPreenche('8',xpar.Valos,xpar.Bancos,.f.,.t.,Iif(crCfComitc.TpBarras=3,.t.,.f.))
						Else
							llRet = PertoPreenche('0',xpar.Valos,xpar.Bancos,.f.,.t.,Iif(crCfComitc.TpBarras=3,.t.,.f.))
						Endif
					Endif
					PertoDesabilita(Iif(crCfComitc.TpBarras=3,.t.,.f.))
				Else
					llRet = ChronoHabilita(crCfComitc.Portas)
					If llRet
						llRet = ChronoData(crCfComitc.Portas,Iif(Gomonth(Date(),CrSigCdPac.MesVencs) < xPar.Vencs Or CrSigCdPac.MesVencs = 0,xPar.Vencs,Date()))
					Else
						llRet = .f.
					Endif
					ThisForm.Podatamgr.cursorquery('SigCdEmp','CrTmpEmp','Cemps',_Empr,[Cidas,Razsocs])
					If llRet 
						lcCidade = fLimpaTexto(Alltrim( CrTmpEmp.Cidas ))
						llRet = ChronoCidade(crCfComitc.Portas,lcCidade)
					Endif
					If llRet 
						lcFavo = Iif(CrSigFiMpf.Nominal,fLimpaTexto(Alltrim(CrTmpEmp.Razsocs)),'')
						llRet = ChronoBenef(crCfComitc.Portas, lcFavo)
					Endif
					fMensagemFixa( "Posicione o cheque na impressora...", 48, "IMPRIME CHEQUE", .F., .t., "\VBMP\a_print4.bmp" )
					If llRet 
						llRet = ChronoBanco(crCfComitc.Portas,xpar.Bancos)
						llRet = ChronoValor(crCfComitc.Portas,xpar.Valos)
						llRet = ChronoPreenche(crCfComitc.Portas)
					Endif
					ChronoDesabilita(crCfComitc.Portas)
				EndIf
			Endif
		EndIf
	Endif
Endif

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE impitemcan
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE impitemcan
*** homologação paf - 09/2012 imprime e cancela item cancelado no DAV ***
SELECT crTpmMvItn
GO top

=ThisForm.PoDataMgr.CursorQuery('SigExMvi','crTpmMvItne','empdopnums',crTpmMvItn.empdopnums,[*])

ImpVen=.T.
Select crTpmMvItnE
Go Top
Do While !eof() And ImpVen
	If CrSigCdOpe.CupFis = 1 And crSigCdOpe.digitens = 1 And crSigFiFis.Imps <> 'BEMATECH'
		ThisForm.SubTotac = .t.
	Endif
	=ThisForm.PoDataMgr.CursorQuery('SigCdPro','CrTmpPro','Cpros',crTpmMvItnE.cpros,[Cpros,Cbars,Icms,Cgrus,tptribs,SitTrIcms,cUnis,DescFis,DescEcfs,IdPro,Reffs,Compos,codimppro,dcodimppro,codnacpro,dcodnacpro,coddcr])
	If ImpVen
		If CrSigOpCdd.IdentPro = 1 
			_Memo = Set( 'Memo' )
			Set Memo to 40
			lcDescr = Padr(fLimpaTexto(MLine( CrTmpPro.DescFis, 1 ) ),40)
			Set Memo to _Memo
		Else
			CodBarra = CrTmpPro.Cbars
			lcDescr  = fLimpaTexto(crTpmMvItnE.Dpros)
		Endif
		If Not Empty(CrTmpPro.DescEcfs)
			lcDescr = CrTmpPro.DescEcfs
		Endif

		lcTipo  = CrSigFiTri.Tipos
		If CrSigFiFis.Imps = 'NSWEDA' And InList(CrSigFiTri.Tipos,'F','I','N')
			lcTipo = Padr(CrSigFiTri.Tipos,3)
		EndIf
		If crSigCdOpe.ChkEDesc = 1
			If InList(crSigFiFis.Imps,'DATAREGIS','NDATAREGIS')
				lnPDesc = round((crTpmMvItnE.Valdescs/(crTpmMvItnE.Units+crTpmMvItnE.Valdescs)) * 100,2)
				ImpVen=VenFis(CodBarra,lcDescr,crSigFiTri.Ordems,crTpmMvItnE.Qtds,crTpmMvItnE.Units+crTpmMvItnE.ValDescs,lnPDesc,oFormulario,lcTipo,.f.,Left(CrTmpPro.Cunis,2))
				ImpVen=cancelItemUlt(oFormulario)
			Else
				ImpVen=VenFis(CodBarra,lcDescr,crSigFiTri.Ordems,crTpmMvItnE.Qtds,crTpmMvItnE.Units+crTpmMvItnE.ValDescs,crTpmMvItnE.ValDescs*crTpmMvItnE.Qtds,oFormulario,lctipo,.f.,Left(CrTmpPro.Cunis,2))
				ImpVen=cancelItemUlt(oFormulario)
			Endif
		Else
			ImpVen=VenFis(CodBarra,lcDescr,crSigFiTri.Ordems,crTpmMvItnE.Qtds,crTpmMvItnE.Units,0,oFormulario,lctipo,.f.,Left(CrTmpPro.Cunis,2))
			ImpVen=cancelItemUlt(oFormulario)
		EndIf
		m.CItens = crTpmMvItnE.citens
		
		lcCodPro = Iif(CrSigOpCdi.SubCdProrF=1,Alltrim(xPro.Reffs),;
				Iif(CrSigOpCdi.SubCdProrF=3, Alltrim(xPro.Compos),;
				Iif(CrSigOpCdi.SubCdProrF=4, Alltrim(xPro.codimppro),;
				Iif(CrSigOpCdi.SubCdProrF=5, Alltrim(xPro.codnacpro),;
				Alltrim(xPro.Cpros)))))
		
		*lcCodPro = Iif(CrSigOpCdi.SubCdProrF=1,Alltrim(CrTmpPro.Reffs),Iif(CrSigOpCdi.SubCdProrF=3, Alltrim(CrTmpPro.Compos), Alltrim(CrTmpPro.Cpros)))
		
		With ThisForm.cnt_Mensagems
			xTexto1 = Padl(lcCodPro,14,'0')+' '+crTpmMvItnE.Dpros
			xTexto2 = Transform(crTpmMvItnE.Qtds,"@Z 999.99")+' '+crTpmMvItnE.cunis+' X '+;
					  Transform(crTpmMvItnE.Units,"@Z 999,999.99")+' = '+;
					  Transform(crTpmMvItnE.Totas,"@Z 999,999.99")
			.mensage1.Caption = xTexto1
			.mensage2.Caption = xTexto2
			.Refresh
		EndWith
		ThisForm.Atuacupom()
		If Not ImpVen
			ImpVen = .t.
			DO While .t.
				llOk = 7
				DO While llOk = 7
					llOk = Messagebox('Impressora Não Responde (51), Tentar Novamente ?',4+32+256,'')
					ThisForm.Subtotac = .t.
				EndDo
				If InList(crSigFiFis.Imps,'BEMATECH','BEMA8501','DARUMA','EPSON')
					VerStatus = RotStatus('NOK',oFormulario)
					If Type('VerStatus')<>'L' And Not Empty(VerStatus)
						Exit
					EndIf
				Else
					If InList(crSigFiFis.Imps,'DATAREGIS','NDATAREGIS')
						VerStatus = RotStatus(.t.,oFormulario)
						If Type('VerStatus')<>'L' And Substr(VerStatus,2,1) = 'S'
							Exit
						EndIf
					Else
						If InList(CrSigFiFis.Imps,'SWEDA','NSWEDA')
							VerStatus = RotStatus(.t.,oFormulario)
							If Type('VerStatus')<>'L' And Not Empty(VerStatus)
								Exit
							EndIf
						EndIf
					Endif
				Endif
			Enddo
			If InList(crSigFiFis.Imps,'BEMATECH','BEMA8501','SWEDA','NSWEDA','DARUMA','EPSON')
				Select crTpmMvItnE
				Loop
			Endif
			oFormulario = Thisform.Parentform.oModemEest
			_ItemCupFis = VerSubt(oFormulario,.t.)
			If _ItemcupFis <> m.cItens
				Select crTpmMvItnE
				Loop
			EndIf
		Endif
		Select crTpmMvItnE
		Skip
		ThisForm.ParentForm.ImpressoItem = .t.
	Endif
EndDo

ENDPROC

* PROCEDURE inchequeserial
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE inchequeserial
Thisform.Leitor = .F.
Thisform.ReadComm.leitura()
Thisform.LeituraSerial=Thisform.readcomm.campo

Thisform.Cnt_fpagto.Cnt_Serial.get_bco.valid
if Thisform.leitor
	if not empty(xpar.bancos)
		if not fChkCheques(xpar.bancos,xpar.agencias,xpar.contas,xpar.numeros,.f.,.t.,xpar.Dopes)
			replace bancos with '', agencias with '', contas with '', numeros with ''
			Thisform.Cnt_fpagto.Cnt_Serial.get_bco.value = ''
			Thisform.Cnt_fpagto.Cnt_Serial.get_age.value = ''
			Thisform.Cnt_fpagto.Cnt_Serial.get_conta.value = ''
			Thisform.Cnt_fpagto.Cnt_Serial.get_num.value = ''
			Thisform.LeituraSerial = ''
			Thisform.Cnt_fpagto.Cnt_Serial.get_bco.setfocus
		else
			Thisform.Cnt_fpagto.Cnt_Serial.get_num.setfocus	
		endif
	endif
else
	Thisform.Cnt_fpagto.Cnt_Serial.get_age.setfocus
endif
Thisform.leitor = .F.
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE iniciarcupom
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE iniciarcupom
If InList(crSigCdOpe.ImpClis,[O])
	cgc = ThisForm.Cnt_Identifica.Destino.Get_cpf.Value
	Cgc = Strt( Strt( Strt( Cgc, ".", "" ), "-", "" ), "/", "" )
	If Empty(CGC)
		MessageBox('Obrigatório Informar uma Conta com CPF/CNPJ.',0+64,'')
		Return .f.
	Endif
Endif

	
	
IF INLIST(crSigCdOpe.CupFis,5,6)
	ThisForm.Inicia = .t.
	RETURN .f.
ENDIF

If Not ThisForm.VerStatus()  &&Checar Status da Impressora
	ThisForm.Barra_Botoes.Impfiscal.enabled = .T.
	Return .f.
Endif

ThisForm.ParentForm.Checkstat = .f.

*** Tiago - 21/12/2010 - Comentado pq a função ainda não existe, mas não esquecer de descomentar
*!*	If CrSigOpCdd.PafEcfs = 1 And Not FchkMd5(CrSigCdOpe.Dopes,ThisForm.PodataMgr)
*!*		MessageBox('Parâmetros de Função Incorreto!!!',0+16,'')
*!*		Return .f.
*!*	EndIf

lnOrdem = 0
lcSql = [Select * From SigFiNtb Where Imps=']+CrSigFiFis.Imps+[' And Ordems = ]+Str(CrSigCdOpe.TrNfis,2)
If ThisForm.Podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcsql,'CrSigFiNtb') > 0
	lnOrdem = crSigFiNtb.OperNF2
Endif
Select crSigFiFis
Goto top
Do Case
	Case InList(crSigFiFis.Imps,'BEMATECH','BEMA8501','DARUMA','S/IMPRESSORA','EPSON')
		If crSigCdOpe.CupFis=1
			ThisForm.Inicia=ThisForm.AbreCupom(oFormulario)
		Else
			ThisForm.Inicia = .t.
		Endif
		ThisForm.parentform.AbriuCupom = ThisForm.Inicia

	Case  InList(crSigFiFis.Imps,'SWEDA','NSWEDA','ELGIN')
		Do Case
			Case crSigCdOpe.CupFis=1
				ThisForm.Inicia=ThisForm.AbreCupom(oFormulario)
				ThisForm.parentform.AbriuCupom = ThisForm.Inicia
			Case crSigCdOpe.CupFis=3
				ThisForm.Inicia=AbrirCupomNF(oFormulario,lnOrdem)
				ThisForm.parentform.AbriuCupom = ThisForm.Inicia
			OtherWise
				Messagebox('Operação não é Cupom Fiscal !!',0+48,'')
		EndCase
	Case  InList(crSigFiFis.Imps,'DATAREGIS','NDATAREGIS')
		If crSigCdOpe.CupFis=1
			ThisForm.Inicia=ThisForm.AbreCupom(oFormulario)
		Else
			ThisForm.Inicia = .t.
		Endif
EndCase

Return .f.

ENDPROC

If CrSigCdOpe.CupFis = 1 And crSigCdOpe.digitens = 1 And crSigFiFis.Imps <> 'BEMATECH'
