------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE mostraobs
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE mostraobs
Lparameters lNewFpg

Local Info, FPag

FPag = xPar.FPags
=ThisForm.parentform.podatamgr.cursorquery('SigOpFp','CrSigOpFp','Fpags',xPar.Fpags)
Info = crSigOpFp.Infos

With ThisForm.Cnt_fpagto
	.GetObs.Visible = ( crSigOpFp.Obs = 1 )
	.SayObs.Visible = ( crSigOpFp.Obs = 1 )
	.get_obs.Visible=.F.
	.say9.Visible=.F.
	.cntnsu.visible=  INLIST(crSigOpFp.nsu,1,3)
	
	*** mena - 26/02/2015 - retirado para testes ***
	.Cnt_ChequepreTef.Visible = .f.

	If Not lNewFpg
		Select xPar
		If (CrSigOpFp.EmiVales = 3) Or (crSigCdOpe.DigObs = 1)
			With .Get_Obs
				.InputMask = Iif( crSigOpFp.EmiVales = 3 And Not Empty(AllTrim(crSigOpFp.cPictures)), ;
							 crSigOpFp.cPictures, '' )
				.MaxLength = Iif( crSigOpFp.EmiVales = 3 And Not Empty(AllTrim(crSigOpFp.cPictures)), ;
							 Len(AllTrim(crSigOpFp.cPictures)), 20 )
				.Width = .MaxLength * 7 + 10 + 2 * .Margin
				.Visible = .T.
				.Refresh
			EndWith
			.say9.Visible=.T.
		EndIf

		*** mena - 26/02/2015 - retirado para testes ***
		If info='C' And CrSigCdOpe.InibcChqs # 1
			Select crSigCdPam
			goto top
			.Cnt_ChequepreTef.Visible = .t.
			Cgc = Strt( Strt( Strt(.Cnt_ChequePreTef.Get_Cpf.Value, ".", "" ), "-", "" ), "/", "" )
			If Empty(Cgc)
				Cgc = Strt( Strt( Strt(ThisForm.Cnt_identifica.Destino.Get_Cpf.Value, ".", "" ), "-", "" ), "/", "" )
			Endif
			.Cnt_ChequePreTef.Get_Cpf.Value = cgc
			.Cnt_ChequepreTef.opc_cpf.Value = 2

			If Len(Alltrim(Cgc))<>14
				.Cnt_ChequepreTef.opc_cpf.Value = 1
			EndIf
			If Not InList(CrSigCdPam.pdvcchqs,1,2)
				.Cnt_chequePreTef.TefChq.Caption = 'Valida C\<heque'
			Endif
			.Cnt_ChequepreTef.refresh
		EndIf
		
	Endif
EndWith
This.Refresh
Select xPar
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE mvalidavalor
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE mvalidavalor
Local lnValor, lnValorUnitario, lnQtde, lnValorTotal

With Thisform.cntitem
	lnValor = .Cnt_val.Get_Valor.Value
	lnValorUnitario = .Cnt_valu.Get_UniVal.Value
	lnQtde = .Cnt_Qtd.Get_Qtds.Value
	lnValorTotal = lnValorUnitario * lnQtde
EndWith

If lnValor # lnValorTotal
	MessageBox('Valor Total Informado Está Incorreto.', 48, ;
			   'Quantidade x Valor Unitário # Valor Informado' )
	Return( .F. )
Else
	Return( .T. )
EndIf

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE setast
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE setast
parameters pnCodSt, pcOri, pcCfis

*** mena 27/05/2015 - limpa as tags de todos os impostos ***
=regAlterarValor_NFCe_Daruma('IMPOSTO\ICMS\ICMS00\orig','')
=regAlterarValor_NFCe_Daruma('IMPOSTO\ICMS\ICMS00\CST','')
*!*	=regAlterarValor_NFCe_Daruma('IMPOSTO\ICMS\ICMS00\modBC','')
*!*	=regAlterarValor_NFCe_Daruma('IMPOSTO\\ICMS\ICMS00\vBC','')
*!*	=regAlterarValor_NFCe_Daruma('IMPOSTO\ICMS\ICMS00\pICMS','')
*!*	=regAlterarValor_NFCe_Daruma('IMPOSTO\ICMS\ICMS00\vICMS','')

=regAlterarValor_NFCe_Daruma('IMPOSTO\ICMS\ICMS10\orig','')
=regAlterarValor_NFCe_Daruma('IMPOSTO\ICMS\ICMS10\CST','')
*!*	=regAlterarValor_NFCe_Daruma('IMPOSTO\ICMS\ICMS10\modBC','')
*!*	=regAlterarValor_NFCe_Daruma('IMPOSTO\ICMS\ICMS10\vBC','')
*!*	=regAlterarValor_NFCe_Daruma('IMPOSTO\ICMS\ICMS10\pICMS','')
*!*	=regAlterarValor_NFCe_Daruma('IMPOSTO\ICMS\ICMS10\vICMS','')
*!*	=regAlterarValor_NFCe_Daruma('IMPOSTO\ICMS\ICMS10\pMVAST','')
*!*	=regAlterarValor_NFCe_Daruma('IMPOSTO\ICMS\ICMS10\pRedBCST','')
*!*	=regAlterarValor_NFCe_Daruma('IMPOSTO\ICMS\ICMS10\vBCST','')
*!*	=regAlterarValor_NFCe_Daruma('IMPOSTO\ICMS\ICMS10\pICMSST','')
*!*	=regAlterarValor_NFCe_Daruma('IMPOSTO\ICMS\ICMS10\vICMSST','')

=regAlterarValor_NFCe_Daruma('IMPOSTO\ICMS\ICMS20\orig','') 
=regAlterarValor_NFCe_Daruma('IMPOSTO\ICMS\ICMS20\CST','') 
=regAlterarValor_NFCe_Daruma('IMPOSTO\ICMS\ICMS20\modBC','')
=regAlterarValor_NFCe_Daruma('IMPOSTO\ICMS\ICMS20\pRedBC','')
=regAlterarValor_NFCe_Daruma('IMPOSTO\ICMS\ICMS20\vBC','')
=regAlterarValor_NFCe_Daruma('IMPOSTO\ICMS\ICMS20\pICMS','')
=regAlterarValor_NFCe_Daruma('IMPOSTO\ICMS\ICMS20\vICMS','')
=regAlterarValor_NFCe_Daruma('IMPOSTO\ICMS\ICMS20\vBCFCP','')
=regAlterarValor_NFCe_Daruma('IMPOSTO\ICMS\ICMS20\pFCP','')
=regAlterarValor_NFCe_Daruma('IMPOSTO\ICMS\ICMS20\vFCP','')
=regAlterarValor_NFCe_Daruma('IMPOSTO\ICMS\ICMS20\vICMSDeson','')
=regAlterarValor_NFCe_Daruma('IMPOSTO\ICMS\ICMS20\motDesICMS','')


=regAlterarValor_NFCe_Daruma('IMPOSTO\ICMS\ICMS40\orig','')	
=regAlterarValor_NFCe_Daruma('IMPOSTO\ICMS\ICMS40\CST','')
*!*	=regAlterarValor_NFCe_Daruma('IMPOSTO\ICMS\ICMS40\vICMS','')	
*!*	=regAlterarValor_NFCe_Daruma('IMPOSTO\ICMS\ICMS40\motDesICMS','')



=regAlterarValor_NFCe_Daruma('IMPOSTO\ICMS\ICMS60\orig','')	
=regAlterarValor_NFCe_Daruma('IMPOSTO\ICMS\ICMS60\CST','')	
*!*	=regAlterarValor_NFCe_Daruma('IMPOSTO\ICMS\ICMS60\vBCSTRet','')	
*!*	=regAlterarValor_NFCe_Daruma('IMPOSTO\ICMS\ICMS60\cICMSSTRet','')



=regAlterarValor_NFCe_Daruma('IMPOSTO\ICMS\ICMSSN102\orig','')	
=regAlterarValor_NFCe_Daruma('IMPOSTO\ICMS\ICMSSN102\CSOSN','')

=regAlterarValor_NFCe_Daruma('IMPOSTO\ICMS\ICMSSN900\orig','')	
=regAlterarValor_NFCe_Daruma('IMPOSTO\ICMS\ICMSSN900\CSOSN','')	
*!*	=regAlterarValor_NFCe_Daruma('IMPOSTO\ICMS\ICMSSN900\modBC','')
*!*	=regAlterarValor_NFCe_Daruma('IMPOSTO\ICMS\ICMSSN900\vBC','')	
*!*	=regAlterarValor_NFCe_Daruma('IMPOSTO\ICMS\ICMSSN900\pRedBC','')	
*!*	=regAlterarValor_NFCe_Daruma('IMPOSTO\ICMS\ICMSSN900\pICMS','')	
*!*	=regAlterarValor_NFCe_Daruma('IMPOSTO\ICMS\ICMSSN900\vICMS','')	
*!*	=regAlterarValor_NFCe_Daruma('IMPOSTO\ICMS\ICMSSN900\modBCST','')	
*!*	=regAlterarValor_NFCe_Daruma('IMPOSTO\ICMS\ICMSSN900\pMVAST','')	
*!*	=regAlterarValor_NFCe_Daruma('IMPOSTO\ICMS\ICMSSN900\pRedBCST','')	
*!*	=regAlterarValor_NFCe_Daruma('IMPOSTO\ICMS\ICMSSN900\vBCST','')	
*!*	=regAlterarValor_NFCe_Daruma('IMPOSTO\ICMS\ICMSSN900\pICMSST','')	
*!*	=regAlterarValor_NFCe_Daruma('IMPOSTO\ICMS\ICMSSN900\vICMSST','')	
*!*	=regAlterarValor_NFCe_Daruma('IMPOSTO\ICMS\ICMSSN900\pCredSN','')	
*!*	=regAlterarValor_NFCe_Daruma('IMPOSTO\ICMS\ICMSSN900\vCredICMSSN','')

*** Seta St conforme parametro ***
do case
	CASE pnCodSt = 00
		=regAlterarValor_NFCe_Daruma('IMPOSTO\ICMS\ICMS00\orig',pcOri)
		=regAlterarValor_NFCe_Daruma('IMPOSTO\ICMS\ICMS00\CST','00')
*!*			=regAlterarValor_NFCe_Daruma('IMPOSTO\ICMS\ICMS00\modBC','')
*!*			=regAlterarValor_NFCe_Daruma('IMPOSTO\\ICMS\ICMS00\vBC','')
*!*			=regAlterarValor_NFCe_Daruma('IMPOSTO\ICMS\ICMS00\pICMS','')
*!*			=regAlterarValor_NFCe_Daruma('IMPOSTO\ICMS\ICMS00\vICMS','')
	CASE pnCodSt = 10
		=regAlterarValor_NFCe_Daruma('IMPOSTO\ICMS\ICMS10\orig',pcOri)
		=regAlterarValor_NFCe_Daruma('IMPOSTO\ICMS\ICMS10\CST','10')
*!*			=regAlterarValor_NFCe_Daruma('IMPOSTO\ICMS\ICMS10\modBC','')
*!*			=regAlterarValor_NFCe_Daruma('IMPOSTO\ICMS\ICMS10\vBC','')
*!*			=regAlterarValor_NFCe_Daruma('IMPOSTO\ICMS\ICMS10\pICMS','')
*!*			=regAlterarValor_NFCe_Daruma('IMPOSTO\ICMS\ICMS10\vICMS','')
*!*			=regAlterarValor_NFCe_Daruma('IMPOSTO\ICMS\ICMS10\pMVAST','')
*!*			=regAlterarValor_NFCe_Daruma('IMPOSTO\ICMS\ICMS10\pRedBCST','')
*!*			=regAlterarValor_NFCe_Daruma('IMPOSTO\ICMS\ICMS10\vBCST','')
*!*			=regAlterarValor_NFCe_Daruma('IMPOSTO\ICMS\ICMS10\pICMSST','')
*!*			=regAlterarValor_NFCe_Daruma('IMPOSTO\ICMS\ICMS10\vICMSST','')

	CASE pnCodSt = 20
		=regAlterarValor_NFCe_Daruma('IMPOSTO\ICMS\ICMS20\orig',pcOri)
		=regAlterarValor_NFCe_Daruma('IMPOSTO\ICMS\ICMS20\CST',ALLTRIM(STR(pnCodSt)))

	CASE pnCodSt = 40 or pnCodSt = 41 or pnCodSt = 50
		=regAlterarValor_NFCe_Daruma('IMPOSTO\ICMS\ICMS40\orig',pcOri)	
		=regAlterarValor_NFCe_Daruma('IMPOSTO\ICMS\ICMS40\CST',ALLTRIM(STR(pnCodSt)))
*!*			=regAlterarValor_NFCe_Daruma('IMPOSTO\ICMS\ICMS40\vICMS','')	
*!*			=regAlterarValor_NFCe_Daruma('IMPOSTO\ICMS\ICMS40\motDesICMS','')
	CASE pnCodSt = 60
		=regAlterarValor_NFCe_Daruma('IMPOSTO\ICMS\ICMS60\orig',pcOri)	
		=regAlterarValor_NFCe_Daruma('IMPOSTO\ICMS\ICMS60\CST','60')	
*!*			=regAlterarValor_NFCe_Daruma('IMPOSTO\ICMS\ICMS60\vBCSTRet','')	
*!*			=regAlterarValor_NFCe_Daruma('IMPOSTO\ICMS\ICMS60\cICMSSTRet','')
	CASE pnCodSt = 900
		=regAlterarValor_NFCe_Daruma('IMPOSTO\ICMS\ICMSSN900\orig',pcOri)	
		=regAlterarValor_NFCe_Daruma('IMPOSTO\ICMS\ICMSSN900\CSOSN','900')	
*!*			=regAlterarValor_NFCe_Daruma('IMPOSTO\ICMS\ICMSSN900\modBC','')
*!*			=regAlterarValor_NFCe_Daruma('IMPOSTO\ICMS\ICMSSN900\vBC','')	
*!*			=regAlterarValor_NFCe_Daruma('IMPOSTO\ICMS\ICMSSN900\pRedBC','')	
*!*			=regAlterarValor_NFCe_Daruma('IMPOSTO\ICMS\ICMSSN900\pICMS','')	
*!*			=regAlterarValor_NFCe_Daruma('IMPOSTO\ICMS\ICMSSN900\vICMS','')	
*!*			=regAlterarValor_NFCe_Daruma('IMPOSTO\ICMS\ICMSSN900\modBCST','')	
*!*			=regAlterarValor_NFCe_Daruma('IMPOSTO\ICMS\ICMSSN900\pMVAST','')	
*!*			=regAlterarValor_NFCe_Daruma('IMPOSTO\ICMS\ICMSSN900\pRedBCST','')	
*!*			=regAlterarValor_NFCe_Daruma('IMPOSTO\ICMS\ICMSSN900\vBCST','')	
*!*			=regAlterarValor_NFCe_Daruma('IMPOSTO\ICMS\ICMSSN900\pICMSST','')	
*!*			=regAlterarValor_NFCe_Daruma('IMPOSTO\ICMS\ICMSSN900\vICMSST','')	
*!*			=regAlterarValor_NFCe_Daruma('IMPOSTO\ICMS\ICMSSN900\pCredSN','')	
*!*			=regAlterarValor_NFCe_Daruma('IMPOSTO\ICMS\ICMSSN900\vCredICMSSN','')
	otherwise
		=regAlterarValor_NFCe_Daruma('IMPOSTO\ICMS\ICMSSN102\orig',pcOri)	
		=regAlterarValor_NFCe_Daruma('IMPOSTO\ICMS\ICMSSN102\CSOSN','102')
ENDCASE

*** altera NCM (classificacao fiscal ***
IF !EMPTY(pcCFis)
	=regAlterarValor_NFCe_Daruma('PROD\NCM',pcCFis)
ENDIF

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE total_item
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE total_item
Lparameters pTotaliza

Select crTpmMvItn
_cit = crTpmMvItn.Citens
=ThisForm.ParentForm.Calc_ValIte()
Thisform.ParentForm.TotIte = fTotalizarItn(ThisForm.parentform.Podatamgr)
If ptotaliza && PAF - Tiago - 11/03/2011 - Atualizava sempre o total
	Thisform.Cnt_tots.Get_Total.Value = Thisform.ParentForm.TotIte
	thisform.calcsubt() && mena 02/03/2015 	
ENDIF

Thisform.parentform.Atuatot()
With ThisForm.cnt_mensagems
	Select crTpmMvItn
	Locate for cItens = _cit
	xTexto1 = ''
	xTexto2 = ''
	If not Empty(crTpmMvItn.Cpros)
	
		lcSql = [Select Cpros,Cbars,DescEcfs,DescFis,clfiscals,reffs,ean13,cgrus,Compos,codimppro,dcodimppro,codnacpro,dcodnacpro,coddcr From SigCdPro Where Cpros = ']+crTpmMvItn.Cpros+[']
		=ThisForm.Podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcSql,'xPro')
		
		lcCodPro = Iif(CrSigOpCdi.SubCdProrF=1,Alltrim(xPro.Reffs),;
				Iif(CrSigOpCdi.SubCdProrF=3, Alltrim(xPro.Compos),;
				Iif(CrSigOpCdi.SubCdProrF=4, Alltrim(xPro.codimppro),;
				Iif(CrSigOpCdi.SubCdProrF=5, Alltrim(xPro.codnacpro),;
				Alltrim(xPro.Cpros)))))
				
		*lcCodPro = Iif(CrSigOpCdi.SubCdProrF=1,Alltrim(xPro.Reffs),Iif(CrSigOpCdi.SubCdProrF=3, Alltrim(xPro.Compos), Alltrim(xPro.Cpros)))
		
		*-- Descrição Fiscal
		lcDescr = crTpmMvItn.dpros
		If CrSigOpCdd.IdentPro = 1 And CrSigCdOpe.OpCrs <> 4 And Not IsEmpty(xPro.DescFis)
			_Memo = Set( 'Memo' )
			Set Memo to 40
			lcDescr = Padr(fLimpaTexto( MLine( xPro.DescFis, 1 ) ),40)
			Set Memo to _Memo
			
			If IsEmpty(lcDescr)
				lcDescr = crTpmMvItn.dpros
			EndIf 
		Endif
		If Not IsEmpty(xPro.DescEcfs)
			lcDescr = xPro.DescEcfs
		Endif
		*
		xTexto1 = Padl(lcCodPro,14,'0') +' '+lcDescr
		xTexto2 = Transform(crTpmMvItn.Qtds,"@Z 999.99")+' '+crTpmMvItn.cunis+' X '+;
				  Transform(crTpmMvItn.Units,"@Z 999,999.99")+' = '+;
				  Transform(crTpmMvItn.Totas,"@Z 999,999.99")
	EndIf
	.mensage1.Caption = xTexto1
	.mensage2.Caption = xTexto2
	.Refresh
EndWith

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE valida24hs
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE valida24hs
Lparameters pDataImp, pSeriFab

If ThisForm.ParentForm.Libera24hs
	Return .t.
Endif

lcDtEcf = Ttoc(pDataImp)
pDiaAnt = ( Ttod(pDataImp) - 1 )
lcHora  = Substr(lcDtEcf,12,2)
pDtMcr  = fDtoSQL(Date(),'23:55:00')

If ( Ttod(pDtMcr) = Ttod(pDataImp) And pDataImp > pDtMcr ) Or InList(lcHora,'00','01')

	lcSql = [Select Top 1 Serifab,dtredzs From SigFiRx Where SeriFab = ']+pSeriFab+[' Order by DtRedZs Desc ]
	If ThisForm.PoDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcSql,'LocalRelx') < 1
		MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão (Valida24Hs - LocalRelx)')
		Return 0
	EndIf
	
	Select LocalRelx
	Go Top
	
	lLiberado = .f.
	
	If InList(lcHora,'00','01') And Ttod(LocalRelx.DtRedzs) = pDiaAnt
		lLiberado = .t.
	Else
		llData = LocalRelx.DtRedZs
		lcSql = [Select a.Nemps, Max(a.Datas) as Datas From SigMvCab a, SigCdOpe b ]+;
				[Where a.Datas > ?llData And a.dopes = b.dopes and b.cupfis in (1,3) ]+;
				[And a.nemps = ']+pSeriFab+[' group by a.nemps ]

		If ThisForm.PoDataMgr.Sqlexecute(lcSql,'MovEest') < 1
			MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão (Valida24Hs - MovEest)')
			Return 0
		EndIf
		
		Select MovEest
		If Reccount('MovEest') > 0
			MessageBox('Obrigatório Realização da Redução Z da ECF.',0+64,'Atenção!!!')
		Else
			lLiberado = .t.
		Endif
	EndIf

	ThisForm.ParentForm.Libera24hs = lLiberado

	Return (lliberado)
Else
	Return .t.
EndIf

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE verifsubtotal
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE verifsubtotal
Local llOK, llError1, lcOnError1

If ThisForm.Subtotac And Not InList(crSigFiFis.Imps,'S/IMPRESSORA')
	Clear Typeahead
	Keyboard '{BACKSPACE}' 
	m.llError1 = .f.
	m.lcOnError1 = On('Error')
	On Error m.llError1 = .t.
	llOk = Inkey(.5, 'MH')
	On Error &lcOnError1.
	If ThisForm.CancelCupom()
		llOk = 7
		DO While llOk = 7
			llOk = MessageBox('O Total do Cupom não Confere Com Total de Itens do Sistema. '+Chr(13)+;
						'Por Favor Tente outra Vez ou Cancele o Cupom!!!'+Chr(13)+;
						'Aperte SIM para continuar o sistema',4+16+256,'Atenção..')
		EndDo
		Return .f.
	EndIf
EndIf
ThisForm.SubTotAc = .f.
Return .t.

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE verstatus
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE verstatus
Local llRet

lcCaption = Thisform.cnt_mensagem.menSAGEM.Caption

lcNoCaixa  = ''
lcNoInterv = ''

oFormulario = Thisform.Parentform.oModemEest
llRet = .f.

If INLIST(CrSigCdOpe.CupFis,5,6)
	RETURN .t.
ENDIF

If CrSigCdOpe.CupFis = 1 And Not ThisForm.Parentform.CheckStat
	llRet = .t.
Else
	Thisform.cnt_mensagem.menSAGEM.Caption = 'Verificando status!!!'
	DO Case
		Case InList(crSigFiFis.Imps,'BEMATECH','BEMA8501','EPSON')
			VerStatus = RotStatus(' ',oFormulario)
			If Type('VerStatus')<>'L' And Not Empty(VerStatus)
				llRet = .t.
			EndIf
		
		Case InList(crSigFiFis.Imps,'DARUMA')
			VerStatus = RotStatus('NOK',oFormulario)
			If Type('VerStatus')<>'L' And Not Empty(VerStatus)
				llRet = .t.
			EndIf

		Case InList(crSigFiFis.Imps,'DATAREGIS','NDATAREGIS')
			VerStatus = RotStatus(3,oFormulario)
			If Type('VerStatus')<>'L' And Substr(VerStatus,2,1) = 'S'
				llRet = .t.
			EndIf
			
		Case InList(CrSigFiFis.Imps,'SWEDA','NSWEDA','ELGIN')
			VerStatus = RotStatus('NOK',oFormulario)
			If Type('VerStatus')<>'L' And Not Empty(VerStatus)
				llRet = .t.
			EndIf

		Case InList(CrSigFiFis.Imps,'S/IMPRESSORA')
			llRet = .F.

	EndCase
EndIf
If llRet
	Thisform.cnt_mensagem.menSAGEM.Caption = 'Verificando No Fabricação!!!'
	lnNoFabr = LeituraFab(oFormulario)
	If Type('lnNoFabr')='C'
		If !Empty(lnNoFabr)
			lcImpf = fDecriptografar(alltrim(lnNoFabr))
			lcWhere = [Select * From SigFiMpf Where cimpfabs = ']+lcImpf+[' And Emps=']+ThisForm.parentform.pEmps+[']
			LnErro = ThisForm.parentForm.Podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcWhere,'CrSigFiMpf')
			If LnErro < 1
				MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão (CrSigFiMpf 2)')
				llRet = .f.
			Else
				Select CrSigFiMpf
				If Reccount() = 0
					MessageBox('Impressora Fiscal Inválida para esta Loja !!',0+48,'ERRO')
					llRet = .f.
				EndIf
				If CrSigFiMpf.ImpAtivas
					MessageBox('Impressora Fiscal Inativa!!',0+48,'ERRO')
					llRet = .f.
				Endif
			EndIf
			If llRet
				ThisForm.parentform.pAbreGaveta = crSigFiMpf.Gavetas
				ThisForm.parentform.NoCaixa = crSigFiMpf.cnCaixas
				ThisForm.parentform.pimpcheque = CrSigFiMpf.Impches
				ThisForm.ParentForm.TemAutentica = ( CrSigFiMpf.Autenticas <> 2 )

				lcNoCaixa = crSigFiMpf.cnCaixas
				lcNoInterv = crSigFiMpf.cnIntervs

				If ThisForm.ParentForm.pAbreGaveta
					DO Case
						Case  InList(crSigFiFis.Imps,'DATAREGIS','NDATAREGIS')
							VerStatus = RotStatus(.t.,oFormulario)
							If Type('VerStatus')<>'L' And Substr(VerStatus,4,1) = 'S'
								DO Form SigOpGvt With CrSigFiFis.Imps,oFormulario
							Endif
						Case InList(CrSigFiFis.Imps,'SWEDA','NSWEDA')
							VerStatus = StatGaveta(oFormulario)
							If Type('VerStatus')<>'L' And VerStatus = '0'
								DO Form SigOpGvt With CrSigFiFis.Imps,oFormulario
							Endif
						Case InList(CrSigFiFis.Imps,'BEMATECH','BEMA8501','DARUMA')
							VerStatus = StatGaveta(oFormulario)
							If Type('VerStatus')<>'L' And VerStatus = '00'
								DO Form SigOpGvt With CrSigFiFis.Imps,oFormulario
							Endif
						Case InList(CrSigFiFis.Imps,'EPSON')
							VerStatus = StatGaveta(oFormulario)
							If Type('VerStatus')<>'L' And VerStatus = 0
								DO Form SigOpGvt With CrSigFiFis.Imps,oFormulario
							Endif
						Case CrSigFiFis.Imps = 'ELGIN'
							VerStatus = StatGaveta(oFormulario)
							If Type('VerStatus')<>'L' And VerStatus = '0'
								DO Form SigOpGvt With CrSigFiFis.Imps,oFormulario
							Endif
					EndCase
				Endif

				If InList(crSigFiFis.Imps,'DATAREGIS','NDATAREGIS') And Not ThisForm.Parentform.CheckStat
					*** Checar somente a primeira vez devido a demora de 5 segundos
				Else
					Thisform.cnt_mensagem.menSAGEM.Caption = 'Verificando No Caixa!!!'
					lcnCaixa = LeCaixa(oFormulario)
					If Empty(lcnCaixa)
						Messagebox('Não foi possível ler o No do Caixa da Impressora Fiscal, Tente Novamente !!',0+48,'')
						llRet = .f.
					Else
						If Not IsEmpty(CrSigOpCdc.CnCaixas) And CrSigOpCdc.CnCaixas <> lcnCaixa
							MessageBox('O Numero do Caixa Cadastrado na Operação está diferente da ECF'+Chr(13)+;
										'OPERAÇÃO:'+CrSigOpCdc.CnCaixas+'  ECF :'+lcnCaixa,0+16,'')
							llRet = .f.
						EndIf
						If ThisForm.parentform.NoCaixa <> lcnCaixa
							MessageBox('O Numero do Caixa do Cadastro de Impressora Fiscal'+Chr(13)+;
										'Nao confere com o No. do Caixa da ECF !!!'+Chr(13)+;
										'CADASTRO:'+ThisForm.ParentForm.NoCaixa+'  ECF :'+lcnCaixa,0+16,'')
							llRet = .f.
						Endif
					Endif
				EndIf
			Endif
		Else
			Messagebox('Não foi possível ler o No de Fabricação da Impressora Fiscal, Tente Novamente !!',0+48,'')
			llRet = .f.
		Endif
	Else
		Messagebox('Não foi possível ler o No de Fabricação da Impressora Fiscal, Tente Novamente !!',0+48,'')
		llRet = .f.
	Endif
	If llRet
		llDataPadrao = Datetime() &&ThisForm.ParentForm.PodataMgr.GetDatetime()
		Thisform.cnt_mensagem.menSAGEM.Caption = 'Verificando Data/Hora !!!'
		lcDataImp = LeituraDataHora(oFormulario)
		If !IsEmpty(lcDataImp)
			If Abs( ( llDataPadrao - Ctot(lcDataImp) ) / 60 ) > 15
				Messagebox('Hora do Micro Difere em Quinze Minutos da Hora da Impressora !!'+chr(13)+;
						'Micro : '+ttoc(llDataPadrao)+Chr(13)+;
						'  ECF : '+ttoc(Ctot(lcDataImp))+Chr(13),16,'ERRO')
				llRet = .f.
			Endif
			If Ttod(llDataPadrao) # Ttod(Ctot(lcDataImp))
				Messagebox('Data do Micro Difere da Data da Impressora !!'+chr(13)+;
						'Micro : '+ttoc(llDataPadrao)+Chr(13)+;
						'  ECF : '+ttoc(Ctot(lcDataImp))+Chr(13),16,'ERRO')
				llRet = .f.
			EndIf
		Else
			Messagebox('Erro na Leitura da Data da Impressora Fiscal, Tente novamente !!',0+48,'')
			llRet = .f.
		Endif
	EndIf

	If llRet And Not ThisForm.Valida24hs(Ctot(lcDataImp),lnNoFabr )
		llRet = .f.
	Endif

	Thisform.cnt_mensagem.menSAGEM.Caption = ' '
	If llRet
		llRet = ThisForm.parentform.Valida_Bemat()
	EndIf
	
	If llRet And InList(CrSigFiFis.Imps,'DATAREGIS','NDATAREGIS','DARUMA','BEMATECH','BEMA8501','S/IMPRESSORA','NSWEDA','EPSON') ;
				And CrSigCdPam.CodOpers = 1 And goSistema.Operador = '000'
		DO Form SIGOPPRR
		If goSistema.Operador = '000'
			MessageBox('Código do Operador Não Cadastrado!!!',0+48,'Obrigatório Operador')
			llRet = .f.
		Endif
	Endif

	If llRet
		Replace TprMvCab.NEmps with lnNoFabr ;
			    TprMvCab.ecfs  with lcNoCaixa+lcNoInterv In TprMvCab

		=ThisForm.Podatamgr.Cursorquery('SigCdUsu','CrUsu','Usuarios',goSistema.Operador,[nComps])
		ThisForm.Caption = AllTrim( TprMvCab.Dopes ) + '     [ Documento : '+Str(TprMvCab.Numes,6)+'] '+Space(40)+'ECF: '+Iif(Used('crSigFiMpf'),crSigFiMpf.cnCaixas,'')+Space(20)+'Operador(a) : '+goSistema.Operador+' '+Alltrim(crUsu.nComps)
	Endif
EndIf

Thisform.cnt_mensagem.menSAGEM.Caption = lcCaption

Return (llRet)
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE gradelcfe
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE gradelcfe
*** mena 08/12/2016 - grava tabela da operacao e Nfe - como cancelado antes de tentar emitir
parameters pnGraDel
LOCAL llOks, lcEdn

IF pnGraDel = 1
	Insert Into crSigPrNfe (EmpDopNums, Emps, Dopes, Numes, Datas, Usuars, Arquivos, cIdChaves, ids, lotes, recibos, prots, stats, statts) ;
					Values (TprMvCab.empdopnums, TprMvCab.Emps, TprMvCab.Dopes, ;
							TprMvCab.Numes, Datetime(), Usuar, '', fUniqueIds(), '', '', '', '', ;
							'CFE', '0')
	replace cancelas with .T. in crSigMvNfi
	replace nfis with ''  in crSigMvNfi
	replace totprods with 0.00 in crSigMvNfi	&& mena 04/04/2017 - zera valores para caso seja cancelado - Brantes

	SELECT * from TprMvCab into cursor crSigmvCab readwrite
	replace lcancelas with .T. in crSigMvCab
	replace ncupoms with 'NAOFIN' in crSigMvCab
	replace notas with '' in crSigMvCab
	replace valos with 0.00 in crSigMvCab		&& mena 04/04/2017 - zera valores para caso seja cancelado - Brantes

	pnGraDel = 5
ENDIF

IF pnGraDel = 2
	SELECT crSigmvNfi
	DELETE ALL
	SELECT crSigPrnfe
	DELETE ALL
	SELECT crSigmvcab
	DELETE ALL
*!*		SELECT crSigmvitn
*!*		DELETE ALL
*!*		SELECT crSigmvits
*!*		DELETE ALL
*!*		SELECT crSigmvpar
*!*		DELETE ALL

	pnGraDel = 5
ENDIF

IF pnGraDel = 3		&& mena 12/12/2016 - update e comit nas tres tabelas
	replace totProds with pnValNf in crSigMvNfi
	pnGraDel = 5
ENDIF

*** 10/05/2017 - volta pendencias ***
IF pnGraDel = 4
	*** mena - 03/04/2017 - volta pendencia ***
	IF USED('tmppends')
		SELECT tmppends
		GO top
		SCAN for flag
			lcEDN = tmppends.Emps + tmppends.Dopes + Str( tmppends.Numes, 6 )
			lcUpDate = [Update SigMvCab Set ChkSubn = 0 Where EmpDopNums = ']+lcEdn+[']
			If (ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcUpDate,'') < 1)
				=MessageBox([Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!], 16, [Falha na Conexão (Update - crSigMvCab)])
				llOks = .f.
			ENDIF
		ENDSCAN
	ENDIF
		
	If llOks
		If (ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr.Commit() < 1)
			=MessageBox([Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!], 16, [Falha na Conexão (Commit)])
			llOks = .f.
		EndIf
	ENDIF
ENDIF

IF pnGraDel = 5

	llOks=.t.
	If Not ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr.Update([crSigMvNfi])
		=MessageBox([Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!], 16, [Falha na Conexão (Update - crSigMvNfi)])
		llOks = .f.
	EndIf
	If Not ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr.Update([crSigPrNfe])
		=MessageBox([Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!], 16, [Falha na Conexão (Update - crSigPrNfe)])
		llOks = .f.
	EndIf
	If Not ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr.Update([crSigMvCab])
		=MessageBox([Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!], 16, [Falha na Conexão (Update - crSigMvCab)])
		llOks = .f.
	EndIf

*!*		SELECT * from crTpmmvitn where cpros <> ''  into cursor crSigmvitn readwrite
*!*		SELECT * from crTpmmvits where cpros <> ''  into cursor crSigmvits readwrite
*!*		SELECT * from xPar where fpags <> ''  into cursor crSigmvpar readwrite
*!*		
*!*		If Not ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr.Update([crSigMvItn])
*!*			=MessageBox([Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!], 16, [Falha na Conexão (Update - crSigMvItn)])
*!*			llOks = .f.
*!*		EndIf
*!*		If Not ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr.Update([crSigMvIts])
*!*			=MessageBox([Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!], 16, [Falha na Conexão (Update - crSigMvIts)])
*!*			llOks = .f.
*!*		EndIf
*!*		If Not ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr.Update([crSigMvPar])
*!*			=MessageBox([Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!], 16, [Falha na Conexão (Update - crSigMvPar)])
*!*			llOks = .f.
*!*		ENDIF
	
	If llOks
		If (ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr.Commit() < 1)
			=MessageBox([Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!], 16, [Falha na Conexão (Commit)])
			llOks = .f.
		ENDIF
	ENDIF
ENDIF

RETURN llOks

ENDPROC

	.SetAll('DynamicForecolor','(Iif(crTpmMvItnc.Cores=0,Rgb(0,0,255),Iif(crTpmMvItnC.Cores=1,Rgb(0,0,160),Iif(crTpmMvItnC.Cores=2,Rgb(0,0,64),Iif(crTpmMvItnC.Cores=3,Rgb(64,0,64),Rgb(255,0,0))))))','Column')

With ThisForm
	With .Barra_Botoes.Apaga
		.Enabled = InList( Thisform.Op_Escolha, 'INSERIR', 'ALTERAR' ) And Thisform.ParentForm.CancelaItem
	EndWith
	
	With .CntItem
		.cnt_pdesc.Get_PValDesc.Enabled = ( CrSigCdOpe.chkDVal = 1 ) And ( CrSigCdOpe.OpCrs # 4 )
		.cnt_pdesc.Get_PValDesc.Visible = ( CrSigCdOpe.chkDVal = 1 ) And ( CrSigCdOpe.OpCrs # 4 )
		.cnt_vdesc.Get_ValDesc.Enabled  = ( CrSigCdOpe.chkDVal = 1 ) And ( CrSigCdOpe.OpCrs # 4 )
		.cnt_vdesc.Get_ValDesc.Visible  = ( CrSigCdOpe.chkDVal = 1 ) And ( CrSigCdOpe.OpCrs # 4 )
		
		ThisForm.TemAcrescimo = fChecaAcesso( padl(CrSigCdOpe.nDopes,8,'0'), "ACRESCIMO" )

		.cnt_pacre.Get_PValAcre.Enabled = ( CrSigCdOpe.chkDVal = 1 ) And ( CrSigCdOpe.OpCrs # 4 ) And ThisForm.TemAcrescimo 
		.cnt_pacre.Get_PValAcre.Visible = ( CrSigCdOpe.chkDVal = 1 ) And ( CrSigCdOpe.OpCrs # 4 ) And ThisForm.TemAcrescimo 
		.cnt_vacre.Get_ValAcre.Enabled  = ( CrSigCdOpe.chkDVal = 1 ) And ( CrSigCdOpe.OpCrs # 4 ) And ThisForm.TemAcrescimo 
		.cnt_vacre.Get_ValAcre.Visible  = ( CrSigCdOpe.chkDVal = 1 ) And ( CrSigCdOpe.OpCrs # 4 ) And ThisForm.TemAcrescimo 

	EndWith

EndWith

With ThisForm.Cnt_Identifica 
	Select TprMvCab
	lcQuery = [Select Iclis, Rclis, Cpfs From SigCdCli Where Iclis = ']+TprMvCab.Contaos+[']
	If (ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'CrTmpCli') < 1)
		=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (CrTmpCli)')
		Return 0
	EndIf
	.origem.get_dconta.value = CrTmpcli.rclis
	.origem.get_cpf.value = CrTmpcli.cpfs

	lcQuery = [Select Iclis, Rclis, Cpfs From SigCdCli Where Iclis = ']+TprMvCab.ContaDs+[']
	If (ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'CrTmpCli') < 1)
		=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (CrTmpCli 2)')
		Return 0
	EndIf
	.destino.get_dconta.value = crtmpcli.rclis
	.destino.get_cpf.value = crtmpcli.cpfs

	If InList( crSigCdOpe.Vendes, 1, 2 )
		lcQuery = [Select Iclis, Rclis, Cpfs From SigCdCli Where Iclis = ']+TprMvCab.Vends+[']
		If (ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'CrTmpCli') < 1)
			=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (CrTmpCli 3)')
			Return 0
		EndIf
		.vendedor.get_dconta.value = CrTmpcli.rclis
		.vendedor.get_cpf.value = CrTmpcli.cpfs
	Else
		.vendedor.get_dconta.value = ''
		.vendedor.get_cpf.value    = ''
	Endif

	If InList( crSigCdOpe.ComisDivs, 1, 3, 4, 5 )
		lcQuery = [Select Iclis, Rclis, Cpfs From SigCdCli Where Iclis = ']+TprMvCab.Resps+[']
		If (ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'CrTmpCli') < 1)
			=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (CrTmpCli 4)')
			Return 0
		EndIf
		.Responsavel.Get_DConta.Value = CrTmpCli.Rclis
		.Responsavel.Get_Cpf.Value = CrTmpCli.Cpfs
	Else
		.Responsavel.Get_DConta.Value = ''
		.Responsavel.Get_Cpf.Value = ''
	Endif

	If !IsEmpty(crSigOpCdc.MascPedCls)
		.CodOperacao.Get_PedCli.InputMask = Alltrim(CrSigOpCdc.MascPedCls)
		.CodOperacao.Get_PedCli.Width = 20 + ( Len(Alltrim(CrSigOpCdc.MascPedCls)) * 7 )
	Endif

EndWith

ThisForm.Barra_Botoes.Altpreço.Enabled= ( Thisform.lAlteraPreco )
ThisForm.Barra_Botoes.credito.Enabled= ( crSigCdOpe.Vendas = 1 And InList(crSigCdOpe.Opcrs, 1, 3) )
ThisForm.Barra_Botoes.pendencia.Enabled= InList( crSigCdOpe.SubNs, 1, 3, 4 )
ThisForm.Barra_Botoes.CancPagto.Enabled= .F.
ThisForm.Barra_Botoes.CancPagto.Enabled= .F.
ThisForm.Barra_Botoes.Apaga.Enabled = .T.
Thisform.Barra_Botoes.Alteraqtd.Enabled= .T.
ThisForm.Barra_Botoes.Alteraqtd.Enabled = .T.
ThisForm.Barra_Botoes.Altpreço.Enabled = .T.
Thisform.Barra_Botoes.Desconto.Enabled= .f.
Thisform.Barra_Botoes.Acrescimo.Enabled= .f.
ThisForm.Barra_Botoes.Impfiscal.Enabled= ( CrSigFiFis.Imps <> 'S/IMPRESSORA' )
ThisForm.Barra_Botoes.pagto.Enabled= .t.
ThisForm.Barra_Botoes.Financia.Enabled= ( CrSigOpCdd.RcFinas = 1 And Not InList(crSigCdOpe.SubNs,1,3,4) And CrSigCdOpe.CupFis = 3 )

*** mena - 26/02/2015 - retirado para testes ***
*!*	ThisForm.Cnt_Fpagto.desconto.get_PorcVariação.Value = 0
*!*	ThisForm.Cnt_Fpagto.Acrescimo.get_PorcVariação.Value = 0
ThisForm.Cnt_Fpagto.desconto.get_TotVariação.Value	= 0
ThisForm.Cnt_Fpagto.Acrescimo.get_TotVariação.Value	= 0

ThisForm.Total_item()

Thisform.MontaTela()

ThisForm.lcFoco = ''
With ThisForm.Cnt_Identifica
	.Refresh
	DO Case
		Case .CodOperacao.Visible And InList( crSigCdOpe.CupFis, 1,3 ) And InList(crSigCdOpe.Numeras,3,4)
			ThisForm.lcFoco = 'ThisForm.Cnt_Identifica.CodOperacao.Get_Codigo.SetFocus'

		Case .CodOperacao.Visible And InList( crSigCdOpe.CupFis, 1,3 ) And InList(crSigCdOpe.PedClis,1,3)
			ThisForm.lcFoco = 'ThisForm.Cnt_Identifica.CodOperacao.Get_PedCli.SetFocus'

		Case .TabDesconto.Visible And InList( crSigCdOpe.tabdescs, 1, 2, 4, 5 ) And Empty(.TabDesconto.Get_tabd.Value)
			ThisForm.lcFoco = 'ThisForm.Cnt_Identifica.TabDesconto.Get_Tabd.SetFocus'

		Case .TabDesconto.Visible And crSigCdOpe.Precops = 1 And InList( crSigCdOpe.LPrecos, 1, 2, 4, 5 ) And ;
				Empty(.TabDesconto.Get_ListaPreco.Value) And .TabDesconto.Get_ListaPreco.Visible
			ThisForm.lcFoco = 'ThisForm.Cnt_Identifica.TabDesconto.Get_ListaPreco.SetFocus'

		Case .Origem.Visible And Empty(.Origem.Get_Conta.Value)
			Thisform.lcfoco = 'ThisForm.Cnt_identifica.Origem.get_conta.Setfocus'
			
		Case .Destino.Visible And Empty(.Destino.Get_Conta.Value)
			ThisForm.lcfoco = 'ThisForm.Cnt_identifica.Destino.get_conta.Setfocus'

		Case .Vendedor.Visible And Empty(.Vendedor.Get_Conta.Value) And CrSigCdOpe.ObrigVds # 1
			ThisForm.lcfoco = 'ThisForm.Cnt_identifica.Vendedor.get_conta.Setfocus'
		
		Case .Responsavel.Visible And Empty(.Responsavel.Get_conta.Value) And CrSigCdOpe.ObrigVd2s # 1
			ThisForm.lcfoco = 'ThisForm.Cnt_identifica.Responsavel.get_conta.Setfocus'
	EndCase
EndWith

ThisForm.Cnt_Fpagto.cnt_forma.get_fpg.Value = ' '
ThisForm.Cnt_FPagto.cnt_descp.get_tvariacao.Value = 0

ThisForm.LiberaIdent = .f.
If InList(crSigCdOpe.ImpClis,[S],[O]) And Not (crSigCdOpe.SubnObrigs = 1)
	ThisForm.lcfoco = ' '
	ThisForm.LiberaIdent = .t.
	If ThisForm.CntItem.Visible
		ThisForm.Cnt_mensagem.Mensagem.Caption = '[F2] - '+Iif(CrSigFiFis.Imps = 'S/IMPRESSORA','Abrir Operação','Abrir Cupom Fiscal')
	Else
		If CrSigCdOpe.CupFis = 3
			ThisForm.Cnt_mensagem.Mensagem.Caption = '[F11] - Abrir '+Iif(CrSigFiFis.Imps = 'S/IMPRESSORA','Operação','Cupom Não Fiscal')
		Else
			ThisForm.Cnt_mensagem.Mensagem.Caption = '[F11] - Abrir '+Iif(CrSigFiFis.Imps = 'S/IMPRESSORA','Operação','Cupom Fiscal')
		Endif
	Endif
	ThisForm.Barra_Botoes.Identifica.Click()
Else
	If ThisForm.CntItem.Visible
		ThisForm.CntItem.Cnt_Prod.Get_Produto.Enabled = .f.
		ThisForm.CntItem.Proximo.Setfocus
		ThisForm.Cnt_mensagem.Mensagem.Caption = '[F2] - Abrir '+Iif(CrSigFiFis.Imps = 'S/IMPRESSORA','Operação','Cupom Fiscal')
	Else
		ThisForm.Barra_Botoes.Altpreço.Enabled= .f.
		ThisForm.Cnt_Fpagto.cnt_pag.get_totforma.Value = 0
		ThisForm.Cnt_Fpagto.cnt_saldo.get_totliquido.Value = 0
		ThisForm.Cnt_FPagto.cnt_descp.get_tvariacao.Value = 0
		ThisForm.Cnt_Fpagto.cnt_apag.get_saldo.Value = 0
		ThisForm.Cnt_Fpagto.Visible = .t.
		ThisForm.CntGrade.Visible = .t.
		ThisForm.Cnt_Fpagto.cnt_forma.get_fpg.Enabled = .f.
		ThisForm.Cnt_fpagto.Proximo.Setfocus
		If CrSigCdOpe.CupFis = 3
			ThisForm.Cnt_mensagem.Mensagem.Caption = '[F11] - Abrir '+Iif(CrSigFiFis.Imps = 'S/IMPRESSORA','Operação','Cupom Não Fiscal')
		Else
			ThisForm.Cnt_mensagem.Mensagem.Caption = '[F11] - Abrir '+Iif(CrSigFiFis.Imps = 'S/IMPRESSORA','Operação','Cupom Fiscal')
		Endif
	EndIf
Endif

******************************************************************
**** Mena - 04/06/2013 - inclui o valor do imposto aproximado ****
**** Mena - 02/02/2015 - retirado e substituido em outro trecho abaixo ***
*!*	lcAImpos =  goSistema.DirFiscais + 'IBPTAX.TXT'
*!*	IF FILE(lcAImpos)
*!*		Create Cursor IBPTAX (c1 c(10), c2 c(10), c3 c(10), c4 c(10), c5 c(10))
*!*		SELECT IBPTAX
*!*		ZAP
*!*		APPEND FROM &lcAImpos DELIMITED WITH CHARACTER ";"
*!*	ENDIF
*!*	thisform.ParentForm.impostoa=0
******************************************************************
thisform.ParentForm.impostoF=0
thisform.ParentForm.impostoE=0
thisform.ParentForm.impostoM=0
*********************************************************

=criaFP()	&&Mena 16/06/2015 - cria cursor com formas de pagamento para NFCe ***

Thisform.LockScreen = .F.


ENDPROC

PROCEDURE KeyPress
LPARAMETERS nKeyCode, nShiftAltCtrl

LoBotoes = ThisForm.Barra_Botoes
lcItem = ThisForm.CntItem.Cnt_Prod.Get_Produto.Value

*** mena - 26/02/2015 - retirado para testes ***
If Not ThisForm.Cnt_fpagto.Cnt_ChequepreTef.Visible And Not ThisForm.CancelaItem.Visible
	*!*If  Not ThisForm.CancelaItem.Visible
	Do Case
		Case nKeyCode = -2 And ThisForm.Cnt_pendencia.Visible And ThisForm.Cnt_Pendencia.Bot_Pendentes.Enabled && F3
			NoDefault
			ThisForm.Cnt_pendencia.Bot_Pendentes.Click()

*!*			Case nKeyCode = -2 And ThisForm.Cnt_Credito.Visible And ThisForm.Cnt_Credito.Bot_Pendentes.Enabled && F3
*!*				NoDefault
*!*				ThisForm.Cnt_Credito.Bot_Pendentes.Click()

*!*			*** Tiago - 09/08/2013 - Alteração do crediário de carne
*!*			Case nKeyCode = -1 And ThisForm.CntFinanc.Visible
*!*				NoDefault
*!*				ThisForm.CntFinanc.Cnt_GrdBarra.Inserir.Click()

*!*			Case nKeyCode = -3 And ThisForm.CntFinanc.Visible
*!*				NoDefault
*!*				ThisForm.CntFinanc.Cnt_GrdBarra.Excluir.Click()

*!*			Case nKeyCode = -4 And ThisForm.CntFinanc.Visible
*!*				NoDefault
*!*				ThisForm.CntFinanc.Cnt_GrdBarra.Ok.Click()
*!*			***			

*!*			Case nKeyCode = 137 And loBotoes.MudaOpe.visible And Empty(lcItem)&& ALT+F11
*!*				NoDefault
*!*				loBotoes.MudaOpe.Click()

*!*			Case nKeyCode = 16 And Empty(lcItem)&& CTRL+P
*!*				NoDefault
*!*				loBotoes.VerPreco.Click()
		
		Case nKeyCode = -5 And loBotoes.Identifica.enabled And ;
					Empty(lcItem) And Not ThisForm.Inicia And ThisForm.LiberaIdent && F6
			NoDefault
			loBotoes.Identifica.Click()
		
	Otherwise
			If ThisForm.validado
				Do Case

					Case nKeyCode = -1 And loBotoes.Itens.Enabled And Empty(lcItem) && F2
						NoDefault
						ThisForm.Barra_Botoes.Impfiscal.Enabled= .f.
						loBotoes.Itens.Click()

					Case nKeyCode = -2 And ThisForm.CntItem.Visible And ;
							loBotoes.Alteraqtd.enabled And ;
								ThisForm.CntItem.cnt_Prod.Get_Produto.Enabled And Empty(lcItem)&& F3
						NoDefault
						loBotoes.Alteraqtd.Click()

					Case nKeyCode = -3 And ThisForm.CntItem.Visible And ;
							loBotoes.Apaga.enabled And loBotoes.Apaga.Enabled And ;
								Not ThisForm.lAlteraqtd And Empty(lcItem) And ThisForm.Inicia && F4
						NoDefault
						loBotoes.Apaga.Click()

					Case nKeyCode = -4 And ThisForm.CntItem.Visible And ;
							loBotoes.AltPreço.enabled && F5
						NoDefault
						loBotoes.AltPreço.Click()


					Case nKeyCode = 133 And loBotoes.Pagto.enabled And Empty(lcItem)&& F11
						NoDefault
						loBotoes.Pagto.Click()

					Case nKeyCode = -3 And ThisForm.Cnt_Fpagto.Visible And ;
							loBotoes.CancPagto.enabled && F4
						NoDefault
						loBotoes.CancPagto.Click()

					Case nKeyCode = -2 And ThisForm.Cnt_Fpagto.Visible And ;
						ThisForm.Cnt_Fpagto.cnt_tot.get_valinic.Value <> 0 And loBotoes.Desconto.enabled && F3
						NoDefault
						loBotoes.Desconto.Click()

					Case nKeyCode = 96 And ThisForm.Cnt_Fpagto.Visible And ;
						ThisForm.Cnt_Fpagto.cnt_tot.get_valinic.Value <> 0 And loBotoes.Acrescimo.enabled && CTRL + F3
						NoDefault
						loBotoes.Acrescimo.Click()

					Case nKeyCode = -5 And loBotoes.Identifica.enabled And ;
							Empty(lcItem) And ThisForm.Inicia && F6
						NoDefault
						ThisForm.Barra_Botoes.Impfiscal.Enabled = .f.
						loBotoes.Identifica.Click()

*** mena - 26/02/2015 - retirado para testes ***
*!*						Case nKeyCode = -6 And loBotoes.Credito.Visible And ;
*!*								Empty(lcItem) And ThisForm.Inicia && F7
*!*							NoDefault
*!*							ThisForm.Barra_Botoes.Impfiscal.Visible = .f.
*!*							loBotoes.credito.Click()

					Case nKeyCode = -7 And loBotoes.Pendencia.enabled And ;
							Empty(lcItem) And ThisForm.Inicia && F8
						NoDefault
						ThisForm.Barra_Botoes.Impfiscal.Enabled = .f.
						loBotoes.Pendencia.Click()

					Case nKeyCode = -7 And loBotoes.Financia.Enabled And ;
							Empty(lcItem) And ThisForm.Inicia && F8
						NoDefault
						ThisForm.Barra_Botoes.Impfiscal.Enabled = .f.
						loBotoes.Financia.Click()						

					Case nKeyCode = -2 And ThisForm.Cnt_pendencia.Visible And ;
							ThisForm.Cnt_Pendencia.Bot_Pendentes.Enabled && F3
						NoDefault
						ThisForm.Cnt_pendencia.Bot_Pendentes.Click()

*** mena - 26/02/2015 - retirado para testes ***
*!*						Case nKeyCode = -2 And ThisForm.Cnt_Credito.Visible And ;
*!*								ThisForm.cnt_Credito.Bot_Pendentes.Enabled && F3
*!*							NoDefault
*!*							ThisForm.Cnt_Credito.Bot_Pendentes.Click()

					Case nKeyCode = -8 And loBotoes.ImpFiscal.enabled And Empty(lcItem)&& F9
						NoDefault
						loBotoes.Impfiscal.Click()

					Case nKeyCode = 11 And ( Thisform.Cnt_identifica.Visible  Or ; && Ctrl+K - chama tela de calculadora...
							(ThisForm.Cnt_fpagto.Visible And Empty(ThisForm.Cnt_fpagto.cnt_forma.get_fpg.Value)) Or ;
							(ThisForm.CntItem.Visible And Empty(ThisForm.CntItem.Cnt_Prod.Get_Produto.Value) And ;
							Not ThisForm.lAlteraqtd ))
						NoDefault
						*** mena - 26/02/2015 - retirado para testes ***
						*!* = fClassCalc(ThisForm.Top, (ThisForm.Left + ThisForm.CaixaLivre.Left), ThisForm)

				EndCase
			EndIf
	EndCase
EndIf

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Click
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Click
Select crTpmMvItn
Go Top
Scan
	Replace ImpCupFis With .T.
	ThisForm.Atuacupom()
	Select crTpmMvItn
EndScan

IF !thisform.parentform.aciosalva(5) 
	ThisForm.Cnt_pendencia.setfocus
	RETURN .f.
ENDIF

*** mena 09/07/2016 mostra os itens na grade de itens do PDV quando SAT/NFCe ***
If INLIST(CrSigCdOpe.CupFis,5,6)
	*** mena 12/07/2016 - inclui grade para apresentar formas de pagamento ***
	IF crSigCdOpe.ParAutos = 1 
		SELECT PADR(fpags,20)+PADL(ALLTRIM(STR(COUNT(fpags)))+' X '+ALLTRIM(TRANSFORM(MAX(valos),'@ 9999,999.99')),20) as linha from xParTef group by fpags into cursor tmpPar
		ZAP in 'xParC'
		SELECT tmpPar	
		SCAN
			SCATTER memvar
			INSERT into xParC (texto) values (m.linha)
		ENDSCAN
		SELECT xParC
		GO top
		thisform.grdFp.colUMN1.ControlSource = 'xParC.Texto'
		Thisform.grdFP.visible=.t.
		Thisform.grdFP.Refresh
	ENDIf
*!*		SELECT crTpmMvItn
*!*		GO top
*!*		SCAN
*!*			thisform.atuacupom() 
*!*		ENDSCAN
ENDIF
ThisForm.Cnt_pendencia.Proximo.picture = '\vbmp\b_arrow2.bmp'
ThisForm.Cnt_pendencia.Proximo.refresh

If ThisForm.Barra_Botoes.Itens.Enabled Or INLIST(CrSigCdOpe.SubNObrigs,1,4) 
	ThisForm.Barra_Botoes.Itens.Click()
Else
	ThisForm.Barra_Botoes.Pagto.SetFocus
	ThisForm.Barra_Botoes.Pagto.Click()
Endif

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE GotFocus
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE GotFocus
Select [ ] as Agrupar, sum(1) as Qt From TprMvCabV Where Not Empty(Contas) Into Cursor TotEestV group by 1
If TotEestv.Qt > 0
	ThisForm.Cnt_identifica.Vendedor.Get_conta.Value = ' '
	ThisForm.Cnt_identifica.Vendedor.get_cpf.Value = ' '
	ThisForm.Cnt_identifica.Vendedor.get_dconta.Value = ' '
EndIf
ThisForm.Cnt_pendencia.Proximo.picture = '\vbmp\b_arrow4.bmp'
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE LostFocus
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE LostFocus
ThisForm.Cnt_pendencia.Proximo.picture = '\vbmp\b_arrow2.bmp'
ENDPROC

PROCEDURE Click
If crSigOpCdi.OrigDest = 1 And Empty(TprMvCab.ContaOs)
	MessageBox('Conta de ' + ThisForm.CfgTitOrigem + ' Obrigatória!',0+48,'Aviso')

	Return .f.
