================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGMVITS.cntItem.Cnt_Prod.Get_Produto
* Classe: fwget (Base: textbox)
* Registro: 43
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE When
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE When
Local lRetorno
ThisForm.Preencheu = .f.
Thisform.AntCPro = crTpmMvItn.Cpros
Thisform.AntValu = crTpmMvItn.Units
ThisForm.CntItem.Cnt_prod.cmdenviaprod.Visible = .f.
Set Confirm on
Thisform.Barra_Botoes.Altpreço.Enabled = .f.
lRetorno = ( InList( ThisForm.Op_Escolha, 'INSERIR', 'ALTERAR' ) And Empty( This.Value ) )
		
Return (lRetorno)
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Valid
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Valid
Local lcod,lfan,codcor,xobj,oper,precop,cuni,moec,moev,pcus,pven,cmoe,;
		fcusto,fvenda,llProducao, lcArquivo3, lnFator

Declare laCampo(2,2)
If !Empty(This.Value)
	Go Top in CrSigCdPam
	oper   = CrSigCdOpe.Opers
	precop = CrSigCdOpe.PrecoPs
	cpro   = ''
	dpro   = ''
	peso   = 0
	qtd    = 0 

	If Thisform.CntItem.cnt_qtd.Get_Qtds.Value = 0
		Thisform.CntItem.cnt_qtd.Get_qtds.Value = 1
		ThisForm.Preencheu = .t.
	EndIf
	qtd = Thisform.CntItem.cnt_qtd.Get_qtds.Value
	Thisform.CntItem.cnt_qtd.Get_qtds.Refresh

	Select crTpmMvItn
	Goto Bottom

	SNumero = fVerificarBarras( This.Value )
	If snumero
		ck=.t.
		Select crTpmMvItn
		_Recnx = Recno()
		_Ordem = Order()
		Set Order to CodBarras
		If Seek( Val( This.Value ) ) And Recno() = _Recnx
			Skip
		Endif

		If !Eof() And CodBarras = Val( This.Value ) And CrSigOpCdc.AltEtiqs <> 4
			Messagebox( 'Código de Barras já digitado nesta Operação...', 48, '' )
			Set Order to &_Ordem
			Go _Recnx
			This.Value = Thisform.antcpro
			With Thisform.CntItem
				.Cnt_qtd.Get_Qtds.Value     = 0
				.Cnt_val.Get_Valor.Value    = 0
				.Cnt_valu.Get_UniVal.Value   = 0
				.Cnt_vdesc.Get_ValDesc.Value  = 0
				.Cnt_pdesc.Get_PValDesc.Value = 0
			EndWith
			Return 0
		Endif
			
		Set Order to &_Ordem
		Go _Recnx
		Set decimals to 0
		lcSql = [Select Contas,Empos,Cpros,Qtds,Pesos,Cbars, codCors, codTams From SigOpEtq Where Cbars = ]+Str(Val(This.Value),14)
		If ThisForm.Podatamgr.Sqlexecute(lcSql,'CrTmpEti') < 1
			MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
			Return 0
		Endif
		
		If Reccount('CrTmpEti') > 0 
			Do Case
				Case Empty( CrTmpEti.Contas ) And CrSigCdOpe.Estoqs = 1 And Val( This.Value ) # crTpmMvItn.CodBarras
					Messagebox( 'Esta etiqueta já foi vendida!', 48, '' )
					This.Value = Thisform.antcpro
					Return 0

				Case CrSigCdOpe.EstOrigs = 2 And TprMvCab.Contaos # CrTmpEti.Contas And CrSigCdOpe.Estoqs = 1 And Val( This.Value ) # crTpmMvItn.CodBarras
					Messagebox( 'Esta etiqueta não pertence a este estoque!', 48, '' )
					This.Value = Thisform.antcpro
					Return 0

				Case CrSigCdOpe.EstDests = 2 And TprMvCab.Contads # CrTmpEti.Contas And CrSigCdOpe.Estoqs = 1 And Val( This.Value ) # crTpmMvItn.CodBarras 
					Messagebox( 'Esta etiqueta não pertence a este estoque!', 48, '' )
					This.Value = Thisform.antcpro
					Return 0

				*!*Case CrSigCdOpe.Transs = 1 And TprMvCab.Emps # CrTmpEti.Empos And CrSigCdOpe.Estoqs = 1 And Val( This.Value ) # crTpmMvItn.CodBarras 
				*** mena 15/07/2015 - checa se empresa ok mesmo sem o flag de trans ***
				Case TprMvCab.Emps # CrTmpEti.Empos And CrSigCdOpe.Estoqs = 1 And Val( This.Value ) # crTpmMvItn.CodBarras 
					Messagebox( 'Esta etiqueta não pertence a esta empresa!', 48, '' )
					This.Value = Thisform.antcpro
					Return 0

				Other
					CPro = CrTmpEti.Cpros
					If Empty( crTpmMvItn.qtds )
						qtd=crtmpeti.qtds
					Else
						qtd=crTpmMvItn.qtds
					EndIf
					peso=crtmpeti.pesos
					replace crTpmMvItn.etiesps with .t., ;
							crTpmMvItn.codbarras with val(This.Value) in crTpmMvItn
					This.Value = Cpro
					ck=.f.
					lcQuePro = [Cpros,Dpros,Cunis,Situas,fVendas,fCustos,Pesoms,Moepcs,Moepvs,Pvens,Pcuss,Valors,CodFinP,]+;
								[FigJpgs,Cbars,Moevs,Moecs,Moedas,cGrus,Linhas,Colecoes,mUltComps,vUltComps,mercs,cftios,sgrus]

					lcSql = [Select Cpros,Dpros,Cunis,Situas,fVendas,fCustos,Pesoms,Moepcs,Moepvs,Pvens,Pcuss,CodFinP,]+;
							[Valors,FigJpgs,Cbars,Moevs,Moecs,Moedas,cGrus,Linhas,Colecoes,mUltComps,vUltComps,mercs,cftios,sgrus ]+;
							[From SigCdPro Where Cpros = ']+Cpro+[']
					If ThisForm.Podatamgr.Sqlexecute(lcSql,'CrTmpPro') < 1
						MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
						Return 0
					Endif
			Endcase
		Endif
		Set decimals to 2
		If ck
			lcQuePro = [Cpros,Dpros,Cunis,Situas,fVendas,fCustos,Pesoms,Moepcs,Moepvs,Pvens,Pcuss,Valors,FigJpgs,Cbars,Moevs,Moecs,Moedas,cGrus,Linhas,Colecoes]
			lcSql = [Select Cpros, Cbars From SigCdPro Where Cbars = ]+Str(Val(This.Value),14)
			If Thisform.Podatamgr.Sqlexecute(lcsql,'LocalPro') < 1
				MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
				Return 0
			EndIf
			If Reccount('LocalPro') = 0
				lcProduto = Space(14)
				DO form SigOpBus With .t. TO lcProduto
				This.Value = lcProduto
				If Empty(This.Value)
					Return 0
				Endif
			Endif
			lcSql = [Select Cpros,Dpros,Cunis,Situas,fVendas,fCustos,Pesoms,Moepcs,Moepvs,Pvens,Pcuss,Valors,CodFinP,]+;
					[FigJpgs,Cbars,Moevs,Moecs,Moedas,cGrus,Linhas,Colecoes,mUltComps,vUltComps,mercs,cftios,sgrus ]+;
					[From SigCdPro Where Cbars = ]+Str(Val(This.Value),14)

			If Thisform.Podatamgr.Sqlexecute(lcsql,'CrTmpPro') < 1
				MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
				Return 0
			EndIf

			If Reccount('CrTmpPro') > 0
				=ThisForm.parentform.podatamgr.cursorquery('SigCdUni','CrTmpUni','Cunis',CrTmpPro.Cunis)
				If CrTmpUni.etiqs='S'  And CrSigCdOpe.SemEtiqs # 1 And ( CrSigCdOpe.opers = 2 Or ( CrSigCdOpe.Opers = 3 And crTpmMvItn.opers = 'S' ) )
					messagebox('Código Errado - Etiqueta Especial !!',0+48,'')
					This.Value=''
					this.Refresh
					Thisform.validok=.f.
					return 0
				Else
					If ( CrSigCdOpe.ItSituas = 1 And crtmpPro.Situas = 2 ) Or ;
					   ( CrSigCdOpe.ItSituas = 2 And crtmpPro.Situas # 2 )
						MessageBox('Situação do Produto ' + AllTrim(crtmpPro.Cpros) + ': ' + ;
						    Iif(CrTmpPro.Situas = 2,'Inativo','Ativo') + '.' + Chr(13) + ;
						    'Operação Permite Somente Produtos ' + ;
						    Iif(CrSigCdOpe.ItSituas = 1, 'Ativos', Iif(CrSigCdOpe.ItSituas = 2, 'Inativa', ;
						    'Ambos')) + '.', 48, 'Situação do Produto Incorreta Para a Operação')
						This.Value=''
						this.Refresh
						Thisform.validok=.f.
						return 0
					EndIf
				EndIf
				If Reccount('CrTmpEti') > 0 Or CrSigCdPac.BarIndivs < 2
					Replace crTpmMvItn.etiesps with .t. in crTpmMvItn
				Endif
				cpro=CrTmpPro.cpros
				dpro=CrTmpPro.dpros
				peso=CrTmpPro.pesoms
			Else
				This.Value = ''
				Thisform.validok=.f.
				Return 0
			EndIf
		EndIf
	Else
		cpro=This.Value
		lcQuePro = [Cpros,Dpros,Cunis,Situas,fVendas,fCustos,Pesoms,Moepcs,Moepvs,Pvens,Pcuss,Valors,FigJpgs,CodFinP,]+;
					[Cbars,Moevs,Moecs,Moedas,cGrus,Linhas,Colecoes,Ean13,mUltComps,vUltComps,mercs,cftios,sgrus,custofs ]
		If fVerificarBarras(Cpro,9) And CrSigCdPac.barindivs <> 3
			lcSql = [Select Cpros,Dpros,Cunis,Situas,fVendas,fCustos,Pesoms,Moepcs,Moepvs,Pvens,Pcuss,cftios,sgrus,custofs,]+;
					[Valors,FigJpgs,Cbars,Moevs,Moecs,Moedas,cGrus,Linhas,Colecoes,Ean13,mUltComps,vUltComps,mercs,CodFinP ]+;
					[From SigCdPro Where Cbars = ]+Str(Val(This.Value),14)
				
			If Thisform.Podatamgr.Sqlexecute(lcsql,'CrTmpPro') < 1
				MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
				Return 0
			EndIf

			If Reccount('CrTmpPro') = 0
				lcSql = [Select Cpros,Ean13 From SigCdPro Where Ean13 = ]+Str(Val(Cpro),13)
				If ThisForm.Parentform.PodataMgr.SqlExecute(lcSql,'CrTmpPro') < 1
					MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
					Return 0
				EndIf
			Endif
			Select CrTmpPro
			Go Top
			If Reccount() > 0 And CrSigCdPac.BarIndivs < 2
				replace crTpmMvItn.etiesps with .t. in crTpmMvItn
				cpro = CrTmpPro.Cpros
			EndIf
		Else
			Cpro = Padr(Cpro,14)
		EndIf
		lcSql = [Select Cpros From SigCdPro Where Cpros = ']+cPro+[']
		If Thisform.Podatamgr.Sqlexecute(lcsql,'LocalPro') < 1
			MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
			Return 0
		EndIf
		If Reccount('LocalPro') = 0
			lcProduto = Space(14)
			DO form SigOpBus With .f.,CrSigCdOpe.ItSituas TO lcProduto
			This.Value = lcProduto
			Cpro = lcProduto
			If Empty(This.Value)
				Return 0
			EndIf
		Else
			cPro = LocalPro.Cpros
		Endif
		If Not ThisForm.Parentform.podatamgr.cursorquery('SigCdPro','CrTmpPro','Cpros',cpro,lcQuepro)
			This.Value = ''
			Thisform.validok=.f.
			Return 0
		Endif
		=ThisForm.parentform.podatamgr.cursorquery('SigCdUni','crTmpUni','Cunis',CrTmpPro.Cunis)
		If crTmpUni.etiqs='S'  And CrSigCdOpe.SemEtiqs # 1 And ( CrSigCdOpe.opers = 2 Or ( CrSigCdOpe.Opers = 3 And crTpmMvItn.opers = 'S' ) )
			messagebox('Código Errado - Etiqueta Especial !!',0+48,'')
			This.Value=''
			this.Refresh
			Thisform.validok=.f.
			return 0
		Else
			If ( CrSigCdOpe.ItSituas = 1 And CrTmpPro.Situas = 2 ) Or ;
			   ( CrSigCdOpe.ItSituas = 2 And CrtmpPro.Situas # 2 )
				MessageBox('Situação do Produto ' + AllTrim(CrTmpPro.Cpros) + ': ' + ;
				    Iif(CrTmpPro.Situas = 2,'Inativo','Ativo') + '.' + Chr(13) + ;
				    'Operação Permite Somente Produtos ' + ;
				    Iif(CrSigCdOpe.ItSituas = 1, 'Ativos', Iif(CrSigCdOpe.ItSituas = 2, 'Inativa', ;
				    'Ambos')) + '.', 48, 'Situação do Produto Incorreta Para a Operação')
				This.Value=''
				this.Refresh
				Thisform.validok=.f.
				return 0
			EndIf
		endif
		This.Value = CrTmpPro.CPros
		cpro = CrTmpPro.CPros
		dpro = CrTmpPro.DPros
		cuni = CrTmpPro.CUnis
		peso = CrTmpPro.Pesoms
	Endif
	If ( CrSigCdOpe.LPrecos = 5 )
		If Empty( TprMvCab.LPrecos )
			MessageBox('Lista de Preços Não Informada.', 48, 'Lista de Preços Obrigatória')
			This.Value = ''
			Return 0
		EndIf
		laCampo(1,1)='Cpros'
		laCampo(1,2)= CrTmpPro.Cpros
		laCampo(2,1)='LPrecos'
		laCampo(2,2)= TprMvCab.LPrecos
		If !ThisForm.parentform.podatamgr.ChkRegister('SigCdLpi',@laCampo)
			MessageBox('Produto ' + AllTrim(CrTmpPro.Cpros) + ' Não Pertence a' + Chr(13) + ;
					   'Lista de Preços ' + Proper(AllTrim(TprMvCab.LPrecos)) + '.', 48, ;
					   'Lista de Preços Obrigatória' )
			This.Value = ''
			Return 0
		EndIf
	EndIf
		
	If crSigCdOpe.TabdObrigs = 3

		lcWhere = [Select * From SigOpTdt Where Codigos = ']+TprMvCab.Tabds+[']
		lnErro = ThisForm.Parentform.PodataMgr.SqlExecute(lcWhere,'CrSigOpTdt')
		If lnErro < 1
			MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão (CrSigOpTdt)')
			This.Value = ''
			Return 0
		EndIf
		Select CrSigOpTdt
		Index On Codigos + Str(Tipos,1) + CodTips Tag CodTips

		If InList(CrSigCdOpe.PrecoPs,8,9)
			=fCustoMedio(TprMvCab.Emps,CrTmpPro.Cpros,TprMvCab.Datas,ThisForm.Parentform.PodataMgr,(CrSigCdOpe.PrecoPs=8))
		EndIf
		lcMoevs = CrTmpPro.Moecs
		If InList(CrSigOpCdd.chkIfors,1,2)
			_Ct = Iif(CrSigOpCdd.ChkIfors=1,TprMvCab.ContaOs,TprMvCab.Contads)
			lcQuery = [Select Moevs, Pvens From SigPrFnc Where Cpros = ']+CrTmpPro.Cpros+[' And Ifors = ']+_Ct+[']
			=ThisForm.ParentForm.Podatamgr.Sqlexecute(lcQuery,'CrTmpFpro')
			If Not Eof('CrTmpfPro') And CrTmpfPro.pvens > 0 and Not Empty(CrTmpfPro.Moevs)
				lcMoeVs   = crtmpfPro.MoeVs
			Endif
		Endif

		lcMoedas = Iif( crSigCdOpe.PrecoPs = 1, PadR( CrTmpPro.MoeVs, 10 ), ;
		           Iif( InList(crSigCdOpe.PrecoPs,2,11), PadR( lcMoevs, 10 ), ;
		           Iif( CrSigCdOpe.PrecoPs = 5, Padr( CrTmpPro.Moedas,10 ), ;
		           Iif( CrSigCdOpe.PrecoPs = 7, Padr( CrTmpPro.mUltComps,10),;
		           Iif( CrSigCdOpe.PrecoPs = 8, Padr( TmpCusMed.Moedas,10), Space(10))))))

		Select crSigOpTdt
		Set Order to CodTips
		If Seek( TprMvCab.Tabds + '4' + CrtmpPro.CPros ) Or ;
				Seek( TprMvCab.Tabds + '9' + CrTmpPro.CodFinP ) Or ;
				Seek( TprMvCab.Tabds + '1' + CrTmpPro.CGrus ) Or ;
				Seek( TprMvCab.Tabds + '2' + CrTmpPro.Linhas ) Or ;
				Seek( TprMvCab.Tabds + '3' + CrTmpPro.Colecoes ) Or ;
				Seek( TprMvCab.TabDs + '5' + lcMoedas ) Or ;
				Seek( TprMvCab.TabDs + '6' + CrTmpPro.Cunis ) Or ;
				Seek( TprMvCab.TabDs + '7' + CrTmpPro.Cftios ) Or ;
				Seek( TprMvCab.TabDs + '8' + CrTmpPro.Sgrus )
		Else
			MessageBox('O Produto '+CrTmpPro.Cpros+' não está configurado na Tabela de Desconto ' + AllTrim(TprMvCab.Tabds),0+48,'')
			This.Value = ' '
			Return 0
		EndIf
	EndIf

	Select crTpmMvItn
	Replace crTpmMvItn.cpros with cpro
	This.Value = Cpro
	This.Refresh

	If CrSigCdOpe.Imagems = 1
		lcArquivo3 = Sys(2023)+'\'+Alltrim(Sys(2015))+'.jpg'
		If Not Empty(crTmpPro.FigJpgs) And Not IsNull(crTmpPro.FigJpgs)
			Clear Resources
			lcFoto=Strconv(Strtran(Strtran(Strtran(CrTmpPro.FigJpgs,[data:image/png;base64,],[]),[data:image/jpeg;base64,],[]),[data:image/jpg;base64,],[]),14)
			StrToFile(lcFoto, lcArquivo3)
			With ThisForm.cntitem.cnt_imgProd.figJpg
				.Picture = (lcArquivo3)
				.Visible = .T.
			EndWith
		Else
			Clear Resources
			With ThisForm.cntitem.Cnt_ImgEmp.figJpg
				If Not Isempty(TmpLogo.logos)
					Copy Memo TmpLogo.Logos to (lcArquivo3)
					.Picture = (lcArquivo3)
					.Visible = .T.
				Else
					.Visible = .f.
				Endif
			EndWith
		EndIf
	Endif
	=ThisForm.parentform.podatamgr.cursorquery('SigCdUni','CrTmpUni','Cunis',CrTmpPro.Cunis)
	=Thisform.parentform.podatamgr.Cursorquery('SigCdGpr','CrTmpGgrp','codigos',CrTmpPro.Mercs)

	If CrSigCdOpe.PesoAuts = 1
		Replace crTpmMvItn.Pesos with Peso In crTpmMvItn
	Endif
			
	If CrSigCdOpe.FatAuts = 1
		lnFator = Iif( CrSigCdOpe.Precops = 1, CrTmpPro.FVendas, Iif( CrSigCdOpe.Precops = 2, CrTmpPro.FCustos, 0 ) )
		Replace crTpmMvItn.Fators With lnFator in crTpmMvItn
	Endif
		
	Select crTpmMvItn
	Replace crTpmMvItn.MoeFats With Iif( CrSigCdOpe.Precops = 1, CrTmpPro.Moepvs, Iif( CrSigCdOpe.Precops = 2, CrTmpPro.Moepcs, CrSigCdOpe.CMoes ) )
		
	Thisform.CntItem.cnt_qtd.Get_qtds.Value = Qtd
		
	If Empty( crTpmMvItn.CodBarras )
		Replace crTpmMvItn.Units With 0 in crTpmMvItn
	Endif
			
	If InList(CrSigCdOpe.PrecoAuts,1,3)
		lcSql = [Select Codigos from SigCdLpc Where lPrecos = ']+TprMvCab.lPrecos+[']
		lnErro = ThisForm.parentform.podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcSql,'CrTmplPre')
		If lnErro < 1
			MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
			Return 0
		EndIf
		lcwhere = [Select * From SigCdLpi Where Cpros = ']+CrTmpPro.Cpros+[' And LPrecos = ']+TprMvCab.lPrecos+[']
		lnErro = ThisForm.parentform.podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcWhere,'CrSigCdLpi')
		If lnErro < 1
			MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
			Return 0
		EndIf
		Select CrSigCdLpi
		Go Top
		If Reccount() > 0 And (IsEmpty(crSigCdLpi.VencFs) Or BetWeen(TprMvCab.Datas,crSigCdLpi.VencIs, crSigCdLpi.VencFs))
			Replace crTpmMvItn.univals   with crSigCdLpi.PVens ,;
					crTpmMvItn.lPrecos   With TprMvCab.lPrecos ,;
					crTpmMvItn.lPrecos   with TprMvCab.LPrecos,;
					crTpmMvItn.CodlPrecs With CrTmplPre.Codigos in crTpmMvItn
		Else
			If InList(CrSigCdOpe.PrecoPs,8,9)
				=fCustoMedio(TprMvCab.Emps,CrTmpPro.Cpros,TprMvCab.Datas,ThisForm.Podatamgr,(CrSigCdOpe.PrecoPs=8))
				lcMoeCus = TmpCusMed.Moedas
				lnVlrCus = TmpCusMed.Valors
			EndIf
			lnpCus = CrTmpPro.PCuss
			If InList(CrSigOpCdd.chkIfors,1,2)
				_Ct = Iif(CrSigOpCdd.ChkIfors=1,TprMvCab.ContaOs,TprMvCab.Contads)
				lcQuery = [Select Moevs, Pvens From SigPrFnc Where Cpros = ']+CrTmpPro.Cpros+[' And Ifors = ']+_Ct+[']
				=ThisForm.ParentForm.Podatamgr.Sqlexecute(lcQuery,'CrTmpFpro')
				If Not Eof('CrTmpfPro') And CrTmpfPro.pvens > 0 and Not Empty(CrTmpfPro.Moevs)
					lnpCus = crtmpfPro.PVens
				Endif
			Endif
		
			Replace crTpmMvItn.univals with Iif( CrSigCdOpe.Precops = 1, CrTmpPro.PVens, ;
										Iif( CrSigCdOpe.Precops = 2, lnpCus, ;
										Iif( CrSigCdOpe.Precops = 5, CrTmpPro.Valors, ;
										Iif( CrSigCdOpe.PrecoPs = 7, CrTmpPro.vUltComps,;
										Iif( CrSigCdOpe.PrecoPs = 8, lnVlrCus, Iif( CrSigCdOpe.PrecoPs = 11, crTmpPro.custofs, 0 ) ) ) ) ) ) in crTpmMvItn
		EndIf
	Endif

	Select crTpmMvItn
	Replace crTpmMvItn.dpros with CrTmppro.dpros, ;
			crTpmMvItn.CUnis with CrTmppro.CUnis,;
			crTpmMvItn.Qtds  With Qtd in crTpmMvItn
		
	Select crTpmMvItn
	If Empty( crTpmMvItn.opers )
		Replace crTpmMvItn.opers with Iif( oper=1, 'E', Iif( oper = 2, 'S', crTpmMvItn.opers ) )
	Endif

	Thisform.Calc_ValIte()
	With Thisform.CntItem
		.Cnt_val.Get_Valor.Value  = crTpmMvItn.Units
		.Cnt_valu.Get_Unival.Value = crTpmMvItn.Units
	EndWith

	Thisform.Total_item()
	
	If Not Empty(crTpmMvItn.Cpros)
		With ThisForm.cnt_mensagems
			xTexto1 = PadL(crTpmMvItn.Cpros,14,'0')+' '+crTpmMvItn.Dpros
			xTexto2 = Transform(crTpmMvItn.Qtds,"@Z 999.99")+' '+crTpmMvItn.cunis+' X '+;
					  Transform(crTpmMvItn.Units,"@Z 999,999.99")+' = '+;
					  Transform(crTpmMvItn.Totas,"@Z 999,999.99")
			.mensage1.Caption = xTexto1
			.mensage2.Caption = xTexto2
			.Refresh
		EndWith
	EndIf
	If CrSigCdOpe.CodBars = 1 And CrTmpGgrp.BarRefs <> 1
		Select crTpmMvItn
		Replace EtiEsps With .f.
	EndIf
	
	If ThisForm.lAlteraQtd
		Replace EtiEsps With .t. in crTpmMvItn
	Endif

	*** Mena - 11/09/2013 - monta registro de cor e tamanho para verificar estoque
	SELECT crTpmMvIts
	lcTag = ORDER()
	SET ORDER TO 

	IF snumero			&& verifica se é barra para incluir cor e tamanho
		LOCATE for codbarras = crTmpEti.cbars
		IF not FOUND()
			APPEND BLANK
		endif
		replace codcors with crTmpEti.codcors in crTpmMvIts
		replace codtams with crTmpEti.codtams in crTpmMvIts
		replace qtds with crTpmMvItn.qtds in crTpmMvIts
		replace qtdembs with crTpmMvItn.qtds in crTpmMvIts
		replace aqtdembs with crTpmMvItn.qtds in crTpmMvIts
		replace qtbaixas with crTpmMvItn.qtds in crTpmMvIts
		replace cpros with crTpmMvItn.cpros in crTpmMvIts
		replace citens with crTpmMvItn.citens in crTpmMvIts
		replace codbarras with crTmpEti.cbars in crTpmMvIts
	ENDIF
	*** Mena - 28/05/2013 - Verifica estoque neste momento pois qdo é código de barra, pula o valid da quantidade e assim não avisa falta de estoque ***
	Select crTpmMvItn
	_cIt = crTpmMvItn.Citens
	If !fverest(ThisForm.parentform.podatamgr)
		*** Mena - 14/10/2013 - se não tem estoque suficiente, exclui item para não dar erro de total na impressao do cupom ***	
		Select crTpmMvIts
		Locate For cItens = _cIt
		IF FOUND()
			DELETE
		ENDIF
		ThisForm.lAlteraQtd = .f.
		Select crTpmMvItn
		Locate For cItens = _cIt
		Thisform.mExcluiItem()
		Thisform.mLimpaCntItem( .T. )
		Thisform.Total_item()
		Return 0
 	EndIf
	SELECT crTpmMvIts
	SET ORDER TO TAG (lcTag)
	******************************************************************************

	If crTpmMvItn.EtiEsps And (CrSigCdPac.BarIndivs < 2 Or ThisForm.lAlteraQtd ) And InList( Thisform.Op_escolha, 'INSERIR', 'ALTERAR' ) 
		If Thisform.citem<CrSigCdOpe.qtdites
			If CrSigCdOpe.Opers # 3 And CrSigCdOpe.EFators # 1 And CrSigCdOpe.EPrecos # 1 And ;
					CrSigCdOpe.EPesos # 1 And CrSigCdOpe.chkDVal # 1 And Not InList(CrSigOpCdc.AltEtiqs,3,4)
				Select crTpmMvItn
				_cIt = crTpmMvItn.Citens
				If fverest(ThisForm.parentform.podatamgr)
					Select crTpmMvItn
					Locate For cItens = _cIt
					If Thisform.CntItem.Cnt_val.Get_valor.Value<>0
						If Not Thisform.ItemFiscal()
							If Thisform.Cancelcupom()
								Thisform.mExcluiItem()
								ThisForm.Total_item()
							Else
								Select crTpmMvItn
								Replace ImpCupFis with .t.
								ThisForm.Atuacupom()
								ThisForm.Total_Item(.t.) && PAF - Tiago - 11/03/2011								
								ThisForm.ParentForm.CancelaItem = .T.
								ThisForm.Barra_Botoes.Apaga.Enabled = .T.
							Endif
						Else
							Select crTpmMvItn
							Replace ImpCupFis with .t.
							ThisForm.Atuacupom()
							ThisForm.ParentForm.CancelaItem = .T.
							ThisForm.Barra_Botoes.Apaga.Enabled = .T.
						Endif
					Else
						Messagebox('Valor do Produto Inválido !!',0+48,'')
						Thisform.mExcluiItem()
					Endif
					ThisForm.lAlteraQtd = .f.
					Thisform.mLimpaCntItem( .T. )
					Return 0
				Else
					ThisForm.lAlteraQtd = .f.
					Select crTpmMvItn
					Locate For cItens = _cIt
					Thisform.mExcluiItem()
					Thisform.mLimpaCntItem( .T. )
					Return 0
				Endif
			Endif
		Endif
	Endif
Endif

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE LostFocus
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE LostFocus
Set Confirm Off
If Not Empty(This.Value)
	ThisForm.lalteraqtd = .f.
Endif
If Empty(This.Value) And Lastkey() = 13
	ThisForm.CntItem.proximo.SetFocus
EndIf

ENDPROC

================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGMVITS.CancelaItem.Get_Produto
* Classe: fwget (Base: textbox)
* Registro: 51
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Valid
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Valid
If !Empty(This.Value)

	SNumero = fVerificarBarras( This.Value )
	If snumero
		Select crTpmMvItn
		Set Order to CodBarras
		If ! Seek( Val( This.Value ) ) 
			This.Parent.Ok.Enabled = .f.
			Messagebox( 'Código de Barras não digitado nesta Operação...', 48, '' )
			Set Order to
			Go bott
			Return 0
		EndIf
	Else
		cpro=This.Value
		If fVerificarBarras(cpro,9)
			lcSql = [Select Cpros From SigCdPro Where Ean13 = ]+Str(Val(Cpro),13)
			If ThisForm.Parentform.PodataMgr.SqlExecute(lcSql,'CrTmpPro') < 1
				This.Parent.Ok.Enabled = .f.
				MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
				Return 0
			EndIf
			Select CrTmpPro
			Go Top
			If Reccount() > 0
				cpro = CrTmpPro.Cpros
			Else
				lcSql = [Select Cpros From SigCdPro Where Cbars = ]+Str(Val(Cpro),14)
				If Thisform.ParentForm.Podatamgr.Sqlexecute(lcsql,'CrTmpPro') < 1
					MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
					This.Parent.Ok.Enabled = .f.
					Return 0
				EndIf
				Select CrTmpPro
				Go Top
				If Reccount() > 0
					cpro = CrTmpPro.Cpros
				EndIf
			EndIf
		Else
			Cpro = Padr(Cpro,14)
		Endif
		loLista = CreateObject('fwbuscaext', ThisForm.parentform.poDataMgr.pnIdConn, 'SigCdPro', ;
							   'crListaRemota', 'Cpros', Cpro , 'Seleção', 1000 )
		If Not loLista.plAchouRegistro
			loLista.mAddColuna('Cpros', '', 'Código',.t.,'Cpros')
			loLista.mAddColuna('Dpros', '', 'Descrição',.t.,'Dpros')
			loLista.Show()
		EndIf
		This.Value = CrListaRemota.Cpros
		Select crTpmMvItn
		Set Order To Cpros
		If ! Seek(This.Value)
			This.Parent.Ok.Enabled = .f.
			Messagebox( 'Codigo do Produto não digitado nesta Operação...', 48, '' )
			Set Order to
			Go bott
			Return 0
		Endif
	EndIf
	lcPro = crTpmMvItn.Cpros
	Select CItens,Cpros,Qtds From crTpmMvItn Where Cpros = lcPro And Empty(EmpDopNums) Into Cursor Selecao ReadWrite
	Select Selecao
	Index On Citens tag Citens
	Go Top
	If Reccount() = 0
		This.Parent.Ok.Enabled = .f.
		Messagebox( 'Codigo do Produto não digitado nesta Operação...', 48, '' )
		Select crTpmMvItn
		Set Order to
		Go bott
		Return 0
	Else
		If Reccount() > 1
			loLista = CreateObject( 'fwbuscasel', 'Selecao', 'cItens', 0 , 'Produtos Digitados' )
			loLista.AddColuna( 'Citens', '9999', 'Item', .f. )
			loLista.AddColuna( 'Cpros', '', 'Produto', .f.)
			loLista.AddColuna( 'Qtds', '999.99', 'Qtd',.f.)
			loLista.ShoW
		Endif
	Endif
	Select crTpmMvItn
	Locate For cItens = Selecao.Citens
	If Not Eof()
		This.Parent.Get_Item.Value   = crTpmMvItn.citens
		This.Parent.Get_dpro.Value   = crTpmMvItn.Dpros
		This.Parent.Get_cbarra.Value = crTpmMvItn.codbarras
		This.Parent.Ok.Enabled = .t.
	Endif
EndIf
Return .t.

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE When
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE When
Return (This.Parent.Get_item.Value = 0)
ENDPROC


================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGMVITS.CancelaItem.Get_item
* Classe: textbox (Base: textbox)
* Registro: 55
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Valid
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Valid
With This.Parent
	If This.Value > 0
		If This.Value < ( ThisForm.citem - 101 )
			MessageBox('Cancelamento somente dos ultimos 100 Itens',0+48,'')
			This.Value = 0
			This.Refresh
			Return .f.
		EndIf
		Select crTpmMvItn
		Set Order To Citens
		Locate For Citens = This.Value
		If Eof() Or Empty(crTpmMvItn.Cpros)
			MessageBox('Item não encontrado!!!',0+48,'')
			Return .f.
		Else
			If Not Empty(crTpmMvItn.EmpDopNums)
				MessageBox('Itens do Sub-Nivel não podem ser cancelados!!!',0+48,'')
				This.Value = 0
				This.Refresh
				Return .f.
			Else
				.Ok.Enabled = .t.
				.Get_Produto.Value = crTpmMvItn.Cpros
				.Get_Dpro.Value = crTpmMvItn.dpros
				.Get_cbarra.Value = crTpmMvItn.codbarras
			Endif
		Endif
	Else
		.Ok.Enabled = .f.
		.Get_Produto.Value = Space(14)
		.Get_Dpro.Value = Space(40)
		.Get_cbarra.Value = 0
		.Refresh
	EndIf
EndWith
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE When
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE When
Return .t.

ENDPROC

================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGMVITS.CancelaItem.ok
* Classe: commandbutton (Base: commandbutton)
* Registro: 58
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Click
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Click
Do Form SigOpSen With 'CANCITEM', 'Cancelamento de Item', '' To _Retorno
llOk = ( Left( _Retorno, 1 ) = "*" )
If !llOk
	MessageBox('Cancelamento não autorizado!!!',0+48,'')
	Return .f.
Else
	ItemCancelado = Thisform.ParentForm.Cancel_Item_Gen(This.Parent.Get_item.Value)
	If Not ItemCancelado
		If ThisForm.CancelCupom()
			ItemCancelado = .t.
		EndIf
	Endif
	If ItemCancelado
		Select CQSigExMvi
		Zap
		Select TprMvCab
		Goto Top
		Scatter Memvar
		Select CQSigExMvc
		If Reccount() = 0
			Insert Into cQSigExMvc From Memvar
		EndIf
		Select crTpmMvItn
		Locate For cItens = This.Parent.Get_Item.Value
		Scatter Memvar
		m.CidChaves = fUniqueIds()
		m.EmpDopNums = TprMvCab.Emps + TprMvCab.Dopes + Str(TprMvCab.Numes,6)
		m.lCancelas = .t.
		Insert Into CQSigExMvi From Memvar

		thisform.parentform.totcanc = thisform.parentform.totcanc + crTpmMvItn.totas && PAF - Tiago - 16/03/2011 - Totaliza valor cancelado

		ThisForm.parentform.PodataMgr4.Update('CQSigExMvc')
		ThisForm.parentform.PodataMgr4.Update('CQSigExMvi')
		ThisForm.parentform.PodataMgr4.commit()

		Select crTpmMvItn
		Locate For cItens = This.Parent.Get_Item.Value
		Delete
		Go Bott
		ThisForm.Atuacupom(.f.,This.Parent.Get_Item.Value)
		ThisForm.Total_item()
		Thisform.CancelouItem = .t.
		ThisForm.UltimoCancel = Iif(This.Parent.Get_Item.Value>ThisForm.UltimoCancel,This.Parent.Get_Item.Value,ThisForm.UltimoCancel)
	Endif
EndIf
Select crTpmMvItn
Set Order To 
ThisForm.CntItem.Enabled = .T.
ThisForm.CancelaItem.Visible = .f.
ThisForm.Enabled = .t.
ThisForm.CntItem.Cnt_Prod.Get_Produto.Setfocus
ENDPROC

================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGMVITS.Cnt_Fpagto.Desconto.Get_TotVariação
* Classe: fwget (Base: textbox)
* Registro: 63
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE LostFocus
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE LostFocus
Local _Total,_Varia,_Value

_Total = ThisForm.AtuaTotal() &&TprMvCab.Valinis
_Varia = IIf(_Total=0,0,Round( This.Value / _Total * 100, 4 ))
_Value = IIf(_Total=0,0,-This.Value)

Select TprMvCab
Replace TprMvCab.valvars with _Value
Replace TprMvCab.vars with -_Varia

Select crSigMvNfi
Go top

ThisForm.parentform.pagina.dados.pgfprincipal.pgoperacional.GetValTos.Value = _Total - This.Value 
ThisForm.Cnt_Fpagto.cnt_saldo.get_totliquido.Value = _Total - This.Value 
Thisform.Cnt_Fpagto.cnt_apag.get_saldo.Value = ThisForm.Cnt_Fpagto.cnt_saldo.get_totliquido.Value - ThisForm.Cnt_Fpagto.cnt_pag.get_totforma.value
*!*ThisForm.Cnt_Fpagto.Desconto.Get_PorcVariação.Value = _Varia
ThisForm.Cnt_Fpagto.cnt_saldo.get_totliquido.Refresh
*!*ThisForm.Cnt_Fpagto.Desconto.Get_PorcVariação.Refresh
ThisForm.CalculaDesconto()
Thisform.parentform.Atuatot()
If This.Value = 0
	ThisForm.Cnt_fpagto.desconto.Visible = .f.
Endif
ThisForm.Cnt_fpagto.cnt_forma.get_fpg.SetFocus

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Valid
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Valid
If This.Value < 0
	This.Value = -This.Value
Endif

_Total = ThisForm.Atuatotal() &&TprMvCab.Valinis
_Varia = IIf(_Total=0,0,Round( This.Value / _Total * 100, 4 ))
_Value = IIf(_Total=0,0,-This.Value)

If ABS(_Value) >= _Total And This.Value <> 0
	MessageBox('Variação Invalida!!!',0+48,'')
	Return .f.
Endif

IF CrSigCdOpe.DeAcrs = 1 And ThisForm.Validadesconto And This.Value <> 0 And NOT ThisForm.parentform.Chklimite(_Total,_Total + _Value,.f.,.f.,.f.,ThisForm.Aplicoutabd )
	This.value = 0
	RETURN .f.
Else
	If This.Value = 0 And CrSigCdOpe.DeAcrs = 1 And ThisForm.Validadesconto 
		Replace MotDscs With ' ' in TprMvCab
	Endif
EndIf

If This.Value <> 0
	ThisForm.Validadesconto = .f.
	ThisForm.parentform.validadesconto = .f.
EndIf

Return .t.

ENDPROC

* PROCEDURE When
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE When
RETURN .t.
*** mena - 26/02/2015 - retirado para testes ***
*!*Return (Empty(ThisForm.Cnt_Fpagto.Desconto.Get_PorcVariação.Value))

ENDPROC

================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGMVITS.Cnt_Fpagto.cnt_forma.Get_fpg
* Classe: fwget (Base: textbox)
* Registro: 89
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE LostFocus
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE LostFocus
Thisform.pcnIdent = ''
If Empty(This.Value) And Lastkey() = 13
	If ThisForm.Cnt_Fpagto.cnt_apag.get_saldo.Value <= 0
		ThisForm.Barra_Botoes.Ok.SetFocus
	Else
		ThisForm.Cnt_Fpagto.cnt_forma.get_fpg.Setfocus
	Endif
Endif
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Valid
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Valid
local lcod,lfan,codcor,xobj,nKeyCode

If !Empty( This.Value ) 
	If ( Val(This.Value) > 0 And !Seek(Val(This.Value),'CcSigOpFp','nfpags')) Or Val(This.Value) = 0
		If !Seek( This.Value, 'ccSigOpFp', 'FPags' )
			This.Value = Upper(This.Value)
			loBrowse = CreateObject( 'fwbuscasel', 'ccSigOpFp', 'FPags', This.Value, 'Cadastro de Forma de Pagamento' )
			loBrowse.AddColuna( 'FPags', '', 'Código', .t., 'FPags' )
			loBrowse.AddColuna( 'FParcs', '99', 'Parc' )
			loBrowse.Show
			This.Value = Iif( Lastkey() == 27, '', ccSigOpFp.FPags )
		Endif
	EndIf
	If ccSigOpFp.Trocos = 1 And Not Thisform.Libtroco
		This.Value = ''
		MessageBox('Cond.Pagamento DINHEIRO não foi utilizada. Troco não pode Ser Utilizado!!!',0+48,'')
		Return 0
	EndIf
	If ccSigOpFp.Autos = 1
		This.Value = ''
		MessageBox('Cond.Pagamento '+ccSigOpFp.Fpags+' Somente via Subnivel.!!!',0+48,'')
		Return 0
	EndIf
	If ThisForm.Cnt_fpagto.cnt_apag.get_saldo.Value < 0 And ccSigOpFp.Trocos # 1 And ThisForm.Barra_Botoes.Itens.Enabled 
		This.Value = ''
		MessageBox('Valor das Condições de Pagamento Maior que Saldo!!!',0+48,'')
		Return 0
	EndIf
	If ccSigOpFp.Acumulas = 1
		Select xPar
		Count to lnpar For Not Empty(fpags)
		If lnPar > 0
			MessageBox('Condição '+ccSigOpFp.Fpags+' só pode ser utilizada como única Condição de Pagamento',0+48,'')
			This.Value = ''
			Return 0
		EndIf
	Else
		If ThisForm.LibAcumula
			MessageBox('Condição de Pagamento Acumulada utilizada. Não pode ser utilizada nenhuma outra Condição de Pagamento',0+48,'')
			This.Value = ''
			Return 0
		EndIf
	Endif
	If Not ThisForm.Barra_Botoes.Itens.Enabled And ThisForm.Barra_Botoes.pendencia.Enabled 
		Select TmpOperacao
		Count to lnPen for codigos > 0
		If lnPen = 0
			MessageBox('Nenhuma Pendencia Selecionada!!! ',0+48,'')
			This.Value = ' '
			Return 0
		EndIf
	Endif

	**** Mena 5/2012 verifica limite de cartoes por venda
	Select xPar
	Count to lnpar For infos = 'T' and parcs = 1	&& MENA 27/05/2014 acrescentada o numero da parcela para contar qtde de cartoes e nao qtde de parcelas
	Count to lnche For infos = 'C'
	If lnPar > 3 and ccSigOpFp.infos = 'T'
		MessageBox('Condição '+ccSigOpFp.Fpags+' não pode ultrapassar 3 vezes.',0+48,'')
		This.Value = ''
		Return 0
	EndIf

	This.Value = CcSigOpFp.Fpags
	This.Refresh
	
	=ThisForm.Parentform.Podatamgr.cursorquery('SigOpFp','CrSigOpFp','Fpags',This.Value)
	If !Empty(This.Value)
*!*			If Seek(This.Value,'xPar','fPags') And ( ( crSigOpFp.Infos = [C] And InList(CrSigOpFp.AcVchqs,0,2) ) Or CrSigOpFp.AcVchqs = 3  Or CrSigOpFp.Situas = [S] )
*!*				Messagebox('Forma de Pagamento já Utilizada !!',0+48,'ERRO')
*!*				This.Value = ''
*!*				Return 0
*!*			EndIf

		*** Mena - 19/05/2014 - verifica se condicao de pagto exige CPF ***
		lcCliente = (TprMvCab.Contads)
		=ThisForm.PoDataMgr.CursorQuery('SigCdCli','CrTmpCli','Iclis',lcCliente,[Cpfs,Rclis,paises,Endes,Nums,cidas,Estas,Rgs,Compls])
		If crSigOpFp.chkcpf = 1 And EMPTY(CHRTRAN(CrTmpCli.Cpfs,'.-/',''))
			Messagebox('Forma de Pagamento não pode ser Utilizada. Cliente sem CPF/CNPJ !!',0+48,'ERRO')
			This.Value = ''
			Return 0
		EndIf

		*** Tratamento para não aceitar cartao e cheque juntos - homologação redecard ***
*!*		If (Seek('CARTAO','xPar','fPags') And (crSigOpFp.Infos = [C])) OR (Seek('CHEQUE','xPar','fPags') And ( crSigOpFp.Infos = [T]))
*** Mena - 26/09/2013 - esta linha foi retirada pois nao estava aceitando dois cartoes juntos 
*!*		If (Seek('CARTAO','xPar','fPags') And ( crSigOpFp.Infos = [C])) or (Seek('CARTAO','xPar','fPags') And ( crSigOpFp.Infos = [T])) OR (Seek('CHEQUE REDE','xPar','fPags') And ( crSigOpFp.Infos = [T]) )
&&		If (Seek('CARTAO','xPar','fPags') And ( crSigOpFp.Infos = [C])) OR (Seek('CHEQUE REDE','xPar','fPags') And ( crSigOpFp.Infos = [T]) )
		*** mena 04/08/2015 retirada esta checagem pois não é mais necessária ***
*!*			If (lnPar > 0 And crSigOpFp.Infos = [C]) OR (lnChe > 0 And crSigOpFp.Infos = [T])
*!*				Messagebox('Não permitida esta combinação de condição de Pagamento !!',0+48,'ERRO')
*!*				This.Value = ''
*!*				Return 0
*!*			EndIf

		If InList(CrSigOpFp.Chkbins,1,3) And Not Empty(TprMvCab.MotDscs)
			lcSql = [Select * From SigCdFs Where codigos = ']+TprMvCab.MotDscs+[']
			If (ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcSql, 'LocalMdsc') < 1)
				=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (LocalMdsc)')
				This.Value = ''
				Return 0
			EndIf
			If LocalMdsc.Chkbins = 1
				MessageBox('Condição de Pagamento Bloqueada pelo motivo de Desconto/Acréscimo Informado!!!',0+16,'')
				this.Value = ''
				Return 0
			EndIf
		EndIf
		If CrSigOpFp.BlqTabDs = 1 And ThisForm.AplicouTabd
			MessageBox('Condição de Pagamento Bloqueada pela Tabela de Desconto Aplicada!!!',0+16,'')
			This.Value = ''
			Return 0
		Endif

		If CrSigOpFp.LibcSens = 1
			Messagebox('Condição de Pagamento Configurada para Utilização somente com Autorização!!',0+48,'ERRO')
			Do Form SigOpSen With 'LIBERAFPG','Libera Condicao de Pagamento',CrSigOpFp.Fpags To _Retorno
			llOk = ( Left( _Retorno, 1 ) = "*" )
			If !llOk
				MessageBox('Autorização não Confirmada!!',0+48,'')
				This.Value = ''
				Return 0
			EndIf
			ThisForm.UsuLibs = Substr(_Retorno,2,10)
		EndIf

		*!* 21/11/2016 If ( Empty(crSigOpFp.cCupomnvs) Or CrSigFiFis.Imps = 'S/IMPRESSORA') And crSigOpFp.FParcs > 0
		If ( crSigOpFp.cCupomnvs<>'TEF' Or CrSigFiFis.Imps = 'S/IMPRESSORA') And crSigOpFp.FParcs > 0
			If Empty(crSigCdOpe.Cmoes)
				lnValor = TprMvCab.Valos
			Else
				lnCotOper = ThisForm.ParentForm.Cotacao( crSigCdOpe.Cmoes ) 
				lnCotfpag = ThisForm.ParentForm.Cotacao( crSigOpFp.moefpgs ) 
				lnValor = TprMvCab.Valos * lnCotOper / lnCotFpag
			Endif
			Declare LaCampo(2,2)
			LaCampo(1,1)='Fpags'
			LaCampo(2,1)='nParcs'
			LaCampo(1,2)=CrSigOpFp.Fpags
			LaCampo(2,2)=CrSigOpFp.fParcs
			If ThisForm.Parentform.PoDataMgr.Cursorquery('SigOpFpi','CrSigOpFpi',@LaCampo)
				If CrSigOpFpi.ValMins > lnValor
					Messagebox('Valor Inválido para esta forma de Pagamento !!',0+48,'ERRO')
					This.Value = ''
					Return 0
				Endif
			Else
				If ThisForm.Parentform.podatamgr.cursorquery('SigOpFpi','CrSigOpFpi','Fpags',CrSigOpFp.Fpags)
					Messagebox('Quantidade de Parcelas não Cadastrada!!!',0+48,'ERRO')
					This.Value = ''
					Return 0
				Endif
			EndIf
			If Not Empty(TprMvCab.Tabds) And Thisform.ParentForm.PodataMgr.Cursorquery('SigOpTdz','CrTmpTabd','Codigos',TprMvCab.Tabds) And CrTmpTabd.nParcs > 0
				If CrSigOpFp.FParcs > CrTmpTabd.Nparcs And CrSigOpCdc.BlqParcs # 2
					Messagebox('Quantidade de Parcelas não permitida pela Tabela de Desconto!!!',0+48,'ERRO')
					This.Value = ''
					Return 0
				Endif
			Endif
		Endif
		Select crSigOpFp
		*!* 21/11/2016 If !Empty(crSigOpFp.cCupomnvs) And CrSigFiFis.Imps <> 'S/IMPRESSORA'
		If crSigOpFp.cCupomnvs='TEF' And CrSigFiFis.Imps <> 'S/IMPRESSORA'
			IF Not Empty(crSigCdPam.NumIps)
				ThisForm.Cnt_Fpagto.cnt_forma.Get_npar.Enabled = .t.
			Else
				ThisForm.Cnt_Fpagto.cnt_forma.Get_npar.Enabled = .T.
			Endif
			ThisForm.Cnt_Fpagto.cnt_forma.fweditdata_venc.Enabled = .F.
		Else
			ThisForm.Cnt_Fpagto.cnt_forma.Get_npar.Enabled = .t.
			ThisForm.Cnt_Fpagto.cnt_forma.fweditdata_venc.Enabled = .t.
		Endif
		ThisForm.Cnt_Fpagto.cnt_forma.get_fpg.Value = FPags
		ThisForm.Cnt_Fpagto.cnt_forma.Get_npar.Value = FParcs
		ThisForm.Digpar = Empty( FParcs )
		
		ThisForm.Cnt_Fpagto.Refresh
	Else
		Thisform.Cnt_Fpagto.cnt_forma.Get_npar.Value = 0
		ThisForm.parcDesconto = 0
	Endif
Else
	Thisform.Cnt_Fpagto.cnt_forma.Get_npar.Value = 0
	ThisForm.parcDesconto = 0
	
Endif
Select TprMvCab

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE When
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE When
ThisForm.parcDesconto = 0
ThisForm.DigPar = .f.
ThisForm.Mostraobs(.t.)
ThisForm.UsuLibs = ' '
Return (Not ThisForm.Tempartef)

ENDPROC


================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGMVITS.Cnt_Fpagto.cnt_forma.Get_npar
* Classe: fwget (Base: textbox)
* Registro: 90
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE LostFocus
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE LostFocus
If Empty(This.Value)
	ThisForm.parcDesconto = 0
	ThisForm.Cnt_Fpagto.cnt_forma.get_fpg.Value = ' '
	ThisForm.Cnt_Fpagto.cnt_forma.get_fpg.Refresh
	ThisForm.Cnt_Fpagto.cnt_forma.get_fpg.Setfocus
EndIf

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Valid
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Valid
If !Empty(This.Value)
	If Empty(crSigCdOpe.Cmoes)
		lnValor = TprMvCab.Valos
	Else
		lnCotOper = ThisForm.ParentForm.Cotacao( crSigCdOpe.Cmoes ) 
		lnCotfpag = ThisForm.ParentForm.Cotacao( crSigOpFp.moefpgs ) 
		lnValor = TprMvCab.Valos * lnCotOper / lnCotFpag
	Endif
	Declare LaCampo(2,2)
	LaCampo(1,1)='Fpags'
	LaCampo(2,1)='nParcs'
	LaCampo(1,2)=CrSigOpFp.Fpags
	LaCampo(2,2)=This.Value
	If ThisForm.Parentform.PoDataMgr.Cursorquery('SigOpFpi','CrSigOpFpi',@LaCampo)
		If CrSigOpFpi.ValMins > lnValor
			Messagebox('Valor Inválido para esta forma de Pagamento !!',0+48,'ERRO')
			Return .f.
		Endif
	Else
		If ThisForm.Parentform.podatamgr.cursorquery('SigOpFpi','CrSigOpFpi','Fpags',CrSigOpFp.Fpags) And This.Value # 1
			Messagebox('Quantidade de Parcelas não Cadastrada!!!',0+48,'ERRO')
			Return .f.
		Endif
	Endif
	If Not Empty(TprMvCab.Tabds) And Thisform.ParentForm.PodataMgr.Cursorquery('SigOpTdz','CrTmpTabd','Codigos',TprMvCab.Tabds) And CrTmpTabd.nParcs > 0
		If This.Value > CrTmpTabd.Nparcs And CrSigOpCdc.BlqParcs # 2
			Messagebox('Quantidade de Parcelas não permitida pela Tabela de Desconto!!!',0+48,'ERRO')
			Return 0
		Endif
	Endif
Endif

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE When
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE When
Return !Empty( Thisform.Cnt_Fpagto.cnt_forma.get_fpg.Value) And Thisform.Digpar And (Not ThisForm.Tempartef)

ENDPROC


================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGMVITS.Cnt_Fpagto.cnt_forma.Get_vpar
* Classe: fwget (Base: textbox)
* Registro: 91
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE LostFocus
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE LostFocus
Local llOkTef, lnIdent, lcNSus, ImpFin, RetImpr, lcTipo, lnValor, lcTipoOK, SendTef, lcIdents
Thisform.pcnIdent = ''
ThisForm.Pcbandeira = ''
ThisForm.pcRede = ''
Impfin = .t.
llOkTef = .t.
lnIdent = 0
RetImpr = .t.
lctipo = ''
lnValor = 0
lcTipoOK = '   '
ThisForm.parcDesconto = 0

=fLerFiscal(CrSigOpCdd.NomEcfs)

If Empty(crSigCdOpe.Cmoes)
	lnValor = TprMvCab.Valos
Else
	lnCotOper = ThisForm.ParentForm.Cotacao( crSigCdOpe.Cmoes ) 
	lnCotfpag = ThisForm.ParentForm.Cotacao( crSigOpFp.moefpgs ) 
	lnValor = TprMvCab.Valos * lnCotOper / lnCotFpag
EndIf

If CrSigOpCdd.Varps = 1 And Not ThisForm.Tempartef
	lcSql = [Select * From SigOpFpP Where Fpags = ']+CrSigOpFp.Fpags+[' And nParcs =]+Str(ThisForm.Cnt_Fpagto.cnt_forma.Get_npar.Value,2)
	If Thisform.ParentForm.PodataMgr.Sqlexecute(lcSql,'LocalFpagP') > 0 And ;
			This.Value >= LocalFpagP.ValMins And LocalFpagP.ParcDes > 0
		ThisForm.parcDesconto = LocalfPagP.ParcDes
		lnTotPar  = Round(This.Value / ThisForm.Cnt_Fpagto.cnt_forma.Get_npar.Value,2) * LocalfpagP.ParcDes
		lnValor2  = This.Value - lnTotPar
		This.Value = lnValor2
	Endif
Endif

If !Empty(this.value)
	If This.peantvalue <> This.value 
		This.peantvalue = This.Value
		*!* 21/11/2016 If Not Empty(crSigOpFp.cCupomnvs) And (CrSigFiFis.Imps <> 'S/IMPRESSORA' or INLIST(CrSigCdOpe.CupFis,5,6))	&& mena 14/06/2016 se SAT ou NFCe controla TEF
		If crSigOpFp.cCupomnvs='TEF' And (CrSigFiFis.Imps <> 'S/IMPRESSORA' or INLIST(CrSigCdOpe.CupFis,5,6))	&& mena 14/06/2016 se SAT ou NFCe controla TEF
			ThisForm.Cnt_mensagem.Mensagem.caption = 'Aguarde processamento TEF'

			=ThisForm.parentform.podatamgr.cursorquery('SigFiNfs','crSigFiNfs','cComprovs',CrSigOpFp.cCupomnvs)
			Select crSigFiNfs
			Locate For Imps = CrSigFiFis.Imps
			If Not Eof()
				Select crSigFiNfs
				Do Case
					Case crSigFiNfs.nFinalzs=2
						lcTipo = 'CHQ'
						With Thisform.Cnt_Fpagto
							.cnt_forma.Get_npar.Enabled = .T.
							.cnt_forma.get_vpar.Enabled = .T.
							.cnt_forma.get_fpg.Enabled = .f.
							.cnt_forma.fweditdata_venc.Enabled = .t.
							SendTef = val(right(Thisform.parentform.NoCaixa,2)+SYS(3))
							RetImpr = .t.
							SendTef = VerifGP('ATV', thisform, '', '', CrSigFiFis.Imps, Thisform.ParentForm.poDatamgr, 'TEF' )
							RetCrtChq = TefChq(TprMvCab.Emps,TprMvCab.Dopes,lcTipo,SendTef,Val(TprMvCab.Notas),This.Value,;
							                    "","","","","","","","","","","",ThisForm.Cnt_Fpagto.cnt_forma.Get_npar.Value - ThisForm.parcDesconto )

						EndWith
					Case crSigFiNfs.nFinalzs=3
						lcTipo = 'CRT' 
					Case crSigFiNfs.nFinalzs=4
						lcTipo = 'PRE' 
					OtherWise
						Return .t.
				EndCase
**					If Thisform.parentform.TransTef = 1 And !ThisForm.Tef And crSigCdPam.Sistef = 1
				If Thisform.parentform.TransTef = 0 And crSigCdPam.Sistef = 1
					=ApagarIntpos(ThisForm.Parentform.podatamgr)	&& apaga tudo - mena - 05/2012
				endif
				If Thisform.parentform.TransTef > 0 And crSigCdPam.Sistef = 1
					lcIdents='R'+TprMvCab.Emps+TprMvCab.Dopes+alltrim(str(Thisform.parentform.IdentTef,20))
					ThisForm.Parentform.podatamgr.CursorQuery('SigFiTef','crSigFiTef', 'cIdents', lcIdents)

					**** salva nsu para verificar se crt já confirmado ****
					lcNsu = crSigFiTef.cnNSUs
					lcQuery = [Select * from SigFiTef Where cnnsus = '] + Alltrim(lcNsu) + [' And ]+;
					[cTipos = 'E' and cheaders = 'CNF']
					ThisForm.Parentform.poDatamgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery,'TmpAux3')

					SELECT * from TmpAux3 where ;
					VAL(Strtran(ALLTRIM(cnhrtrans), [ ], [0])) >= VAL(Strtran(ALLTRIM(crSigFiTef.cnhrtrans), [ ], [0])) ;
					order by cemps,cdopes,cnidents desc into cursor tmpAux readwrite

					IF RECCOUNT('tmpaux') = 0		&& envia CNF se ainda não confirmou	
						*** Ativa gp Mena 05/2012 ****
						SendTef = VerifGP('ATV', thisform, '', '', CrSigFiFis.Imps, Thisform.ParentForm.poDatamgr, 'TEF' )
						ThisForm.Parentform.podatamgr.CursorQuery('SigFiTef','crSigFiTef', 'cIdents', lcIdents)
						lcTipoOK = 'CNF'
						RetImpr=TefImpr(TprMvCab.Emps,TprMvCab.Dopes,TprMvCab.Numes,lcTipoOk,;
										val(crSigFiTef.cnIdents),crSigFiTef.cNmRedes,crSigFiTef.cnNSUs,;
										crSigFiTef.cFinalzs,crSigFiTef.cCupomnvs,CrSigFiTef.Fpags)
						ThisForm.Tef = .t.
						=apagaResp(ThisForm.Parentform.podatamgr)	&& apaga intpos.001 - mena - 05/2012
					endif
				EndIf
				If RetImpr And llOkTef
					If lcTipo = 'CHQ'
						Thisform.parentform.IdentTef = SendTef
						Thisform.Cnt_Fpagto.cnt_forma.fweditdata_venc.setfocus
						Return .t.
					Else

						lnIdent = 0
						lcNSus = ''
						lnOrdem = crSigFiNfs.nOrdems
						SendTef = VerifGP('ATV', thisform, '', '', CrSigFiFis.Imps, Thisform.ParentForm.poDatamgr, 'TEF' )
						=ApagaResp(ThisForm.Parentform.podatamgr)	&& apaga intpos.001 anterior
						SendTef = SigFiTefReq(ThisForm.Cnt_Fpagto,Thisform.parentform.NoCaixa,TprMvCab.Emps,TprMvCab.Dopes,TprMvCab.Numes,this.Value,;
											lcTipo,lnOrdem,crSigOpFp.cCupomnvs,.f.,.f.,.f.,.f.,.f.,.f.,.f.,.f.,.f.,.f.,;
											ThisForm.Cnt_Fpagto.cnt_forma.Get_npar.value - ThisForm.parcDesconto ,.f.,.f.,Thisform.Cnt_Fpagto.cnt_forma.get_fpg.Value,;
											.f.,.f.,.f.,ThisForm)
						If Type('SendTef')='N'
							=FazBkpIntpos(ThisForm.Parentform.podatamgr)	&& Faz bkp do confirmado - mena - 05/2012
							lnIdent = SendTef
							lcIdents='R'+TprMvCab.Emps+TprMvCab.Dopes+alltrim(str(lnIdent,20))
							IF ThisForm.Parentform.poDatamgr.CursorQuery('SigFiTef','crSigFiTef', 'cIdents', lcIdents)
								lcNSus = crSigFiTef.cnNSUs
							Else
								Messagebox('Erro Resposta Tef !!',0+48,'ERRO')
								llOkTef = .f.
		 					Endif
							lcIdents='R'+TprMvCab.Emps+TprMvCab.Dopes+alltrim(str(lnIdent,20))
							ThisForm.Parentform.poDatamgr.CursorQuery('SigFiTef','crSigFiTef', 'cIdents', lcIdents)
							lcTipoOK = 'CNF'
							If Val(crSigFiTef.cQtdParcs) > 0 And Val(crSigFiTef.CtpParcs) = 0
								Declare LaCampo(2,2)
								LaCampo(1,1)='Fpags'
								LaCampo(2,1)='nParcs'
								LaCampo(1,2)=CrSigOpFp.Fpags
								LaCampo(2,2)=Val(crSigFiTef.cQtdParcs)
								If ThisForm.Parentform.PoDataMgr.Cursorquery('SigOpFpi','CrSigOpFpi',@LaCampo)
									If CrSigOpFpi.ValMins > lnValor
										Messagebox('Valor Inválido para esta forma de Pagamento !!',0+48,'ERRO')
										llOkTef = .f.
										lcTipoOK = 'NCN'
									Endif
								Else
									If ThisForm.Parentform.podatamgr.cursorquery('SigOpFpi','CrSigOpFpi','Fpags',CrSigOpFp.Fpags) And ThisForm.Cnt_Fpagto.cnt_forma.Get_npar.Value # 1
										Messagebox('Quantidade de Parcelas não Cadastrada (2)!!!',0+48,'ERRO')
										llOkTef = .f.
										lcTipoOK = 'NCN'
									Endif
								Endif
							Endif
*!*								If ( Thisform.parentform.TransTef > 0 And crSigCdPam.Sistef = 1) or lcTipo = 'CHQ' or lctipoOk='NCN'
*!*								SendTef = VerifGP('ATV', thisform, '', '', CrSigFiFis.Imps, Thisform.ParentForm.poDatamgr, 'TEF' )
*!*								RetImpr=TefImpr(TprMvCab.Emps,TprMvCab.Dopes,TprMvCab.Numes,;
*!*													lcTipoOk,val(crSigFiTef.cnIdents),crSigFiTef.cNmRedes,;
*!*													crSigFiTef.cnNSUs,crSigFiTef.cFinalzs,crSigFiTef.cCupomnvs,CrSigFiTef.Fpags, Val(crSigFiTef.cnValors) / 100 )
*!*									If !RetImpr
*!*										Messagebox('Problemas Comunicação com Tef !!',0+48,'')
*!*									Endif
*!*								Endif
							If ( lcTipo = 'CRT' or lcTipo = 'PRE' ) And llOkTef
								Thisform.parentform.TransTef = Thisform.parentform.TransTef + 1
							Endif
							Thisform.parentform.IdentTef = lnIdent
						Else
							llOkTef = .f.
						EndIf
					Endif
				Else
					llOkTef = .f.
				Endif
			Endif
			ThisForm.Cnt_mensagem.Mensagem.caption = ''
		Endif
		If !ImpFin or !llOkTef
			If !Impfin
				Messagebox('Erro na Impressão !!',0+48,'')
			Endif
			This.Value = 0
			With Thisform.Cnt_Fpagto
				.cnt_forma.Get_npar.Enabled = .t.
				.cnt_forma.get_vpar.Enabled = .t.
				.cnt_forma.get_fpg.Enabled = .t.
				.cnt_forma.get_fpg.Value = ''
				.cnt_forma.Get_npar.Value = 0
				.cnt_forma.fweditdata_venc.Value = {}
				.refresh
			EndWith
			ThisForm.Cnt_Fpagto.cnt_forma.get_fpg.Setfocus
		Else
			Thisform.pcnIdent = alltrim(str(lnIdent,20)) 
			With Thisform.Cnt_Fpagto
				.cnt_forma.Get_npar.Enabled = .f.
				.cnt_forma.get_vpar.Enabled = .f.
				.cnt_forma.get_fpg.Enabled = .f.
				If lctipo = 'CRT' or lctipo = 'PRE' Or CrSigOpFp.tVens = 'S'
					***Mena - 18/01/2014 - salva o desconto dado pela operadora **
					.desconto.get_TotVariação.Value = IIF(VAL(crSigfiTef.cnDesco) > 0, VAL(crSigfiTef.cnDesco)/100, .desconto.get_TotVariação.Value)
					.cnt_forma.get_vpar.Value = VAL(crSigfiTef.cnValors)/100
					.desconto.get_TotVariação.LostFocus()		&& atualiza valvars (xeest)
					**************************************************************
					.cnt_forma.fweditdata_venc.Value = 	Dtot(Ttod(TprMvCab.Datas) + crSigOpFp.Entradas)
					.cnt_forma.fweditdata_venc.Enabled = .f.
					.cnt_forma.fweditdata_venc.Valid()

*!*					=AjustaFp()		&& mena 28/07/2016 - ajusta fp conforme retorno do tef (SIGPRSAT)
					IF !AjustaFp()	&& mena - 05/10/2016 - verifica se existe forma de pagamento configurada para dados retornados do TEF
						thisform.barra_Botoes.cancpagto.Click(.T.)  
						RETURN .f.
					ENDIF
						
				Else
					.cnt_forma.fweditdata_venc.Enabled = .t.
				Endif
*!*	2014		If (.get_totliquido.value = .get_totforma.value) and .get_totliquido.value # 0 and .get_totforma.value # 0 And CrSigOpFp.tVens # 'S'
				If (.cnt_saldo.get_totliquido.value <= .cnt_pag.get_totforma.value) and .cnt_saldo.get_totliquido.value # 0 and .cnt_pag.get_totforma.value # 0 And CrSigOpFp.tVens # 'S'
					ThisForm.Barra_Botoes.Ok.Enabled = .t.
					ThisForm.Barra_Botoes.Cancela.Enabled = .t.
					*** mena 12/07/2016 - retirado para não finaliza imediatamwente e permitir cancelar formas de pagamentos erradas - RJ*!*Thisform.Barra_Botoes.Ok.Click
					*** Mena - 03/12/2018 - recolocado para finalizar pagamento imediatamente
					Thisform.Barra_Botoes.Ok.Click
					&& Mena 03/12/2018 Thisform.Cnt_Fpagto.proximo.SetFocus 
				Else
					Thisform.Cnt_Fpagto.cnt_forma.get_fpg.setfocus
				Endif
			EndWith
		Endif
	Endif
Endif
Select xPar
Return (llOkTef)
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Valid
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Valid
Local valtot,nparc,anttot,ndia,tven,total,valpar,ncarne,x,dcart,lcFpag,lnP,lnValP
Local lnTroco,lcTipo,lnOrdem

If Lastkey() = 5
	Return .f.
EndIf
lcFpag = ThisForm.Cnt_fpagto.cnt_forma.get_fpg.value
lnP    = ThisForm.Cnt_Fpagto.cnt_forma.Get_npar.Value

=ThisForm.Parentform.Podatamgr.cursorquery('SigOpFp','CrSigOpFp','Fpags',ThisForm.Cnt_fpagto.cnt_forma.get_fpg.value)
=ThisForm.Parentform.Podatamgr.cursorquery('SigPrInf','CrSigPrInf','infos',crSigOpFp.infos)

oFormulario = Thisform.Parentform.oModemEest

CotOper = ThisForm.ParentForm.Cotacao( crSigCdOpe.Cmoes ) 
Cotacao = ThisForm.ParentForm.Cotacao( crSigOpFp.moefpgs ) 

If ( Empty( This.Value ) Or CrSigOpFp.Trocos = 1 ) And Not Empty(Thisform.Cnt_Fpagto.cnt_forma.get_fpg.value)
	If crSigOpFp.Trocos = 1
		lnTroco = Round( ( TprMvCab.valos - ThisForm.Cnt_Fpagto.cnt_pag.get_totforma.Value ) * CotOper / Cotacao, 2 ) * (-1)
		If lnTroco < 0
			This.Value = 0
			Return .t.
		Else
			This.Value = lnTroco
		EndIf
	Else
		lnValor = Round( ( TprMvCab.valos - ThisForm.Cnt_Fpagto.cnt_pag.get_totforma.Value ) * CotOper / Cotacao, 2 )
		This.Value = iif(lnValor < 0 ,0,lnValor)
	EndIf
	If CrSigOpCdd.VarPs = 1 And Not ThisForm.Tempartef 
		If ThisForm.Bcds > ThisForm.ItSemBonus
			lnBcd = 0
		Else
			lnBcd = ThisForm.ItSemBonus - ThisForm.Bcds
		EndIf
		If This.Value < lnBcd
			lnBcd = This.Value
		Endif
		lnValp = lnBcd
		Select * From ItemcFpagp Where Fpags = lcFpag And nParcs = lnP And lnValP >= ValMins And lnValP <= ValMaxs ;
		Into Cursor LocalFpagP
		If Reccount('LocalFpagP') > 0 And LocalFpagP.Descs > 0
			ThisForm.Cnt_Fpagto.Get_pVaria.Value = LocalFpagP.Descs 
			ThisForm.Cnt_Fpagto.Get_pVaria.Refresh
			lnDesc = Round(lnBcd * ThisForm.Cnt_Fpagto.Get_pVaria.Value / 100,2)
			This.Value = This.Value - lnDesc
		Endif
	EndIf
Else
	If Not Empty(ThisForm.Cnt_Fpagto.cnt_forma.get_fpg.Value) And This.peantvalue <> This.value 
		If CrSigOpCdd.VarPs = 1 And Not ThisForm.Tempartef
			lnValor = Round( ( TprMvCab.valos - ThisForm.Cnt_Fpagto.cnt_pag.get_totforma.Value ) * CotOper / Cotacao, 2 )
			lnvPar  = Round(This.Value * Cotacao / CotOper,2)
			lnDesc  = 0

			If ThisForm.Bcds > ThisForm.ItSemBonus
				lnBcd = 0
			Else
				lnBcd = ThisForm.ItSemBonus - ThisForm.Bcds
			EndIf
*!*				If ( CrSigOpFp.Fpags = 'DINHEIRO' Or CrSigOpFp.Infos = [M] ) And lnVpar > lnValor
			*** Mena - 30/03/2013 - procura a palavra DINHEIRO dentro da FP
			If ( 'DINHEIRO'$CrSigOpFp.Fpags Or CrSigOpFp.Infos = [M] ) And lnVpar > lnValor
				If lnValor < lnBcd
					lnBcd = lnValor
				Endif
				lnValp = lnBcd
				Select * From ItemcFpagp Where Fpags = lcFpag And nParcs = lnP And lnValP >= ValMins And lnValP <= ValMaxs ;
				Into Cursor LocalFpagP
				If Reccount('LocalFpagP') > 0 And LocalFpagP.Descs > 0
					ThisForm.Cnt_Fpagto.Get_pVaria.Value = LocalFpagP.Descs 
					ThisForm.Cnt_Fpagto.Get_pVaria.Refresh
					lnDesc = Round(lnBcd * ThisForm.Cnt_Fpagto.Get_pVaria.Value / 100,2)
				Endif
			Else
				If This.Value < lnBcd
					lnBcd = This.Value
				Endif
				lnValp = lnBcd
				Select * From ItemcFpagp Where Fpags = lcFpag And nParcs = lnP And lnValP >= ValMins And lnValP <= ValMaxs ;
				Into Cursor LocalFpagP
				If Reccount('LocalFpagP') > 0 And LocalFpagP.Descs > 0
					ThisForm.Cnt_Fpagto.Get_pVaria.Value = LocalFpagP.Descs 
					ThisForm.Cnt_Fpagto.Get_pVaria.Refresh
					lnDesc = Round(lnBcd * ThisForm.Cnt_Fpagto.Get_pVaria.Value / 100,2)
					This.Value = This.Value - lnDesc
				Endif
			Endif
		ENDIF
		If !(crSigCdOpe.DigItens = 2 And crSigCdOpe.Valpres = 2)	
		**** mena - homologação - dinheiro não aceita troco - 05/2012 ****
**			If  CrSigFiFis.Imps <> 'S/IMPRESSORA'
*!* 21/11/2016			If  Not Empty(crSigOpFp.cCupomnvs) And (CrSigFiFis.Imps <> 'S/IMPRESSORA' or INLIST(CrSigCdOpe.CupFis,5,6))	&& mena 14/06/2016 se SAT ou NFCe controla TEF
			If  crSigOpFp.cCupomnvs='TEF' And (CrSigFiFis.Imps <> 'S/IMPRESSORA' or INLIST(CrSigCdOpe.CupFis,5,6))	&& mena 14/06/2016 se SAT ou NFCe controla TEF
				_Saldo  = (Thisform.Cnt_Fpagto.cnt_saldo.get_totliquido.value - Thisform.Cnt_Fpagto.cnt_pag.get_totforma.Value)	&& homologação paf 09/2012 - dinheiro aceita troco
				lnValCp = This.Value
				If Round(lnValCp * CotOper / Cotacao,2) > _Saldo
					ThisForm.Cnt_mensagem.Mensagem.caption = 'f:'+crSigOpFp.fpags+' v:'+Str(lnValCp,12,2)+' s:'+Str(_saldo,12,2)
					Messagebox('Valor Forma Pagto Inválido (1) !!',0+48,'ERRO')
					This.Value = 0
					ThisForm.Cnt_mensagem.Mensagem.caption = ''
					Return .f.
				Endif		
			Endif
		Endif		
		If (Thisform.Cnt_Fpagto.cnt_saldo.get_totliquido.value = Thisform.Cnt_Fpagto.cnt_pag.get_totforma.value) and Thisform.Cnt_Fpagto.cnt_saldo.get_totliquido.value # 0 and Thisform.Cnt_Fpagto.cnt_pag.get_totforma.value # 0
			Messagebox('Valor da Cond. Pagto Inválido (2)!!',0+48,'ERRO')
			This.Value = 0
			Return .f.
		ENDIF
		*** homologação paf 10/2012 - não deixa passar valor em dinheiro >= total quando já existe outra forma de pagamento - dá erro na impressora ***
		If (this.value >= Thisform.Cnt_Fpagto.cnt_saldo.get_totliquido.value) and Thisform.Cnt_Fpagto.cnt_saldo.get_totliquido.value # 0 and Thisform.Cnt_Fpagto.cnt_pag.get_totforma.value # 0
			Messagebox('Valor da Cond. Pagto Inválido (2)!!',0+48,'ERRO')
			This.Value = 0
			Return .f.
		Endif
		*** mena - 13/07/2016 - so deixa valor maior que saldo se for dinheiro ***
		If (this.value > Thisform.Cnt_Fpagto.cnt_saldo.get_totliquido.value) and !'DINHEIRO'$UPPER(crSigPrInf.descrs)
			Messagebox('Valor da Cond. Pagto Inválido (3)!!',0+48,'ERRO')
			This.Value = 0
			Return .f.
		Endif
	Endif
EndIf

If Not Empty(This.Value)
	If This.Value < 0
		Messagebox('o Valor não pode ser Negativo !!!',0+48,'ERRO')
		This.Value = 0
		Return .f.
	EndIf

	If Empty(crSigCdOpe.Cmoes)
		lnValor = TprMvCab.Valos
	Else
		lnCotOper = ThisForm.ParentForm.Cotacao( crSigCdOpe.Cmoes ) 
		lnCotfpag = ThisForm.ParentForm.Cotacao( crSigOpFp.moefpgs ) 
		lnValor = TprMvCab.Valos * lnCotFpag / lnCotOper &&lnCotOper / lnCotFpag
	EndIf

	If CrSigOpFp.Situas = [S]

		lcGrupo = CrSigOpFp.GrupoDs
		lcConta = Iif(Empty(CrSigOpFp.ContaDs),Iif(CrSigOpFp.OriDes=1,TprMvCab.ContaOs,TprMvCab.ContaDs),CrSigOpFp.ContaDs)
		GruConMoe = lcGrupo + lcConta + CrSigOpFp.MoeFpgs

		lcSql = [Select Saldos From SigMvSlc Where GruConMoes = ']+GruConMoe+[']
		If Thisform.ParentForm.PodataMgr.Sqlexecute(lcSql,'CrSigMvSlc') < 0 
			=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (CrSigMvSlc)')
			Return .f.
		EndIf
		_Dispon = ( CrSigMvSlc.Saldos * -1 )
		
		If This.Value > _Dispon
			MessageBox('Saldo Conta Corrente INSUFICIENTE!!!'+Chr(13)+;
						'Disponivel : '+CrSigOpFp.MoeFpgs+' '+Alltrim(Str(_Dispon,12,2)),0+16,GruConMoe)
			This.Value = 0
			If _Dispon > 0
				Return .f.
			Else
				This.Parent.cnt_forma.get_fpg.Value = ' '
				Return .t.
			Endif
		Endif
	Endif

	Declare LaCampo(2,2)
	LaCampo(1,1)='Fpags'
	LaCampo(2,1)='nParcs'
	LaCampo(1,2)=CrSigOpFp.Fpags
	LaCampo(2,2)=ThisForm.Cnt_Fpagto.cnt_forma.Get_npar.Value
	If ThisForm.Parentform.PoDataMgr.Cursorquery('SigOpFpi','CrSigOpFpi',@LaCampo)
		If CrSigOpFpi.ValMins > lnValor
			Messagebox('Valor Inválido para esta forma de Pagamento !!',0+48,'ERRO')
			Return .f.
		Endif
	Else
		If ThisForm.Parentform.podatamgr.cursorquery('SigOpFpi','CrSigOpFpi','Fpags',CrSigOpFp.Fpags) And ThisForm.Cnt_Fpagto.cnt_forma.Get_npar.Value > 0 &&# 1
			Messagebox('Quantidade de Parcelas não Cadastrada (1)!!!',0+48,'ERRO')
			Return .f.
		Endif
	Endif
EndIf
Return .t.
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE When
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE When
This.peantvalue = This.value
Return !Empty( Thisform.Cnt_Fpagto.cnt_forma.get_fpg.Value)
ENDPROC


================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGMVITS.Cnt_Fpagto.cnt_forma.fweditdata_venc
* Classe: fweditdata (Base: textbox)
* Registro: 92
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE LostFocus
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE LostFocus
If ThisForm.Cnt_Fpagto.cntnsu.Visible
	Thisform.Cnt_Fpagto.cntnsu.get_Nsu.SetFocus
	RETURN 	&& retorna para não mudar o foco
ENDIF
	 
If crSigOpFp.EmiVales = 3
	Thisform.Cnt_Fpagto.Get_Obs.SetFocus
Else
	*** mena - 26/02/2015 - retirado para testes ***
	If ThisForm.Cnt_Fpagto.Cnt_ChequepreTef.Visible
		SELECT xpar
		lcFPag = FPags
		lnChv  = nChaves
		DO WHILE lcFpag = FPags And nChaves = lnChv AND ! BOF()
			SKIP -1
		ENDdo
		IF !BOF()
			SKIP
		Endif
		ThisForm.Cnt_Fpagto.Cnt_ChequepreTef.Get_Cpf.SetFocus
		return		&& mena - 27/01/2014 - retorna para não executar o click abaixo quando fp total
	EndIf 
EndIf
If (Thisform.Cnt_Fpagto.cnt_saldo.get_totliquido.value = Thisform.Cnt_Fpagto.cnt_pag.get_totforma.value) and Thisform.Cnt_Fpagto.cnt_saldo.get_totliquido.value # 0 and Thisform.Cnt_Fpagto.cnt_pag.get_totforma.value # 0
	ThisForm.Barra_Botoes.Ok.Enabled = .t.
	ThisForm.Barra_Botoes.Cancela.Enabled = .t.
	*** mena 12/07/2016 - retirado para não finaliza imediatamwente e permitir cancelar formas de pagamentos erradas - RJ*!*Thisform.Barra_Botoes.Ok.Click
endif

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Valid
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Valid
Local valtot,nparc,anttot,ndia,tven,total,valpar,ncarne,x,dcart, lnParcs, lnParTef

If !Empty(ThisForm.Cnt_Fpagto.cnt_forma.get_fpg.Value) And ThisForm.Cnt_Fpagto.cnt_forma.get_vpar.Value <> 0

	If (fVerificaVencimento(This.Value, ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr) <> 0)
		Messagebox( 'Vencimento Bloqueado !!!',0+48,'')
		This.Value = Date()
		Return .f.
	Endif

	=ThisForm.Parentform.Podatamgr.cursorquery('SigOpFp','CrSigOpFp','Fpags',ThisForm.Cnt_Fpagto.cnt_forma.get_fpg.Value)

	lnParTef = 0
	ndia = crSigOpFp.dias
	tven = crSigOpFp.tvens
	moefpg = crSigOpFp.moefpgs
	dcart = crSigOpFp.dcarts
	cartao = 0
	nocredito = 0

	CotOper = ThisForm.ParentForm.Cotacao( crSigCdOpe.Cmoes ) 
	cotacao = ThisForm.ParentForm.Cotacao( crSigOpFp.moefpgs ) 

*!* 21/11/2016	If !Empty(crSigOpFp.cCupomnvs) And (CrSigFiFis.Imps <> 'S/IMPRESSORA' or INLIST(CrSigCdOpe.CupFis,5,6))	&& mena 14/06/2016 se SAT ou NFCe controla TEF
	If crSigOpFp.cCupomnvs='TEF' And (CrSigFiFis.Imps <> 'S/IMPRESSORA' or INLIST(CrSigCdOpe.CupFis,5,6))	&& mena 14/06/2016 se SAT ou NFCe controla TEF
		lnParTef = 1
		*** mena - 15/08/2016 - sai para não dar erro de programa ***
		IF VARTYPE(Thisform.parentform.IdentTef) = 'L'
			Messagebox( 'TEF não está configurado corretamente.!!!',0+48,'')
			Return .T.
		ENDIF
		lcIdents='R'+TprMvCab.Emps+TprMvCab.Dopes+alltrim(str(Thisform.parentform.IdentTef,20))
		ThisForm.Parentform.poDatamgr.CursorQuery('SigFiTef','crSigFiTef', 'cIdents', lcIdents)
		If val(crSigFiTef.cqtdparcs)<> 0 And Val(crSigFiTef.CtpParcs) = 0
			Thisform.Cnt_Fpagto.cnt_forma.Get_npar.value =  val(crSigFiTef.cqtdparcs)
		Else
			Thisform.Cnt_Fpagto.cnt_forma.Get_npar.value = 1
		Endif
		If Used('CrTefParc')
			Select CrTefParc
			If Reccount('CrTefParc')<>0
				lnParTef = 2
			Endif
		EndIf
	Else
		ThisForm.Cnt_Fpagto.cnt_forma.Get_npar.Value = ThisForm.Cnt_Fpagto.cnt_forma.Get_npar.Value - ThisForm.parcDesconto
	Endif

	nparc 	= ThisForm.Cnt_Fpagto.cnt_forma.Get_npar.Value
	nParctt = ThisForm.Cnt_Fpagto.cnt_forma.Get_npar.Value
	total 	= ThisForm.Cnt_Fpagto.cnt_pag.get_totforma.Value
	valpar 	= Round( ( ThisForm.Cnt_Fpagto.cnt_forma.get_vpar.Value ) / nparc, 2 )
	Valdsc  = 0
	pDsc    = 0
	totpar	= 0
	totdsc  = 0
	ncarne 	= 0
	nump 	= 1
	dvenc  = (This.Value)
	dvenci = (This.Value)
	If Type('dvenc') = "T"
		dvenc  = Ttod(This.Value)
		dvenci = Ttod(This.Value)
	EndIf
	_parcelas = 1

	lnNewChv = ParcTef.nchaves
	If Not ThisForm.Tempartef
		Thisform.Newchv = Thisform.Newchv + 1
		lnNewChv = ThisForm.NewChv
	EndIf
	
	If ThisForm.pcBandeira = '00000'
		If InList(ThisForm.pcRede,'00004','00125')
			ThisForm.pcBandeira = '00001'
		Endif
	Endif

	lcnNsuParc = ''
	lnParcs = 1
	x=1
	lcdtvenc = ''
	Select xpar
	Count to lntpar for !Empty(fpags)
	llGrava = ValPar # 0 Or ( ValPar = 0 And lntpar = 0 ) Or crSigOpFp.Trocos = 1
	do while nparc > 0 And llGrava
		If lnParTef = 2
			Select CrTefParc
			Set Order to parc
			lcdtvenc = '019-'+transform(lnParcs,'@L 999')
			lcvalparc = '020-'+transform(lnParcs,'@L 999')
			lcnnsu = '021-'+transform(lnParcs,'@L 999')
			If seek(lcdtvenc)
				dvenctef = alltrim(Substr(CrTefParc.cinfos,11,2)+'/'+Substr(CrTefParc.cinfos,13,2)+'/'+Substr(CrTefParc.cinfos,15,4))
				dvenc = ctod(dvenctef)
			Else
				lcdtvenc = ''
			Endif
			If seek(lcvalparc) 
				valpar = val(alltrim(Substr(CrTefParc.cinfos,11)))/100
			Endif
			If seek(lcnnsu)
				lcnNsuParc = alltrim(Substr(CrTefParc.cinfos,11))
			Else
				lcnNsuParc = crSigFiTef.cnnsus
			Endif
		Else
			If lnParTef = 1
				lcnNsuParc = crSigFiTef.cnnsus
			EndIf
		Endif

		Select xPar
		append blank
		replace Parcs   With lnParcs, ;
				emps    with TprMvCab.emps, ;
			 	dopes   with TprMvCab.dopes, ;
			 	numes   with TprMvCab.numes, ;
			 	datas   with TprMvCab.Datas, ;
			 	valos   with valpar, ;
			 	moefpgs with moefpg, ;
			 	vencs   with dvenc, ;
			 	fpags   with Thisform.Cnt_Fpagto.cnt_forma.get_fpg.value, ;
			 	dcarts  with dcart, ;
			 	cotfpgs with cotacao, ;
			 	cotusus with Thisform.cotusu ;
			 	cnIdTefs with Thisform.pcnIdent ;
			 	cnNsuParcs with lcnNsuParc;
			 	Trocos  With crSigOpFp.Trocos ;
			 	nChaves With lnNewChv ;
			 	Infos   With CrSigOpFp.Infos ;
			 	Acumulas With CrSigOpFp.Acumulas ;
			 	ValDescs With ValDsc ;
			 	pDescs   With pDsc   ;
			 	tparcs   With nparctt ;
			 	Bcds     With Iif(Valdsc<>0,ValPar + ValDsc,0),;
			 	CodBcrts With ThisForm.pcbandeira

		totpar = totpar + valpar
		TotDsc = TotDsc + ValDsc

*!*			If xPar.Fpags = 'DINHEIRO' Or xPar.Infos = [M]
		If 'DINHEIRO'$xPar.Fpags Or xPar.Infos = [M]	&& Mena - 30/03/2013 - procura a palavra dinheiro dentro da FP
			ThisForm.Libtroco = .t.
		Endif
		If xPar.Acumulas = 1
			ThisForm.Libacumula = .t.
		Endif

		If Nparc = Nump
			If totpar # ThisForm.Cnt_Fpagto.cnt_forma.get_vpar.Value 
				_dif = ThisForm.Cnt_Fpagto.cnt_forma.get_vpar.Value - totpar
				Replace xPar.Valos with xPar.Valos + _dif
			Endif
		Endif

		Thisform.mostraobs()

	 	if fpags='CARNE'
	 		if ncarne=0
				ncarne=fGerUniqueKey('CARNE')
	 			Select xpar
	 		endif
	 		replace ncarnes with trans(ncarne,'@L 999999')+'/'+str(x,1)
*!*		 		Thisform.Cnt_Fpagto.get_carne.refresh	&& mena 31/05/2013 - retirado pois nao existe este campo
	 		x=x+1
	 	endif
	 	if crSigOpFp.infos='T' And crSigOpFp.CarAuts='S'
	 		if x=1
		 		do form SigTfCrd with Thisform,ThisForm.Cnt_fpagto
		 		replace cartaos with Thisform.Cnt_Fpagto.get_cartao.value
		 		replace nocreditos with Thisform.Cnt_Fpagto.get_cred.value
		 		cartao=Thisform.Cnt_Fpagto.get_cartao.value
		 		nocredito=Thisform.Cnt_Fpagto.get_cred.value
		 		Thisform.Cnt_Fpagto.get_cartao.refresh
		 		Thisform.Cnt_Fpagto.get_cred.refresh
		 		x=x+1
		 	else
		 		replace cartaos with cartao
		 		replace nocreditos with nocredito
		 		Thisform.Cnt_Fpagto.get_cartao.refresh
		 		Thisform.Cnt_Fpagto.get_cred.refresh
		 		x=x+1
		 	endif
		 endif
		 If empty(lcdtvenc)
			if tven='F'
				dvenc = gomonth( dvenci, _parcelas )
				_parcelas = _parcelas + 1
			else
				dvenc = dvenc + ndia
			endif		
		Endif
		nparc = nparc - 1
		lnParcs = lnParcs + 1
		total = round( total + ( round( xpar.valos * xpar.cotfpgs / cotoper, 2 ) * Iif( crSigOpFp.Trocos = 1, -1, 1 ) ), 2 )
	EndDo 

	If Not Empty(ThisForm.UsuLibs)
		Update xPar Set NoCreditos = ThisForm.Usulibs Where Fpags = CrSigOpFp.Fpags
		ThisForm.UsuLibs = ' '
	Endif

	Select crSigMvNfi
	Go top

	Select xpar
	Go bottom
	With Thisform.Cnt_Fpagto
		If !Empty( .cnt_forma.get_fpg.Value )
			ThisForm.CalculaDesconto()
			_Enc = 0
			lnvTot = ThisForm.AtuaTotal() + .Acrescimo.Get_TotVariação.Value - .Desconto.Get_TotVariação.Value - .cnt_descp.get_tvariacao.Value
			Thisform.Parentform.pagina.dados.pgfprincipal.pgoperacional.GetValtos.value = lnvTot
			.cnt_saldo.get_totliquido.Value = lnvTot
		Endif
		.cnt_pag.get_totforma.Value = total
		.cnt_apag.get_saldo.Value = .cnt_saldo.get_totliquido.Value - .cnt_pag.get_totforma.value
		.cnt_forma.Get_npar.Enabled = .t.
		.cnt_forma.get_vpar.Enabled = .t.
		.cnt_forma.get_fpg.Enabled = .t.
		.cnt_forma.get_fpg.Value = ''
		.cnt_forma.Get_npar.Value = 0
		.cnt_forma.get_vpar.value = 0
		.cnt_forma.fweditdata_venc.Value = {}
	Endwith
	ThisForm.Atuacupom(.t.)
	ThisForm.Cnt_Fpagto.Refresh
EndIf
Return .t.
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE When
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE When
ThisForm.Cnt_mENSAGEM.Mensagem.Caption = '' 
If Empty( This.Value ) And Not Empty(Thisform.Cnt_Fpagto.cnt_forma.get_fpg.Value)
	This.Value = Dtot(Ttod(TprMvCab.Datas) + crSigOpFp.Entradas)
Endif
Return !Empty( Thisform.Cnt_Fpagto.cnt_forma.get_fpg.Value) And (Not ThisForm.Tempartef)
ENDPROC

EndCase

ENDPROC

* PROCEDURE Valid

PROCEDURE Valid

Local llOkTef,lnIdent,lcTipo,SendTef,lcNSus,lnOrdem,RetImpr,_RetAprov,_RetcChep,lcErro

With Thisform.Cnt_Fpagto.Cnt_ChequepreTef
	If Empty(.get_bco.value) Or ;

		This.parent.Get_pca.Setfocus
*!*			If crSigFiMpF.Pertos

*!*					PertoExpulsa(Iif(crCfComitc.TpBarras=3,.t.,.f.))
*!*					PertoDesabilita(Iif(crCfComitc.TpBarras=3,.t.,.f.))
*!*				Else
*!*					llRet = ChronoHabilita(CrCfComItc.Portas)
*!*					ChronoExpulsa(CrCfComItc.Portas)
*!*					ChronoDesabilita(CrCfComItc.Portas)
*!*				EndIf 
*!*			Endif
		Return .f.
	Else
		Select xpar
		If !fChkCheques( bancos, agencias, contas, numeros,.f.,.t.,Dopes)
			ThisForm.Cnt_mensagem.Mensagem.caption = ''
			Replace xPar.nidcheps with 0 in xpar
			If ! plTefChq
				Thisform.Limpa_cheptef()
				This.Parent.Get_pca.SetFocus
			Endif
*!*				If crSigFiMpF.Pertos
*!*					If CrCfComItc.TpLeChqs = "K"
*!*						llRet = PertoHabilita(CrCfComItc.Portas,Iif(crCfComitc.TpBarras=3,.t.,.f.))
*!*						PertoExpulsa(Iif(crCfComitc.TpBarras=3,.t.,.f.))
*!*						PertoDesabilita(Iif(crCfComitc.TpBarras=3,.t.,.f.))
*!*					Else
*!*						llRet = ChronoHabilita(CrCfComItc.Portas)
*!*						ChronoExpulsa(CrCfComItc.Portas)
*!*						ChronoDesabilita(CrCfComItc.Portas)
*!*					EndIf 
*!*				Endif
			Return .f.
		EndIf
	Endif
EndWith
ThisForm.Cnt_mensagem.Mensagem.caption = ''
lcErro=''
Select crSigCdPam
goto top
Thisform.NOkLostChe=.t.

If CrSigCdPam.pdvcchqs = 1

Else

	If CrSigCdPam.pdvcchqs = 2
	
		If Not ThisForm.Consultaexecutada

			ThisForm.Cnt_mensagem.Mensagem.caption = 'Aguarde processamento Consulta'
			llOkTef = .t.

			=ThisForm.parentform.podatamgr.cursorquery('SigOpFp','CrSigOpFp','Fpags',xPar.Fpags)

				SendTef = VerifGP('ATV', thisform, '', '', CrSigFiFis.Imps, Thisform.ParentForm.poDatamgr, 'TEF' )
			Else
				SendTef = 1
			Endif

			If Type('SendTef')='N'
				lcTipo = 'CHQ'

				lcNSus = ''
				lnOrdem = 0

				If InList(Upper(csTmpFpag.pCheque), Upper('Garantia Cheque Papel Tecban'), Upper('Garantia Cheque Papel RedeCard'))

					lnValorCHQ = crTotCHQ.Valos
				Else
					lnValorCHQ = XPAR.valos
				Endif

				Endif

					Thisform.Cnt_Fpagto.Cnt_ChequepreTef.get_num.value,substr(dtoc(xPar.Vencs),1,2)+;
					substr(dtoc(xPar.Vencs),4,2)+;

					Thisform.Cnt_Fpagto.Cnt_ChequepreTef.get_contadc.value,;
					Thisform.Cnt_Fpagto.Cnt_ChequepreTef.get_numdc.value,;
					ThisForm.Cnt_Fpagto.Get_npar.Value,;
					Alltrim(Thisform.Cnt_Fpagto.Cnt_ChequepreTef.get_pca.value),;
					ThisForm.Cnt_Fpagto.cnt_forma.Get_fpg.Value,.f.,ALLTRIM(crSigOpFp.PCheque),ThisForm.CMC7,ThisForm.pcIdcheque)
				If Type('SendTef')='N'

						Select xPar
						Replace cnIdTefs With Alltrim(Str(SendTef))
					Else
						Messagebox('Erro Resposta Tef !!',0+48,'ERRO')
						llOkTef = .f.
					Endif
				Else
					llOkTef = .f.
				Endif
			Else
				Messagebox('Gerenciador Padrão não Ativo !!',0+48,'')
				llOkTef = .f.
			Endif
			ThisForm.Consultaexecutada = (llOkTef)
			If llOkTef 
				ThisForm.Retchpsa = SendTef
				lcIdents='E'+TprMvCab.Emps+TprMvCab.Dopes+alltrim(str(lnIdent,20))  &&'R'
				IF ThisForm.Parentform.poDatamgr.CursorQuery('SigFiTef','crSigFiTef', 'cIdents', lcIdents)
					If !empty(crSigFiTef.cnDtChqs)

						Thisform.Cnt_Fpagto.fweditdata_Venc.Value = ctod(lcDtChqs)
						Select xpar

					Endif
					If !empty(crSigFiTef.cBancos)
						Thisform.Cnt_Fpagto.Cnt_ChequepreTef.get_bco.value = Transform(alltrim(crSigFiTef.cBancos),'@L 999')
						Select xPar

					Endif
					If !empty(crSigFiTef.cAgencias)
						Thisform.Cnt_Fpagto.Cnt_ChequepreTef.get_age.value = Transform(left(crSigFiTef.cAgencias,4),'@L 9999')
						Select xPar
						Replace agencias with Transform(left(crSigFiTef.cAgencias,4),'@L 9999') in xpar
					Endif
					If !empty(crSigFiTef.cAgendcs)
						Thisform.Cnt_Fpagto.Cnt_ChequepreTef.get_agendc.value = Transform(val(left(crSigFiTef.cAgendcs,1)),'@L 9')
					Endif
					If !empty(crSigFiTef.cContas)
						Thisform.Cnt_Fpagto.Cnt_ChequepreTef.get_conta.value = Transform(val(left(crSigFiTef.cContas,10)),'@L 9999999999')
						Select xPar
						Replace contas with Transform(val(left(crSigFiTef.cContas,10)),'@L 9999999999') in xpar
					Endif
					If !empty(crSigFiTef.cContadcs)
						Thisform.Cnt_Fpagto.Cnt_ChequepreTef.get_contadc.value = Transform(val(left(crSigFiTef.cContadcs,1)),'@L 9')
					Endif
					If !empty(crSigFiTef.cnCheques)
						Thisform.Cnt_Fpagto.Cnt_ChequepreTef.get_num.value = Transform(val(left(crSigFiTef.cnCheques,6)),'@L 999999')
						xNumero = val(left(crSigFiTef.cnCheques,6))
						Select xPar
						Replace numeros with Transform(xNumero, '@L 999999') in xpar
					Endif
					If !empty(crSigFiTef.cnumdcs)
						Thisform.Cnt_Fpagto.Cnt_ChequepreTef.get_numdc.value = Transform(val(left(crSigFiTef.cnumdcs,1)),'@L 9')
					Endif
					If !empty(crSigFiTef.cTpPess)
						Thisform.Cnt_Fpagto.Cnt_ChequepreTef.opc_cpf.value = iif(left(crSigFiTef.cTpPess,1)='F',1,2)
					Endif
					If !empty(crSigFiTef.cnDocPess)
